*BILKISU BILYAMINU*



              1-5


     Ta gefin ido take kallan shi,gaba d'aya ya  tafi da imanin ta,ita dai sosai yake burgeta,zata zauna gidan auran ta kuda kullum zai dinga yankar naman jikinta ne karewar shariya,kuma badan haka ba,zata yima baffan ta biyayya kamar yanda ya bu'kata.

    Daga saman bene take sak-kuwa gaba d'aya ilahirin jikinta rawa yake,riga ce iyakarta gwaiwa da kadan ma ta idasa gwaiwar,mai hannun vest ce,kuma daman ba'a maganar breaziya  saboda da breaziya take had'e,santala santalan chinyoyin ta a bayyane suke daga saman benan da take sak-kuwa ya kalleta ya kauda kai,idan akwai wadda ya tsana gaba daya rayuwar shi bai wuce *_MUNAYYA_* ba,wadda tun farkon haduwarsu ya d'auki karan tsana ya dura mata,gashi wai yau itace matarshi,tsaki yayi ya kara tamki fuska,d'aya daga cikin kojeran _daining table_ din ta jawo ta zauna, tana yatsina fuska,wani mugun kallo ta watsama *_DIJE_* ai bata san sadda ta mike ba ta nufi saman bene ta shige dakinta.

   _Flacks_ ta jawo da niyyar ta zuba ruwan zafi sai ta ga koko ne,da sauri ta ajeyi shi ta kwalama peter kira,cikin sauri ya bayyana gabanta yadan rissina yace _"good moning_ ma"

    _"You are such an animal,how dia you_ zaka dama mana koko a cikin wannan gidan, an gaya maka muma dangin yarbawa ne?"

    "Sorry ma,ban dama koko ba wallahi"

     _Flacks_ din ta d'auka ta jefa mashi, bayan yayi saurin gocewa da ya fasa mashi ciki,ya buge bango ya fad'i kasa ya fashi tace"uwar kace nan ciki?kana nufin na maka 'karya kinan?"

      "Sorry ma!amman ban dama koko ba wallahi,ga _flacks_ din da na dafa ruwan zafi can"ya nuna matashi da hannunshi.

    'Kara kulewa takeyi idan yace bashi ya dama kokon ba" akwai wani kuku a gidan nan bayan kai ko?"

    Kai ya kad'a yace"babu ma" 

     Tashi tayi daga inda take taje har inda yake ta d'aukishi da wani mari tace"uban wa ki maka karya?bayan ga kokon ina gani"

     "Ina ganin _Aunty_ karamace ta dama ma oga saboda naga jiya ta aiki Audi driver ya kai mata markadan gero"

       "Wacece _Aunty_ karama kuma a nan gidan"ta fad'a tana yamutsa fuska.

   Peter yace "Mai kama da larabawan nan"

     Ta gane wadda yake nufi,duk wannan abin dake faruwa tsakanin peter da *_MUNAYYA_* yana jinsu amman ku kallan banza basu ishi shiba.

      Dawowa tayi ta zauna saman kujera sannan takai duban ta inda *_ALI_* yake yanayin fuskar shi ya tabbatar mata da babu sassauci a tare da shi, tsaki ya ja mata ya kara kauda kai ya cigaba da shan kokon shi da kusai wanda *_DEJI_* tai karambanin had'a mashi,a tsamanin bazai shaba sai gashi kuma yasha sosai.

      Tsaki tayi niyyar maida mashi sai kuma ta tuna da rashin mutuncin shi,sai ta fasa,saboda bata san daliliba yau kawai sai taji tana shakkar shi,  _flacks_ din ta jawo da niyyar ta had'a shayin da zata zuba ma cikinta.

    Daga chan bayan ta taji ana fadin"wake mana hayaniya tunda safin nan kamar gidan haya?kuma naji ana maganar koko,kamar gidan tsiya"

    *_MUNNERA_*ce take sakkuwa daga saman benan kumai na jikinta bayyane yake idan akayi duba da wata riga da ta saka,slik ce amman gaba d'aya ta d'ameta,ga _eirpace_ ta makala a kunnuwan ta, tana danna waya,cikin sakan din da baifi goma ba ya kare mata kallo,babbar mtsalarta karya da jin kai ga gadara kamar diyar shugaban kasa,shi kuma ya tsani makaryace farkon ganin da ya fara yi mata da karya ta tareshi shiyasa ku ganinta bayya sanyi,kai ya kauda ya taba baki yana jin wani bakin ciki a zuciyarshi,da Alhajin shi ya tara mashi mata hudu a gida babu wacce ta zaba,kuwacce ya tsani ganin ta ma sannan ga wadan nan rid'a rid'an da kullum sai sun tada mashi hankali da iri irin muggan shigar da sukiyi,bai saniba ku da gayya ma suke Mashi haka,oho amman sannu a hankali zaiyi maganinsu d'aya bayan d'aya.

    'Daya daga cikin kujirun _daining table_ din itama taja ta zauna,sai suka sakashi tsakiya gefan shi ta kalla domin ta gaida shi,idanuwan shi ya dago suka had'a idanuwa da sauri ta dauki kanta,sai ta wayanci ta kwalama peter kira,gargadi ta hango na zakici ubanki idan kika daman.

      Ran *_MUNAYYA_* ya gama baci da *_MUNNERA_* saboda tana mata shigar hanci da kudun-dune tsaki tayi tace"gidan tsohu mai kike ce inba koko da kusan ba,dan kin samu an had'a da Allah da Annabi an wurguki nan gidan shine zaki kara farashin karyarki,tunda Allah ya halicci duniya ban taba ganin makaryaciya irin kiba"

     "Kina bani mamaki,kina mace amman fitinar ki tafi ta ta'kadarin namiji yawa,ki kuwa baki barshi ba anya kuwa har tsofaffinki kina raga masu kuwa?ku da yake wanda ya gaji tsiya,cikin tsiyar zai daw-wama"

     Duk abinda ki faruwa tsakanin su *_ALI_* yana jinsu,amman ku kallo basu ishi shiba,yama 'kagara ya gama abinda yake ya fita gidan,idan yasu su kunna ma kansu wuta kariwar futina.

     Peter ne yazo,ya rissina cikin girma mawa yace ma *_MUNNERA_* _"good monin ma"_ 

     Hannu ta dan d'aga mashi tace" _please help me with is"_ ta nunama shi tarkacan kayan shayin,daman kuma yasan aikin shi, saboda duk sanda zata zauna cin abinci sai dai ya _saving_ dinta kumai, a cewar ta wai ai biyan shi akeyi,saboda haka duk cikin aikin shi ne.

     Yana had'a mata tea din amman gaba d'aya hankalin shi na kan *_MUNNERA_* wadda kirjin ta yake tantsan da dukiyar fulani,saboda kyansu da girman su har wani kyalle sukeyi,gaba d'aya ya raja'a ga kallan ta, *_ALI_* ya gama _break_ dinshi ya dago zai ma peter magana ya kai mashi _briefcase_ mota,sai ya ankara da abinda ki faruwa,ranshi yayi kululuwar bace,gyaran murya yayi,da sauri peter ya bar wajan har yana tun-tube saboda yasan gaman su da *_ALI_* a wannan gan-gancin da yayi.

      *_MUNNERA_* da bata san abinda ki faruwa ba tace"bazan iya had'a wajan _break_ daki ba ,bari in bari ki gama na dawo inyi kada ki tambaru wata karyar da zaisa in zubda yaran cikina gaba d'aya"tashi tayi ta nufi _stairs_ din hadda d'an gudunta take had'awa,sai ya zamana hatta yar rigar dake jikin ta tana d'agawa,ta wutsiyar ido yake kallan duk abinda takeyi,wani mugun tsaki ya ja wanda har *_MUNNERA_* sai da ta waiga tana kallan shi,hantar cikin ta ta kad'a,yanda taga idanuwan shi sunyi ja,fuskar shi ma tayi ja, saboda tsananin bacin rai,a zuciyar ta take ayyana wannan akwai kudugu sarkin zuciya.

             10-15


   Ranshi ya gama baci da abinda ya faru,lallai yazama sakarai da zaibar wadan nan karnukan yaran su nime su sa mashi hawan jini,kuma a gidanshi,wani banzan arni can yake kallan abinda aka aji a gidanshi da sunan wai mallakinshi ne,da sauri shima ya tashi ya haura saman stairs din,kachibis sukayi da *_AZEEMA_* wadda take shirin sakkuwa itama,domin halartan wajan breakfast din,yana yinshi kawai ta kalla ta bashi hanya ya wuce,da kallo ta bishi tana mamakin abinda ya hawo dashi saman su, bata ta'ba ganin ko kallon saman ma yayi ba, ballantana ace ya haura tun ranar da aka kawosu a matsayin matanshi,baki ta taba tace"any way"

    Bayan *_MUNAYYA_* ta shige dakinta ta turoshi,amman bata rufe ba,saboda tasan babu wanda zaiyi gigin shigo mata daki,wani dan kurido ta nufa ta cire 'yar rigar dake jikinta,ta d'aura towel da niyyar ta shiga wanka,jin budewar kofa tayi da mamaki take kallan wanda ke niyyar shigo mata, *_ALI_* ta gani ya shigo sannan ya maida ya rufi hadda murza key,gadan- gadan ya tunkaruta da mamaki take kallan shi.

       Sannan tace"lafiya malam?mai ya kawoka d'akina?"

      Shakaruta yayi sannan yace"ubanki ya shigo dani,kedin banza da zaki tambayine mai ya shigo dani wani sashi na gidana,ku gidan ubankine?"

    Shakarar da yayi mata bata wasa baci dakyar take iya magana tace"dalla malam sakarmani wuya kada ka kashine"

     Kara farashin shakar ta yayi yace"ai gwamma in aika daki inda ba'a dawowa,'yar iska in huta da tsiyar da aka kawo mani gida,karya kawai kazar kuwa sai da aka gama daki sannan aka kawo mani fanko cikin gida"

    Ai kuwa da gaski yake zai iya kaitan lahira,sai da ta tattaru miyan bakinta sannan ta tufa mashi a fuska,ai kuwa akayi rashin sa'a ya shigar mashi idanuwa kuma dukka guda biyun,ai kuwa babu shiri ya sakita yana goge fuskar shi,da tunanin idan ya kamata tabbas kuda bata mutuba zata shaki kamshin lahira.

      Sai da taje kusa da toilet,ta tabbatar ta rike handle din toilet din sannan tace"aikin banza aikin wufe,ku nima zuwa akai ai mani fin karfin da zalincin da aka saba yima mutane,kuma ka sani nafi karfin karya sai dai idan kaini karan,saboda takamar ka ana cemaka _young millonia_,kana da kudi,kuma da kake cimani 'yar iska,ai kafi kuwa sanin waye d'an iska tsakanina da kai,saboda iskancin ka shiyasa tsoho ya cika maka gida da mata har guda hudu,wama ya sani ta sanadiyyarka yara nawa akaima fyde"tagama sanin idan ya riga ya kamata wahalar da zataci kilama ya aikata inda bazata taba dawowa ba,sai tai wup da niyyar ta shige bayi ta kulle kofan.

       Ai kuwa cikin zafin nama ya danna kofar daman abunka ga namiji,kuma namijin mai kirar mazan sosai,ai kuwa sai gashi shima cikin bayin hannuwanshi dukka biyu yasa ya d'aukuta chak,ya fito da ita daga bayin, sannan ya direta a kasa,sai da ya bata lafiyayyun mari guda ukku sannan yace"lallai yau duk wani rashin imanina da iskancin nawa da zalincin da kika lissafa zasu kari a kanki"

      Kara d'aukarta yayi ya direta a kan gado,sannan yasa kafarshi guda d'aya ya tukari mata hannu d'aya yanda bama zata iya gigin tashi ba,rigar shaddarshi dake jikinshi ya cire ya zamana dagashi sai dugun wandan shaddar da vest din ciki.

      Ihu ta saka saboda ta gama sanin yau kwananta sun kari tunda ya damkita,sai da ya tattaru gashin dukin datake karawa a saman kai,ya murda shi sosai sannan yajashi wani ihu ta sake mai wahala,sannan kuma ya had'a hannuwan ta waje guda,ya matsesu sannan ya had'a bakinshi da nata,ya fara tsotsar shi,cikin mugunta da zalinci babu halin kuka amman hawaye sunata ratata ta gefin idanuwan ta gashi ku mutsi bata iyayi kuya ta mutsa sai taji kamar ana d'aurita ne,sai da ya gaji dan kanshi ya saki bakin nata,ai kuwa yana sakin bakin nata ta tattaru yawon bakinta ta tufar.

     Sannan tace" Allah ya isa mugu azzalimi, da in had'a baki dakai gwamma na hada da aladi zaifi man sauki"
 
     Dariya yayi a cikin zuciyarshi a fili kuma sai yace" lallai ki jarumace inasan game da irinku masu dauriya da jure wahala"

     Bakinta ya kamu ya matse sannan ya zuba mata miyan bakinshi sannan ya danneta ya tushi mata hanci,tana ta mutsu-mutsu ta kwaci kanta amman ta kasa,bata da wani zabi wanda ya wuce ta had'eyi miyan kawai, saboda idan ta cika mutsima zata iya karkaryewa,kutttttt kakeji ta hadiye miyan

      Cemata yayi"to ya kika gani? yanzo kuma sai ki fito da 'yan hanjin cikin ki kinan ko?"

     Kakarin amai ta fara tana kuka,kara dannita yayi ya bude bakinta ya kara maida nashi ya cigaba da tsotsar ta a hankali,yaji yanayin shi ya fara canzawa yana san ya kama wata tashar,a wahalci ya zare jikin shi daga nata, sannan ya d'auki rigar shi da niyyar ya fita,saboda bazai ta'ba yadda wani abu ya shiga tsakanin shi da wannan ashawo girl din ba,tunda ya gama sanin ku itadin wacece.

     "Mugu kawai Allah ya isa"

[7/7, 06:59] Umar Dalha: *_MATAN ALI_*
_(The most lovely story of nice famly)_

_Nagarta writter's Association._


             20-25


  Dawowa yayi da baya ya ajeyi rigar sannan ya kara matsawa kusa da ita yace"ina gudan maki rashin imani na kada ya rataya a wuyanki,amman kina kukarin tunzirani ga aikata maki abinda khaf danginku zasuyi dana sani,da wannan tsohun naki dangin mayu,amman bakyaji ku?"

    "Akwai dangin mayu sama daku?kud'in mai dattin hula ai anci na dodo ne,bana Allah da Annabi bane"

   Ranshi ya gama baci da mugun kazafin da taima Alhajin shi,wani zazzafan mari ya wankita dashi sannan yace"inaga zan kawo karshan abinda kike takama dashi,kuma daman iskancin nake yasa kin fara tallar kanki wajansu peter kina yawo tsirara,a cikin gidana"

    Dan towel din jikinta ya finciki yayi jifa dashi gefi guda,sannan ya d'aura hannuwan shi dukka biyun bisa na shanonta,murzasu yakeyi cikin mugunta,ihu take tana kuka,sannan ya maida hannunshi wajan pant dinta,shima ya fincike shi da karfin tsiya, cikin rashin sa'a sai ga pad ta biyu pant.d'ayan hannun nashi ya maida saman breast dinta ya matsa da karfin tsiya ai kuwa ta fasa wani ihu,daga bisani kuma sai kumai na jikinta ya sake(suma).

     A can palo kuma matan gidan sun hallara suna jiyo ihun *_MUNAYYA_* sunyi cirko-cirko,hankulansu tashi,da yake *_DEJI_* mai saurin kuka ce har ta fara matsar kwalla.

     *_MUNNERA_* ta dubesu tace"kada fa ya kashi masu yarinyar mutane,gaskiya ni dai kuma mai zai mani zan shiga in taimaka mata"

      *_AZEEMA_* tace"baki da hankali kin kuwa san waye ya Ali?ai da ki shiga dakin can da sunan citon wata gwamma kin rungume transformer kinsan irin bala'in da kika saka kanki ciki,amman kuma ga fili ga mai doki"

     *_DIJE_* tace"to dan Allah ki fad'a ma Alhajin shi mana,idan yayi mashi magana nasan zai kyaleta tunda dole yaji maganar mahaifinshi"

     *_AZEEMA_* tace"Allah ya kyauta in tafka wannan babban kuskuran da har in kuma ga mahaliccina ina dana sani,nasan bayan nabar gidan nan kuma zan kara samun wani tukwaicin daga garishi,nikuwa idan har inason barin gidan nan to inason mutuwata,nida gidan ga sai mutuwa"

     *_MUNNERA_* tace"to yanzo ya zamuyi haka zamu saka ido,ayi mana kisa cikin gida?"

     *_AZEEMA_* tace"abu d'aya zan iyayi,shima sai na tabbatar da asirina bazai tunu ba"

     *_MUNNERA_* tace"please menene?"

    *_AZEEMA_* tace"ya *_OMMAR_* shi kadai zai iya zuwa gidan nan ya taka mashi birki kuma ya hanashi aikata abinda yake niyyaryi"

    Da sauri *_DEJI_* tace"dan Allah kirashi mana"da sauri ta fad'a dakinta ta d'auku waya ta nemo number yayaynta *_O'MARA_* ringin d'aya zuwa ukku ya d'auka daga bangaranshi yace"ya akai?"

     "Yaya wallahi ga ya ALI_* nan zaiyi kisa cikin gida,kuma ya saka ma d'akin sakata"

     "Kisa kuma!?mai ya faru?"

     "Bamu sani ba shida *_MUNAYYA_* ne dai"

      "Ok,ganinan zuwa please"

      "Ah yaya dan Allah ka rufa mani asiri kada kaci ni na fad'a maka,kasan dai halinshi"

     Bai bata amsaba sai dai taji ditttttttt an kashi wayan,kallansu tayi tace"ina tunanin yanzo zaku ganshi ya iso"gaba d'aya sun manta kishiyoyine su,kuma babu mai shiga harkar kuwa.

    Bangaran *_MUNAYYA DA ALI_* kuwa bayan ta suma,sai ya matsa side din gado ya d'auku rubar ruwan faro cikin friage mai d'ankaran sanyi sannan daga tsayan da yake ya bude ya kwarara mata tundaga kanta har zuwa kafafuwanta,firgigit ta tashi ta kwalla wata kara.

     Dai-dai lokacin da ta kwalla 'kara dai-dai lokacin *_OMMAR_* ya shigo gidan,cikin sauri yayi hanyar stairs din yana tambayar mike faruwa,gaba d'aya suka had'a baki wajan fadin suma basu sani ba,murd'a masangalin kofar yayi ya jita kulle,bubbugawa ya hau yi amman shiru kakeji(kamar an shuka shirwa)wayan shi ya zaro yana neman *_ALI_* amman sai ya jita can saman sofan falon kusa da briefcase dinshi tana ringin,tsaki yayi ya kara bubbuga 'kofan amman still ba'a bud'e ba.
      
    Kafa d'aya ya d'auka ya d'ura saman bedside din gadanta, sannan ya rankwafu yace"ina tausaya maki zaman da zamuyi gidan nan nida ke,zan tabbatar da cewa duk bayan minti d'aya zaki dauwama cikin 'kuncin rayuwa,mai tunaninki yake baki?nashigo dakin kine saboda wata manufa?ku daya kada kiyi tunanin haka saboda jaka na daukike,a cikin jakunan ma no'1,"

     Kunnanta yaja da karfi sannan yace"ki kiyayi kanki da fito mani zindir cikin gida kina tallar kanki wajan masu aiki da masu gadi,idan kuma baki gyara ba lallai ina maki albishir da zamanki gidan nan zai kari da wuri,bayan na tabbatar nayi maki babban tabo,wanda har iya rayuwarki bazaki manta dashi ba"

     Sannan ya sakar mata kunne,hawaye ne kita kwarara a idanuwanta gaba d'aya ilahirin jikinta yayi jawur saboda wuyar da tasha wajan *_ALI_* ,a zuciyarta tana ayyana irin fansar da zata d'auka wajan shi,dan ba lallai ya sha ba.

    Rigarshi ya d'auka yayi hanyar fita daga dakin keyn ya murza sannan ya bud'e ya fito,da ka kalle fuskarshi zaka tabbatar da babu manzon rahama a cikinta,da sauri *_OMMAR_* ya tasu ya tareshi yace" kana da hankaline da kakesan yin kisa a gidanka?"

    Tsaki yayi sannan ya d'auki kanshi daga kallan da yake mashi ya kama hanyar sauka daga stairs din, *_OMMAR_* ya kara binshi a baya sun sauka daga kan binan yana binshi a baya yana kara tambayarshi abinda ya haddasa fitinar,sai da ya sauka kasa ya aji rigarshi gefan kujera shima ya zauna sannan ya dube *_OMMAR_* da rinannu idanuwan shi da suka can za kala daga farari zuwa jajaye sabida tension.

     Yace"dalla malam kada ka daman mana"

            30-35


      Tsaki ya 'karayi, sannan ya d'auki rigara shi yayi hanyar site d'in shi,da yake duka matan nashi saman binan suke,shi kuma yana 'kasa.

   Girgiza kai *_OMMAR_* yayi sannan ya 'kara haurawa saman ya iske su kuwacce tana tsaye suna tunanin shiga,saboda a iya zaman da sukayi na wata d'aya a gidan sun gama fahimtar wani abu daga cikin rashin mutuncin *_MUNAYYA._*

     A haka *_OMMAR_* ya same su yace"bai kamata ni in shiga ba, saboda rashin dacewar haka, *_MUNNERA_* shiga kiga what going on"

     "ok ,bari in shiga,man dincan dai bazai ce kumai ba ku?"

     "Bazai kara wai-wayar tanan ba, kuma idan ma zaice wani abu akai,i will handle the issue"

   *_MUNNERA_* tace "That is fine"

  'Dakin ta nufa ta shiga,har bakin gadon ta isa,fuskarta ta kalla taga tayi jawur,alamun tasha maruka kinan, gefin gadon ta zauna tace"wai mai ya hadaki da Man ne?"

       Banza tayi da ita,ta kauda kanta,saboda tsananin rad'ad'in da takeji a jikin ta,da kuma bakin cikin abinda *_ALI_* yayi mata suke damunta.

    Gaban tane ya bada daram!!!da ta ankara da cewa babu kaya jikinta,amman domin ta tabbatar da zarginta sai ta dafa kafadarta tace"bari in taimaka maki duk da dai baki da mutunci,ba dan halinki za'ayi maki ba"

    Kukarin janye bedsheet d'in take,sai taga jini gefen blanket d'in da ta rufe jikinta dashi,zargin ta ya tabbata gaskiya kinan.
   
      Baki ta taba tace"kuda yake ma babu taimakon da zan maki,shida ya aikata aikin tunda ya iya tafiya ya kyaleki ni me zan iyayi maki,nima tafiyata zanyi a karata lafiya"

    Hanya ta kama tayi tafiyar ta,inda ta barsu nan dai ta kara iske su, *_OMMAR_* yace" mai yayi mata,bugunta yayi?kume?"

     *_MUNNERA_* tace"Shi ya kamata ka tambaya tunda yasan abinda yayi,ni kaga tafiyata" ta shigewar ta d'aki ta banku kofa da karfi saboda bacin rai.

      Tsaki *_OMMAR_* yayi yace"babu wani bayani cikakke da tayi da zai fahimtar damu abinda ki faruwa"

    Idan shi ya maida sai tin da *_AZEEMA_* take,tun kafin yayi magana tayi hanyar d'akin ta,saboda ta gane abinda *_MUNNERA_* take nufi.

    Dafe kanshi yayi ya maida duban shi wajan da *_DIJE_* take a zuciyar shi yana ayyana kan-kantar *_DEJI_* bazata iya taimakon wani ba, ita ma taimaku take nema,dan inda za'a cigaba da rainan ta tana bukatar hakan.

    "Ina ta ganki ta aiku ki?kuma mai tace ki fad'a"

    "Cewa tayi in kawo maka kai ya kamata ka wanki tunda kai ka aikata,jinin ya fito,kuma zanji baya in wankine sai tace in kawo maka kai ya kamata ka wanki"

    Cikin bacin rai da kunan zuciya yace _"dat is fantastic"_ da hannu ya nuna kan shi yace"ni zan wanki zanin gado"?

        kumawa yayi ya zauna ya dafi kai zuciyar shi na tafasa, wai ya Alhaji yake so da shi ne,so yake sai bala'in yaran nan ya kashi shi,ku minti goma cikakku ba'ayi ba ya gama da wata, yanzo ga wasu na kukarin kunna shi yayi _spark"_ya Allahu"

     *_DEJI_* tayi tsoro tsoro kamar wadda take jira aci tsen ta tsuge da fitsari gaba d'aya hankalin ta baya jikinta.

  *_OMMAR_* yace"aji ki tafi to"

      Wani mugun kallo *_ALI_* ya jifa mata,'kara 'kan'kami zanin gadon tayi yace _"stop all this nonses_,ka barta ta kuma dashi inda ta fito,kawai kada raina ya idasa baci inyi abinda har ni kaina sai nayi dana sani _so please"_

     *_OMMAR_* yace"khadeeja ki tafi kawai, idan zaki iya wankiwa ki wanki idankuma baki iyawa ki magana sai a samu mai wankiwar?"

     *_OMMAR_* yace"to je ki wanki kinji"

    Safa da marwa ya fara yana neman hanyar da ya kamata ya fara dai-dai ta yaran daki cikin gidan shi kamar an tsinkule shi ya mike da sauri zai fita waje,shima *_OMMAR_* da sauri ya kamu hannun shi, ya rike yace _"please control your self_ mana,wannan shine 'kalubalan auran hakuri da d'auki kai akan abubuwa da dama"

   *_ALI_* yace"Kana _moking_ dina ne ku maye?an gaya maka mahaukaci ne ne, _am in control_ ka jirani in dawo mana"

     _"Ok, to please_ kada kayi somthing stopid"

    _"Never mind"_

     Kamar zaki haka ya fita,har ta kusa fita ta 'kofar baya ya 'kwala mata kira,da sauri ta juyu,zuba mashi idanuwa tayi,cikin kad'uwa,shima idanuwan ya zuba mata cikin yan mintika 'kalilan ya gama gane ta sai dai ita wannan babu kazan ta jikin ta, amman _still_ da ka ganta kaga yar kauye fitk ba laifi babu kazanta ku d'aya jikinta,sannan kuma farin wannan yafi na wacan yawa ma, ga idanuwan ta manya  da tsari,ranshi baci yayi tsaki yace"zo nan wajan"

    Hawaye ta sharce tace"gashi can na tsakiya"

    Ya juya ya tafi har yayi taku ukku sai kuma ya juyu yace mata"idan ta tambayi ki kin bada kice kin bane"

     *_DEJI_* cikin sauri tace"to"

    Dakin ya kuma yace ma *_OMMAR_* _"lest go"_

     _"I hope_ bakai mata kumai ba?"

    "Mai kuwa zan mata?"

    "Haba *_ALI_* nafa san halinka, _please_ ka dinga hakuri tunda haka Allah ya 'kaddara maka, _after all_ kuma kai kaja ma kanka"

    Banza yayi dashi ya dauki key din mota ya fita ya nufi _parking space_ ya shiga d'aya daga cikin motocin da ke wajan wata bakar _mercedez c-class_ baka wulik yayi mata key ya bar ta tana _worming_,sai ga *_OMMAR_* ya iso inda yake ya shiga motar yana dariya yace *_"ALI_* gadanga kusar yaki , *_ALI_* mai mata hudu kyawawa"

     Horn yayi tun kafin ma ya rufe marfin motan,da sauri maigadi ya wangame mashi gate,da gudu ya kwashi motar.

     *_OMMAR_* yace"taimaka mani kada kayi sanadin barina duniya,ba tare da nayi aure ba,kai tunda ka riga ka cika sunnar,ni ka aika dani lahira"

       *_Ahmad &Son's Nigerian Limited_* aka rubuta daga gefin titin jikin wani katin _symbol,_ga wata katuwar masana anta wadda da gani kasan wani daji ne aka diba aka maida shi masana 'an wadda ga alama ta tara bil'adama masu yawa,idan akayi duba da yawan masu shiga da fita.

     *_ALI_* yace "babba ma kuwa,Alhaji dan girman Allah kayi mani rai ka rabani da wadan nan yaran,wallahi bana cikin natsuwa saboda halin da nake fuskanta da zama dasu"

             100-105


         Dakyar ta iya tashi saboda baccin daki idanta in ma bacin shine da gaskiya bazata iya tashi ba, friage din  dake d'akinta ta d'auku ruwan ever da cup tayi  hanyar falon 'kasa kan kujira ta iske shi kwance kamar mara lafiya,isa tayi inda yake ta mi'ka mashi,bayan ta zuba mashi cikin cup din,amsa yayi ya shanye sannan ya kara mika mata ta kara zu'ba mashi rabin cup,kai ya d'aga yana kallanta,ta gane abinda yake nufi,rubar ruwan ta 'kara d'agawa ta ida sa cika mashi cup din,shima ya shanyi, anan ta gani yana cikin damuwa, d'aya daga cikin alamun da take gani yana cikin 'bacin rai kinan,shan ruwa mai sanyi a kalla wajan kofi ukku ku hud'u,kumawa yayi ya kara kwanciya a hankali yaji zazzabi da wani mugun ciwan kai  suna sakku mashi,ga zuciyar shi tana tashi,cup din da rubar ta d'auka ta mai da inda ta d'aukusu, bayan ta biya kichin ta d'auku mashi snack da danbun nama,a falon dai ta 'kara iski shi bisa kujera ya 'kudun-dune, matsawa tayi kusa dashi.

      Tace"yaya *_ALI_* ga  sanck da danbun nama na kawo maka, jiya nayisu _only for you_ nasan kana san su _the most"_

     Hannun shi ta kama tace _"get up"_

      Kamar baya so yace _"plese Meenah leave me,_ bana iya cin kumai in _fact_ ma bana jin dad'in jikina, _but later"_

     Kallan shi tayi cikin tausaya wa tace _"sorry_ Yaya, ai ban sani ba"

    Plate din ta d'auka ta mai da snack din da danbun naman inda ta d'ibosu,ta saka plate din a ma'ajiyar wanke- wanke, sannan ta 'kara kumawa palon, waje ta samu kusa da kujeran da yake kwanci ta zauna tana kallan shi,tayi uban tagumi tana tunanin tun farkon fara wannan hatsaniyar auran Yayan ta bai zauna lafiya ba, kullum cikin damuwa yake da tashin hankali, baida isha-shan kwanciyar hankali, yanzo haka rayuwar shi zata zauna kullum cikin damuwa _that is not possible_

    A hankali ya bud'e idanuwan shi yaga ta zuba mashi idanuwa,yanayin ta ya nuna tana cikin damuwa a hankali ya bud'e baki yace"zunan My Menah"

     Tashi tayi ta kuma kusa dashi ta zauna, can karshan kujera sannan ta daga kan shi ta dura saman cinyar ta tana shafawa, sun d'auki kusan minti biyar babu wanda yace uffan a cikina su hawaye suka zubu mata a hankali ta sharisu,amman sai da suka zuba saman gushin sa,idanuwan shi suna lumshi amman ba bacci yake ba,hannun ta d'aya ya kamu yace _"what make you cry?"_
     Matsa hannun shi ita ma tayi tace _"Nothing Yaya"_

     _"I dont like all this so please"_

       "Yaya _how long_ zaka cigaba da saka damuwa a ranka,gashi ka saka ma kanka ciwu kuma duk wannan damuwarci, wai mai yasa ka kasa fad'a mashi cewa bazaka iya ba,tunda gashi kana cutuwa, muma muna cikin damuwa ,bazan ta'ba jurima ganin ka a wannan yanayin ba,akwai kya- kyawar fahimta tsakninmu da iyayanmu zan sami Alhajin,amman _i cant take it anymore"_

       _"Dont ever think of doying such king of this,_ ki bari kawai duk rintsi yana tari da sauki,yasan zan iya ne shiyasa yayi haka, badan kuma baya sona ba"

      "Bafa zaka iya ba, tunda daga lukacin zuwa yanzo baka 'kara kwanciyar hankali da farin ciki ba,kullum cikin damuwa gashi ankai matakin da ciwo zai kama ka ciwan ka na farawa ne daga zazzabi da ciwan kai kuma gashi duk akwaisu"

      _"Forget it,_kawo man _paracetamol_ insha, insha Allahu zai sauka na sani"

     "Ok,Allah yasa bari in d'auku maka a d'akina ina dashi"

      Tashi tayi ta d'auku _paracetamol_ ta had'u da sauran ruwan da ta maida d'azun ta kawo mashi,ta balle maganin ta mika mashi,tashi yayi da kyar ya jingina da kujera sannan ya mika hannu ya kar6a ta mika mashi ruwan cikin kofi ya amsa ya sha,sannan ya kuma ya kwanta,matashi ya 'karayi da chinyarta,ta cigaba da shafa kanshi tana tufa mashi addu'a a hankali yake iya magana saboda zazzabin yake ji a jikin shi sosai yace _"tank you very much"_

    _"Please stop you deserve to do everthing to you_ in dai zakai farin ciki _am ready to do it_ ku kad'ai ne garine _i love the both of you,very much"_

      Murmushi yayi ya kara riko hannunta yace _"thank you so much_cigaba da addu'ar _i like it_ ina samun sassauci"

     'Kara damki kan tayi ta cigaba da yi mashi addu'a,a hankali bacci ya dinga firzgar shi daga karshi taji kumai na jikin shi ya saki alamu sun nuna yalula bacci kinan,a hankali take zami jikinta daga kanshi kamar mai lalla'ba 'karamin yaro,sannan ta kashi mashi kallon da yake tayi tun wayewar garin da aka kunna shi,dakin Hajja ta nufa,ta iske ta tashi tana ta yan gyare gyare da goge-goge wanda daman sabanta ne, amshi tsumman hannunta tayi tace"oh Hajjana baki rabuwa da aikaci aikaci wallahi,duk sanda mutum zai samiki baki rasa abinyi ace rayuwa babu hutu?"

      " ki kika ga hutu a rayuwar duniyar nan,malalaciyar mace ce bata da aiki sai kwanciya da kallo,in anyi magana ace hutu hutun banza dai da sakaci"

      "To ai dai ana san mutum ya dinga samun realif ko?"

      "Mu iyayanmu sun gina mu da aiki shiyasa kuda mukayi aure bamu sha wahala ba wajan kumai,kuma ba 'kaza mai bane mu,tunda duk rintsi zaka tashi kayi aikin ka,ni dan ma Alhaji ya'ke yadda a ragi masu aikin nan wallahi"

      "Tab kice kin dinga aikin ki ki d'aya kinan"

      "Ko alama na kusa daina kumai ai ki cigaba da aiyukan gidan nan"

      "Su kike in mutu kinan Hajja yuh ai ni kuda girki kika barni ai na kad'i ballan tana kuma aikin gida dukka"

   "Yuh menene a cikin girkin?"

  "Sai fa kiyi girki kala hud'u lokaci guda"

     "To ja can ki bani waji in 'karasa ayuka na Alhaji ya kusa isuwa"

      "Hmmmm wai duk wannan gyaran na dawowar Alhaji ne,kinfa gyara d'aki Hajja,kika ce in 'kara gyarawa na 'kara gyarawa,yanzo kuma gashi kina gyarawa bayan banga abinda kike gyarawa ba"

     "To wai zaki fita ki bani waji ku kuwa"

      "Toba naki Hajjarmu,Yaya *_ALI_* fa yashigo,yana palon kasa kwanci bashi da lafiya"

    "Baida lafiya?"

        Shiru-shiru yaga Saude ta kawu ruwa bai ga ku 'kiyarta ba,ga kishin ruwa yana ji,ana cikin wannan hali ne sai ga Yayan *_DEJI_* ya shigo gidan,sa'bi da fatanya a kad'ar shi, yayi sallama daga can d'akin Malam Audu ya amsa yayi kiran shi.

      Yace"bani ruwa mai sanyi a tulu insha"

     "Inna bata nan ne?"

       Yake tambaya yayi hanyar d'akin Saude ya d'auku kofi ya kaima Malam Audu ruwa,turus yayi da ya iske Saude zaune saman tabarma tana daman fura.

     Yace "nama d'auka bakya nan ne Baffa yace in kawo mashi ruwa"

     Banza tayi dashi ta cigaba da dama furarta,amman cikin zuciyarta tana jin ba dadi yanda take ma Malam Audu haka,duk da a ciwarta shiya d'aukar ma kanshi, kuma ta kudurta cewa bata ga ranar barin wannan rigima ba har sai sanda ya maidu mata d'iyarta gida.

     Tsawa ta daka mashi tace"zaka tashi ka kaima Malam ruwa ku kuwa,ka nime waji ka zauna"

     Tashi yayi yana kara mamakin canjin da yake gani a gidan tun bayan auran 'Kanwarshi *_DEJI_* abubuwa da yawa sun sauya ciki hadda kuda yaushi Innarshi tana cikin bacin rai da kuka, kuma ta daina fira da Baffan shi hatta abinci sai dai ya d'auka ku kuma ta aika shi ya kai mashi.

     Mud'a ya d'auka ta silba inda ya ga tana dibar mashi ruwa ya cika ya saka marfi ya d'auka ya kai mashi turakar shi,amsa yayi yasha yace"da fatan yau ka gama idashin nomar nan?"

     "Eh na gama Baffa,yaushi za'a zuba takin?"

      "Muna iya bari kamar nan da kwana ukku haka ku hud'u ko?"

      "Eh to Allah ya kaimu"

    "Ameen,idan ka gama hutawa zan aiki ka gidan Alhaji Iro(mahaifin Saude kinan)"

     "To Baffa,ya zanci mashi saboda daga masallaci in nayi sallah sai in wuce"

     "Kaci zanzo in same shi bayan sallar isha'e"

     "To Baffa,bari in kawo maka furar"

     "Babu damuwa,ngd Allah yayi maku albarka,yasa alkairi a rayuwarku"

     Har cikin ranshi yaki jindad'i addu'ar mahaifinshi gari su shida 'yar uwarshi,ya Inna take tunanin Baffa zai iya saida *_DEJI_* zai samu lokaci su zauna yayi mata bayanin yanda abin yaki, kuma mutumin da Baffa ya ba d'an shi Auran *_DEJI_* mutumin kirki ne yana da alheri sosai da karrama mutane.

     Tashi yayi ya kuma dakin Saude ya tsaya yace"ina furar Baffan in kai mashi"

     Kwarya ta mika mashi sannan ta tashi ta haye gado tana jin ranta babu dadi.

     Bayan ya kaima Malam Audu furan ya kuma d'akin saude ya zuba tashi yasha ya kushi sannan ya shiga dakin shi ya cire kayan da yaje gona dasu ya watsa ruwa, sannan yasan ya masu haske sosai, yana da tasafta matuka,kuda yau shi zaka ganshi cikin tsafta gwanin ban sha'awa,alwala yayi sannan ya ji bakin 'kufar Malam Audu yace"Baffa sai ka tahu nayi gaba"

     "Nima ganinan bayanka ruwa kawai zan watsa in tahu,insha Allahu"

     Masallaci Jauro ya nufa shikuma Malam Audu ya dibe ruwa yayi hanyar bayan daki  da zummar yayi wanka,shima yayi shirin masallaci.

     Ita ma dai Saude buta ta d'auka ta zagaya bayan wani d'aki tayi tsarki ta dawo tayi alwala ta shige daki domin gabatar da sallah.


        **************


     *_DEJI_* ta kasa zauni ta kasa tsayi tun sanda ta ankara da katubarar da *_MUNNERA_* ta ingiza ta tayi ba tari da tunanin mai zaiji ya dawo ba,kuma a bayyane yake cewa ranshi ya baci matu'ka,lallai bazata zauna gidan nan yazo ya samita ba, kuka ta saka da ta tuna irin mugun bugun da taga ya gama yima *_MUNAYYA_*, hadda fitar mata da jini duk ilahirin jikinta rawa yake,da sauri ta isa bakin _save_ dinta ta fiddo iya adadin kayan da zasu ishita, ta kuma bakin gado ta dauki filo ta fidda shi cikin gidan shi ta d'auku ta dinga cusa kayan ta ciki har sai da ya cika ta kulle bakin shi, gyalanta ta d'aura saman kugu sannan ta 'kara 'karima dakin kallo tana kuka kamar wadda aka kura,cikin sand'a take tafiya saboda batasan kuwa yasan tana niyyar barin gidan,Allah ya taimakita bata hadu da kuwa ba a cikin falon,kufa ta bud'e a hankali ta tassama main gate din gidan, tun kafin ta idasa karasawa taji ana _horn_ bata kawo kumai ba ta cigaba da dusar bakin gate din da sauri Lawai buzu ya bude gate din gaba d'aya mota ta shigo, *_DEJI_* tsayi tayi tana kallan motar tama manta da abinda ta kudurta a zuciyarta na barin gidan,bata ankara ba sai ganin *_ALI_* tayi da *_OMMAR_* sun fito cikin motar da sauri tayi baya ta buya bayan wani gini,ta makara dan *_OMMAR_* ya riga ya ganta,da sauri ya mika ma *_ALI_* keys din yace _"get in,am on may way"_

      Baki *_ALI_* ya ta'ba ya nufi kofar da zata sada shi da cikin gidan, *_OMMAR_* kuma ya iske *_DEJI_* ta dura hannu aka tana kuka mara sauti.

     Cikin mamaki *_OMMAR_* yake kallanta  yace"lafiya ,ina zaki da kullin kaya haka?"

      Ajiyar zuciya tayi da ta ga *_OMMAR_* ne tace"dan girman Allah ka rufa mani asiri ka maidani garinmu kada nima yayi mani irin bugun da yayi mata,a gidan mu Baffana bai ta'ba buguna ba,haka ma Innata nasan mutuwa zanyi kada aima Innata asara ta"

     Hannuwan *_OMMAR_*  ta kama tace"mu tafi kafin ya fito ya kama ne"

      Janshi ta farayi yace"ki tsaya mana babu fa abinda zai maki dan kinga ya buge *_MUNAYYA_* laifi ta mashi ki kuma baki mashi laifi ba"

       "Na mashi mana tunda na kai zanin gado ya wanki dan Allah mu tafi ka maidani"

      "Kwantar da hankalinki bafa zai daki ki ba,da zai bugeki tun sanda kika kai zai daki ki ai"

     Dariya ta saki tace"eh kuma fa haka ne"

      "Yauwa to kin gani,To mu shiga babu abinda zai maki"

     Hanya ta kama ta shiga gidan da kullin kayanta a bisa kai,shi kuma yana bayanta tana shiga sukayi ido hud'u da *_ALI_* da yake kwanci bisa duguwar kujira,ido ya zuba mata yana kare mata kallo ji yayi zuciyar shi na tashi dakyar ya iya cewa.

         "ke daga gidan uban wa kike,kuma da izinin wa kika fita?"

       "Ja'iri kawai,na kusa nima nayi maka aure, ai tunda baka sanshi, yaro kamar dan nasara."

    "Haba Alhaji, nifa kila sai nayi karatun boko sannan zanyi aure."

    Shigowar Karime mahaifiyar Saude d'akin ta katse mashi magana, ta hanyar cewa "Allah ya kyauta, muga wani cikin ahalinmu yana karatun yahudawa."

    Dariya jauro ya k'yalk'yale da ita yace "hooo Inna, ai ba yahudawa ke wannan karatun ba, shifa karatun yana kara wayarma damu makiyaya kai, da sauran masu zama a cikin kauye. Sannan kuma a kauda jahilci."

     "Kai ar, yiman shiru ma da wannan maganar."

     "To nayi shiru Inna, amman nima ku daina maganar auran nan kawai, dan har yanzo akwai yarinta a tare dani, dukka fa shikarata 20."

    Alhaji yace "Ai kuwa, ni har na zab'o maka mata ma."

     "Ehhm na zauna kuna ta tsokanata, ban fad'i sak'on da aka aiko ni ba."

     "To, wani sako aka aiko ka, ka zauna kana mana surutu kai da duk sanda kazo baka rasa abin tsokana."
Ya fada yana rankwashin kanshi.

     " uch Alhaji zaka fasa mani kai in rasa mai aurena, da fasashshen kai."

     "Karime tace "Ai gwamma ka fasa kan Alhaji, tunda bai son aure boko yake so." ta fad'a tana dariya.

     "Yi maza fadi aiken da aka yi maka mana."

    "Baffa ne yace, idan anyi sallar isha'i yana nan zuwa wajanka kayi jirànshi."

     "Lafiya dai ko?"

  "Wannan sai ka bari sai yazo kun tattauna tukunna."

    "To ja'iri maza tashi ka kama gabanka."

     Tashi yayi yana dariya yace "na tafi tsohu mai ran k'arfi."

     Daga karime har Alhaji suka dinga dariya, a duk cikin jikokinsu jauro yafi soyuwa a cikin zuciyarsu, saboda ya iya girmama na gaba gashi, farat-farat da kowa ga kamun kai da tsantsan da rayuwa, yaro mai halin manya, da yawa daga cikin dattawan 'kauyan ke fad'a mashi,babban abinda ke burge mutane da yawa dashi, shine yana da tausayi da hakuri, da wuya kaji an rabashi fada da wani, tun yana yaro ballantana yanzu da ya kara zama mutum sosai.
 Yan matan kauyan basu da abin so sama da jauro, amman shi ina babu wannan maganar a rayuwarshi.

    Bayan ya koma gida ya shaida ma Baffanshi ya aiwatar da aikin da yayi mashi, sannan ya dauko jikkar makarantar arabinshi ya fita kofar gida domin telawa, da dan buzunshi rik'e ga hannu.

     Daga masallaci Malam Audu ya zarce gidan surukinshi, domin tattaunawa game da lamarin Saude, tun yana hakuri har dai yaga dacewar ya zauna da manyanta.

     Keken shi ya jingeni jikin bangon gidan, sannan ya kira wani yaro, bayan yazo ya durkusa har kasa yace "ina wuni Baban Jauro?"

"Lafiya lau" ya fada yana shafa kan yaran, yace"yi man sallama da Alhaji dan Allah."

     "To, yana ciki kuwa yanzu ya fito daga masallaci."

   "To maza kace ana sallama."

     Da gudu yaran ya shiga gidan Alhaji, minti biyu tsakani, sai gashi ya dawo yace "yace yana zuwa."

     "To babu damuwa nagode."

   Minti ukku tsakani sai ga Alhaji Iro ya fito, tun kafin ya idasa k'arasowa, Malam Audu ya cimma shi har kasa ya duka yana gaida shi, cikin farin ciki da annashuwa ya amsa,bayan nan yace "bismilla mu shiga daga ciki."

    A kunyace yace "To Alhaji."

     Bayan sun shiga turakar Alhaji Iro, kira ya kwalama yarinya daya daga cikin gidan, bayan tazo yace tayi mashi magana da Karime (mahaifiyar Saude), bayan tazo suka gaisa da surukinta, sannan ta koma. Tayo aike da ruwa masu sanyi cikin kofin silba.

    Bayan sun 'kara gaisawa, da fira wadda ta shafe yau da kullum da kuma noma da kiwo ,sai Alhaji Iro yace ina fatan dai lafiya ko?mai ke tafe da kai?"

     "Alhaji game da maganar Saude ne, nayi iya bakin k'ok'arina mu zauna lafiya amman abin yaci tura."

     Cikin mamaki yake tambaya "Lafiya? mai ke faruwa tsakaninku da har ka kasa maganin abin?"

    "Game da maganar auran DEJI ne"

     "Me auran yayi?"

  "Tun sanda akai auran Saude take tsoru da halayan da ba nata ba, to sai nai mata uzuri da abubuwa da dama a tunanina zata sauko, bayan ta daina fushi, to abu ya gagara sai ma cigaba da take."

     "Ban fahimci inda maganar ka ta dusa ba ,ka fahimtar dani yanda zan gane mana."

    "Ai kasan wannan Alhajin wanda yake da babbar gonar nan, ta kusa da yankinka."

     "To to to na gane, Alhaji Ahmadu ko"

      "Eh,to makwafcina ne, a gonata ta kusa da rafi, muna abun arziki dashi, sosai yana da mutunci da dattako, to DEJI takan kawo mana abinci lokaci-lokaci, to ranar nan sai yace mani yana san inba danshi auran DEJI, duba da nagartatun halayanshe sai na amince mashi, akan cewa duk sanda bukatar hakan ta tasu, zai man magana kamar dai yanda nazo na fada maka, watanni goma da suka wuce, to shine bayan wata bakwai yake fada mani cewa wannan magana da mukayi kwanakin baya to yana da bukatar DEJI yanzu, babu gaddama na amince mashi duba da daman anyi maganar, shine aka fara shirin biki kamar yanda kasan komai."

     "Muje, ina sauraranka."

         "To shine tace an saida mata da d'iya, har ya kaiga ta hana kanta sukune kullum kuka,duk wanda ta gani ko yazo za tace na saida mata da d'iya domin inyi kudi,duk wannan baya damuna irin hatta abinci sai dai inji in d'auka da kaina, ku in Jauro na nan ya kawo mani,kufa magana batayi mani"

      Da d'an yatsa ya nunata ranshi a matukar bace yace"kuda yake nasan baki da wannan mugun halin,amman ban sani ba ku saki a kai,tunda tsawan wata biyu kina wahalar da mijinki,bayan kinsan cewa aljannar ki tana gaf dashi amman kike wasa da damar ki ko?"

             150-155


       Bayan ya tafi ta dawo gida taci uban kuka ta share hawayan ta,bata da yanda ta iya amman kuma ai itama tana da hakki akan *_DEJIn_*, kuma ita ta haifita,da Baffan ta zaice d'iyar tace?sai a yau wani sashi na zuciyar ta yake karanta mata rashin dacewar abinda tayi,na nisan ta kanta da maigidan ta,har na tsawan wata biyu,lallai yayi juriya da dauriya, kuma ma ai bazai sai da mata tilon d'iyar da tafi soyuwa a zuciyar shi feye da ita ba,duk mai ya kawo wannan rashin hankalin dana tafka? *_ZUCIYA.._* da kuma shaid'an la'anane,ai bata san sanda ta fara tuba wajan ubangiji ba.

      Tashi tayi da sauri ta zagaya katangar bayan gidansu,ta hau d'an dutse ta le'ka makwaftansu tace"Fatseema dan Allah idan Baffana(mai sunan mahaifinta)yana nan yazo ya d'ebo mani ganyan gauda"

      "Aikuwa kinyi sa'a yanzo ya shigo gidan"

     "Yauwa,ae kuwa nagode"

      Fatseema ta 'kwala mashi kira"kai Iro kana ina?maza fito ka dibo ma Saude ganyan gauda can bakin 'kurama"

    "Ganinan Inna ban idasa cinye tuwan ba"

    "To, maza ka idasa tana jiranka"

     Fatseema tace"yau kuma gauda za'ayi"

     "Eh wallahi,Malam za'ai ma wa"

     "Ayuh naga ai kin rabu da yinshi,tun kafin auran *_DEJIn_* Baffa"

     A kai kaice magana ta gaya ma Saude, amman bata nuna mata cewa taji haushi ba,ku kuma tasan inda maganrta ta dusa ba tace"idan ya gama dan Allah Fatseema ina jiran shi,inasan in gama da wuri kada Malam ya dawo"

     "To yanzo zaki ganshi ya dawo, ballan tana yasan zaki bashi 'yar biyar ku goma"

      Dariya tayi sannan ta sauka,ta kuma d'akinta ta gyara ta kunna tsintsiyar kamshi ta canza zanin gado,da sauran abinda ya kamata,sannan ta kuma dakin Malam Audu shima ta gyara ta fiddo kayan shi masu datti ta aji gefin d'akin shi da zummar idan ta gama ta d'an d'aurayi mashi,sabon zanin gado ta d'au ku cikin wanda aka bata na auran *_DEJI_* ta sanya mashi,,ta saka tsintsiyar kamshi ta rufe dakin,sai da ta d'ebo gero ta surfa ta wanki ta barshi ya d'an jika saboda yau ita zata kirba furar da Malam Audu zais ha da kanta.

     Wajan kayan shi ta kuma ta fara wanke su fes,suka wanku kamar ma bata ta'ba wankisu haka ba,ta shanya.

      Sai ga Iro ya shigo da ganyan gauda maikyau lafiyaye,naira goma ta dan'ka mashi tace"amshi Naira darin nan ka sawu mani kifi wajan Yalbe,kaci ya za'bo maka mai kwai"

     A guje ya fita gidan yana murnar gomar da aka bashi,shi yasa duk masifar rashin san aikin shi baya yi mata,saboda tana bashi biyar ku goma wataran ma hadda abincin gidan ta mai dadi.

     Dawa ta wanke ta aje kafin ya dawo daga sayan kifin,ya mika mata nan injin ne'kan daki kofar gidansu.

     'Daki ta shiga tana tunanin kayan da ya kamata ta saka, kwallar kayanta ta d'auku masu kyau,sannan ta fara binsu a hankali idanuwan ta suka kai kan wata yelluwar atamfa mai hutan tsintsiya a jiki ta hanga,wadda Malam Audu ya sawu mata ita a cewar shi yana bala'in sonta,ta d'auku ta ajiye saman gado tana murmushi.

     Garkin shanun su taje ta tatsu madarar shanu,sannan ta nufi d'aki ta d'auku garin rid'inta ta bad'a ta shanye ta girgije silbar ta shanye abinta _(abin ban dariya matan fulani da sanin kayan gyaran aure)_

     Ta kammala kumai, kama daga d'angaudan ta, da furarta,miyarta sai kamshi take ta bad'e anguwa da kamshi,ta sanya kuwanne a inda ya kamata,ta dama fura da kindirmu mai kyau da tsafta,ta kai dakin Malam Audu.

    Sauri-sauri ta fad'a ban d'aki ta sulle jikinta da sabulun Giv mai kamshi,ta fito ta chakara kwalliya ta musamman ta nufi d'akin Malam Audu _(turakar maigida)_tana tsumayan dawo warshi.

         Kukanta ta mai da,dan daga jiya zuwa yau tayi amanna da zalincin *_ALI_* zai iya kashi mutum ma bawai ya karya shi ba.

    *_MUNNERA_* kuwa tana dana sanin abinda tayi,ku d'aya batayi tunanin cewa *_DEJI_* zata fad'a mashi cewa ita tace ta kawo mashi zanin gadan ba,lallai gaskiyar shi da yace sai tafi kuwa jin jiki,ku gizau batai ba na shiga firgeci,a zahirance, a badine kuwa babbar gobara aki a cikin jikinta,na yanda zasu kare da _young millonia_take, tayi imani yanda yake jin haushin sun nan jikinta zai gaya mata,tun ma ba'a fara kumai ba ya kwalfa mata marin da sai da tayi dana sanin tirsasa mahaifinta na cewa tana sonshi zata aure shi.

    "Ban sani ba,mai fad'a da mace 'yar uwarshi"

     Dariya ya saka yace"haba mutumina kai kanka kasan bana fad'a,idan kuma zanyi fad'a kasan yanda fad'an yake kasanciwa,saboda haka kada ka d'auka wai fad'a naki dasu,zan dai fara fad'an da su ga wacce ta zabi nacin zama dani"

     "Sai kayi duk abinda kaga dama,ai matan ka ne,idan Allah idan baka kashisu ba baka cika dan iska ba"

     Dariya yake hadda buga binchin dressing mirrown da yake gabanshi yace"kuda ban kashi su ba zasu kashi kasu da kansu ne ai"

     "Kai dai wallahi anyi dan iska nasiha batayi maka,to zan fad'a ma Alhaji duk abinda ki faruwa,in yasu daga ranar nida kai muzama manyan makiya"

     "Kanka dai ake ji,tunda da kanwarka a cikinsu ba,ai sai da na fad'a maka cewa kada ta aikata abinda zai sa tayi dana sani takiya,ai yanzo bazaka ga laifina ba"

     "File nazo ka bani wanda na bari cikin motanka jiya dana kawo ka na manta ban d'auka ba"

     "Ok ga keys din can ka d'auka nima yanzo zan fito ai"

     Ran OMMAR ya gama baci da maganganun ALI idan zai tsaya suyi musayar yawu to lallai zasuyi fad'a sosai shiyasa ya kyaleshi da mugun halinshi amman yana da tabbacin akwai ranar dana sani

     Gaba d'aya jikin DEJI zafi yake mata ga kafarta da ta kasa takawa wani mugun zuge take mata,ga marin da ya faska mata gefin fuskar ta ya kumbura,gaba d'aya zazzabi ya kamata ga wata yunwa da takeji wadda bama ta iya juriwa,da bin bango ta dinga fituwa daga d'akinta,dinga kama masangalin binan tana saukuwa,a wahalci ta isa bisa daining table ta zauna tana goge hawaye,MUNNERA ta kalla wadda ta zuba mata idanuwa tana tausaya mata,tasan duk ita ta ja mata wannan wahalar.

      "A'a saboda ni dai ba auro ni akayi ba dai"

     Da mamaki MUNNERA take kallanta zuciyarta take mamakin yanda yarinyar tai saurin ganuta akan zata yi mata zagwan 'kasa.

     "Ana nima maki sauki ne yarinya,saboda baki san yanda rayuwa take ba"

      "Kada kiyi man rashin kunya in 'kara karya maki 'kafa"

       "Dole ka tafi da guda biyu kabar guda biyu,kuma duk bayan wata ukku zaka dinga dawowa,sai ka dawo da wadan can ka tafi da wasu,tunda Allah bai hana maka yanda zakayi ba,idan zakayi maganar responsiblity,ina ganin idan kayi haka ka tsayar da adalci a gidanka"

    Zufa ce ke karyu mashi gaba daya ya shiga tashin hankali,anya kuwa Alhaji na sanshi,wannan wace irin *_KAD'DARA_* ce take bibiyarshi,idanuwan shi jawur ya dagu yace"Alhaji kayi hakuri bazan tafi da ku daya ba amman zan dinga dawowa duk bayan wata ukkun"

     "Bazan fa yadda ba,ka manta dalilin da yasa na aura maka mata hudun,to wannan dalilin zai sa ka tafi da matanka guda biyu duk tsiyarka dai bazaka hangen na waje ba,amman idan aka barka ka tafi kai daya ai sai abinda hali yayi"

     Wajan ajiyar motoci ya iske Ommar da kyar ya isa wajan ya dafa kofar motan ya dafe kanshi da yake sara mashi yace ma Ommar _"take me home"_

     "Ok" Ommar yace saboda yasan halin yan mazan idan ranshi ya baci.

     Tuke yake yana waiwayan inda Ali yake,babu abinda yake sai ajiyar zuciya,idanuwan shi sunyi ja,sai wani huce yake furzarwa mai zafin gaski,shi kanshi Ommar a tsoraci yake da yanayin Ali,hankalin shi ya maida saman tuken yace" _please come dwrn,_ ka dinga fadin inalillahi wa inna ilaihirraji'un,zaka samu sauki a ranka"

    Kamar baiso yace" _tanks for ur care"_

     Horn yayi mai gadi ya bude mashi ya shiga yayi parking tun kafin ya idasa gyara zaman motan Ali ya fita kamar anjefar dashi tafiya yake kamar wani zaki,addu'a Ommar yake Allah yasa babu kuwa a falon kada ya huci akan wandda yayi karu da ita,yau kuwa ta gwammaci kida da karatu.

     Bayan Ali ya shigo shima ya samu kusa dashi ya zauna ya dafa kafadarshi yace"kayi hakuri Ali,duk abinda yayi farko yana da karshi,ka cigaba da addu'a Allah ya sama maka mafita"

     Sai sannan hawaye suka cigaba da zubu mashi yace"ya zanyi da wannan masifar da take bibiyata,wai mai nayi ake mani wannan huran kamar dan sadaka ku kuma dan Allah bani"

     _"Don't say that,_an isa da kai ne,shi yasa kuma gata ake maka"

     "Gatan ne zai sa a hadani da yan iska cikin gida?iye wannan ne gata idan har ansu yiman adalci a aura mani daya mana,amman har hudu saboda ga bunsuru ku?"

     "Ka daiyi hakuri kawai,mu kuma cigaba da addu'a shine magani"

     Dakinshi ya tashi ya shige yana ta tunanin hanyar da zaibi ya kauci ma tafiya da su,amman ya kasa toilet ya fada ya sakar ma kanshi ruwa ya dadi yana sakar ma kanshi ruwa,daga bisani kuma ya dauro alwala ya shimfida sallaya ya dukufa kaima ubangiji kukanshi.

    Bayan ya tafi daki Ommar ya dauki waya ya kira Azeema bayan ta fito ta gaidashi ya amsa,yana tausaya mata yace"ki shirya kayanki zaku tafi da Ali london wajan karatunshi kida Munayya sai ki fada mata ta zauna cikin shiri"

     Wani tsalle ta daka ta rungume Ommar tace"yaya Ommar nasan wannan _fighting_ dinka ne nagode sosai"

     "Ku daya kada ki dauka ni na saka Ali ya tafi da ku,Alhaji ya matsa mashi sai ya tafi daku,banso aci dake za'ayi tafiyar ba saboda ina tausaya maki zama da Ali,amman na lura baki gani,soyayya ta rufi maki ido baki ganin abinda zai cutar daki, _any way_ ina tayaki jajan ukubar da zaku fuskanta,sannan ina tausaya maki,kuda yake tsontson da ya ja ruwa shi ruwa zai ma bugun tsiya"

      Kafi shi tayi da idanuwa"yaya inson yaya Ali kuda zai sanadin mutuwa ta KAUNA CI SILA, akalla nayi shada tunda ina matarshi kuma bazan taba daina sonshi ba,wai mayi laifina dan naci ina sonshi"

     "Kina tambaya nane ku wa kike tambaya"

     "Sorry, zan zama cikin shiri insha Allahu"

     _"Dont forget_ ki fada ma abokiyar tafiyar taki,gobe _by 4:00pm_ zaku tashi"

     "Ok,ka gaida momcyna"

     Baki ya taba yace"Allah tsari a dawo lafiya"

    Yayi gaba abinshi yana mamakin yanda take saima kanta wahala da kanta.

    Daga jiya zuwa yau MATAN ALI sun fara tantanci zama dashi amman banda Deji wadda ta iya sama ma kanta abinda zata saka ma cikinta,ita ma Azeema tadan kwaba taci ma cikinta,Munayya da Munnera ne suka tantanci saboda yunwa daman kuma babu kumai cikin gidan wanda mutum zai iya ci ba tari da ya sarrafashi ba lemonan daki cikin friage tun shikaran jiya ya kwashi ya bama su peter babu kumai cikin friage din idan ka dauki ruwan ruba faro,shi kadai ya bar masu,Munayya waya tayi ma kawarta tace tayi mata take away na chibs da jalop din shinkafa da lemuka masu dadi carton ukku,maza ta kawo mata,bata aminci zata kawo mata ba sai da ta samu tabbacin Ali baya gidan sannan ta kawo mata,ku shigowa batayi ba ta tsaya bakin gate taje ta amsa ta juya tayi tafiyarta saboda _she know who is Ali,_akwai jikarka tsakaninsu a wata mahada da suka tabayi dashi taga zallar rashin mutuncin shi,shiyasa da taji kawarta zata shiga gidan take tausaya mata,ta aure shine saboda bata san waye shiba amman ta fara sani tunda gashi ya fara masu huran yunwa.Munayya tana ta tsaya tasha labari.
     Tace"bari sai mun hadi wani wajan amman ba nan ba"

     Mamaki take na duk iya rashin kunyar Laila take tsoran Ali haka,akwai wata a kasa gaskiya baki ta taba tace"sai mun hadun dai"

     Falo ta baji kayanta tana ta cin abincinta cikin gadara saboda Munnera tana wajan kusan duk suna kasan a falo ita daman Deji ta dafa taliyarta taci da mai da yajin da innarta ta daka mata,ita ma Azeema tadan kwaba indome ta watsa ma cikinta,Munnera ci kawai baci kumai ba, har yanzo saboda ku agidan iyayanta sai dai a girka taci bata ma san hanyar cikin din gidansu ba,saboda karya kuma ita ubanta diriban babbar motane amman da yake ansa bariki gaba sai take fantamawa san ranta,kudi ba matsalarta baci,yanzo kuma ai faduwace ta aika gidansu a kawo mata abinci ita da ta aure _young millonia._

     Deji kallanta kawai take a katuwar plasma din dake manne jikin bangon falon suna kallan bollywood an saka film din Destiny na india English version,hankalinta kwanci sai dan radadi da kafarta ke mata kadan kadan.

    Azeema kuwa indome ba abincinta bani ta saba gidansu sai dai momcy ta girka aci mata abincinki na kicin tafi dauki kice,amman dai kuma maye ta ragi hanya.

     Munnera kuwa cin abinta take cikin yanga da kwalele ma Munayya,gaba daya hankalinta na kan abincin da munnera take ci,ji take kamar ta ruka to tasan idan ta roka bayan ta gama zagi mata iyayi sai ta hada mata da fallasa.

             210


   Munayya ta rasa inda zata saka kanta, yunwa tana neman illata ta,daki ta tafi ta saka kuka tana Allah wadai da auran Ali,duk yanda take tunanin abun yafi gaban nan,kasa sukune tayi ta kara saukuwa kasa idanuwanta jajir,ta karavneman kujira daya daga cikin na falon ta zauna,Munnera ta tashi ta nufi daki domin ta adana lemokanta cikin friage,tana tafiya tayi sauri ta isa bakin ledar da aka yi mata take away ta dauki guda daya,ta buye bayan kujira baki Deji da Azeema suka saki suna kallanta,wannan wace irin mace ce haka wadda bata da kamun kai bata da gudun abin wulakanci,Tsaki Azeema tayi tace"wai ana nufin dake zamu london kina irin wannan halin?"

    "London dawa kuma?"

     "Wai dake,amman gaskiya zaki sha wahala wajan Yaya Ali"

   Da sauri ta karasa inda Azeema take tace"dan Allah da gaske kike dani za'a london?"

     Kai ta kauda sannan tayi tsaki ta tashi ta nufi hanyar dakinta ba tare da tace mata kumai ba,sai da ta isa bakin kofa sannan tace"ki kasanci cikin shiri gobe zamu tashi"

    Gudu gudu ta dinga hawan binan,tsananin farin ciki ya hanata jin yunwar da ta sata,satar abinci"ke dan Allah tsaya dan girman Allah da gaski dani za'a?"

    "Idan baki yadda ba idan ya dawo ki tambaye shi mana"

    "Oh my Allah am so lucky any time,london oh yah fantastic"

    Rawa ta fara tana cikin tsananin murna,a sukwane ya shigo gidan kamar an jeho shi,ranshi a masifar baci turus yayi da ya fara cin karo da robar faro,idanuwanshi ya kara warawa sai yaga falon da take away na abinci da abubuwa dai gasu nan,gaban Deji ya dinga faduwa saboda ita kadai ce ki a falon su kuma duk suna saman binan kuwacce tsayi,daman Munnera tunda ta fito take tsayi tana jin abinda suke fada tsakanin Munayya da Azeema,su kuma daman suna bakin kofar Azeema,wani mugun kallo ya watsa masu wanda ya tada hankalinsu da kakkausar murya yace"wace shegi yace ta shigo man da abinci a gida?"

    Munayya tace" gatanan shafarfa da mai diyar masu kudi,diyar dan karuna"ta nuna Munnera da hannu.

     Ranshi bace ya maida kallanshi kanta"are you?"

   Idanuwanta ta watsa mashi tace"akwai abinyi ne,ka wani zubu mani idanuwa kamar zaka cinyeni"

     Kamar zaki ya wurgar da jikka a tamanin ya dinga haura binan,da sauri ta juya ta shige daki ta murza makulle,kamar zai cire kofar yake bugawa har huce yake saboda bacin rai da tashin hankali zuciyarshi ta fara sama tana kasa,da gudun tsiya Deji ta fada kicin ta kulle ta danne tana haki tana kuka mara sauti a zuciyarta take fadi _anya gidan nan akwai rahama a zamanta kuwa,gida kullum mutum cikin zullumi,gaskiya zata samu Alhaji ta fada mashi ita dai ya maidata gidansu, wajan Innarta da Baffanta dan bata ga wajan zama ba,daman haka auran yaki,kawarta Asabi da akai mata aure mijinta kullum sai yayi mata waka kuma yana sonta bai san abinda zai bata mata rai,amman ita kullum da tashin hankali take kwana da zullumi take tashi_daman Munayya da Azeema kuwa yasan matsayin shi cikin daki,sun dadi da fadawa daki suka saka makulle suma.

    Haka ya sauku binan cikin tsananin damuwa na rashin kamata da yayi a zuciyarshi yake ayyana irin azabar da zai mata idan sukayi haduwa ta biyu,dakinshi ya shiga ya fada saman gado a fili ya furta"wannan wace irin masifa ce,take barazana da rayuwata"

    Tashi yayi da sauri ya fada toilet ya dauro alwala ya gabatar da salla raka'a biyu sannan ya daga hannu ya dinga jiro ma Allah kirari _ya Allah kada ka hukuntani da laifin da na tuba a kanshi,ya Allah ka duba lamurana kaji tausai na ka zaba mani mafita,ya Allah ka sassauta zuciyar mahaifina ya aminci mani da sauwake ma wadannan bayi naka,ya Allah ka zaba mani mafita da abinda yafi alkairi a rayuwa ta,ya Allah na kaskantar da kaina a garika ina tawassali da sunayanka kyawawa,Allah ka bani mafita ka sanyaya mani zuciyata da tsananin tsanarsu_

   Tashi yayi ya kwanta bisa duguwar kujirarshi yana maimaita _innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirra'ji'un,_a hankali yaji zuciyarshi na sauka tana sanyi a nan bacci ya dauki shi,mai dadi da kwanciyar hankali,bayan gari ya waye ya tashi ranshi sayau ya fara hada kayanshi yana 'yar waka.

             215




     Wayarshi ce tayi ringing ya d'aga,gaban shi na fad'uwa cikin girmama wa yace"Alhaji barka da tashi"

    "Ka tashi lafiya?"
 Daga daya bangaran aka fada.

    Ali yace"Lafiya lau"

  "Alhamdulillahi,ya maganar tafiya ina fatan kun fara shiri"

    "Eh na fara"

  "Sauran matan naka fa?"

   "Suma sun fara"

  "Masha Allahu,ku zauna cikin shiri zamu zo domin muyi maku rakiya zuwa airport"

    Kamar baya so yace"to"

    Daina hada kayan yayi ranshi na matukar suya,baya da wata mafita sai na tafiya dasu din,amman fa zasu yaba ma aya zakinta, saboda zasu gwammaci basu wanzo a duran duniya ba,tsaki yayi ya cigaba da hada kayan shi,kafin ya shiga toilet ya d'auku wayan shi ya danna kira daga d'aya bangaran aka dauka"ya ne?"

   "Kazo ina gida please"

    "Mai zan maka,idan nazo din?"

     "Dalla malam kazo mana muyi ban kwana kada in tafi muna fushi da juna,ka sani ko idan na tafi bazan dawo ba"

    Tsaki yayi yace"bana kusa amman idan na dawo zan zo"

     "Babu damuwa sai kazo,amman kada ka manta 4:00pm zan tashi"

     "Zaku tashi dai,in gyara maka"

    "Kai dai ka sani,sai ka shigo ina jiranka"

     Girgiza kai Ommar yayi yana tausayama 'yar uwarshi na zaman da zasuyi da Ali mai wuyar hali da mugunta.

     Munnera shiri take gadan-gadan kamar wadda zata bar duniya gaba daya,duk wani abu da tasan zata bukata ta adanashi cikin jikkar da take tunanin zata iya daukar mata duk abinda zata bukata,sannan ta dauki wayarta ta danna ma kawarta kira.

    "Slimy ya bada magungunan kuwa? kinsan daman yau na ce maki zamu tashi"

     " kidai ki bari inzo zan maki bayanin kumai, idan kikayi yanda ya fada sai yanda kikayi da _young millonia_ kima ki zama tajira"

    Dariya ta sheki da ita tace"dan Allah kizo yanzo kada ya ganki,ya hanaki shigowa kinsan dan bala'i ne"

     " zai bari ne sai, yanda mukayi dashi"

     "Shegiya sai yanda nayi dashi dai"

     "Ganinan dai zuwa muney"

  Tsalle tayi tace _"Ali am very sorry,you have to pay for your crime"_

    Magungunan matan da ta ba Slimy ta seyo mata ta zuba cikin jikkarta ta adana abunta saboda tana da tabbacin zasuyi mata amfani.

   Azeema kuwa babu abinda takeyi sai kuka tana tausaya ma zaman da zasuyi da Ali,saboda bata hangu akwai al'fanuba zaman da zasuyi ba,zata nemi alfarma a tafe da d'aya daga cikin sauran,saboda bazata juri wahala ba, wayanta ta dauku ta danna ma Ommar kira ya dauka "akwai matsala ne"

      "Yaya dan Allah ka sa ya canza tafiyar nan a saka wata ta bishi zuwa muga yanda za'ayi nan gaba"

    "Mai ke faruwa?"

 Cikin kuka tace"bana ganin alfanu a tafiyarnan kuma kasan ba sanmu yake ba zamu sha wahalar zama dashi, musamman tunda zamuyi nisa daku"

     "Baki da wani zabi sai na tafiya tunda ki kika zaba ma kanki Ali a natsayin mijin aure,nasha zaunar daki in fada maki cewa kada ki aura Ali a lokacin haushina ma kika dinga ji"

     "Yaya ka taimaka mani"

    "Lest talk another matter,idan kuma baki da wani abun fadin sai na shigo gidan" ya kashi wayanshi.

    Minti goma tsakani ya shigo gidan yayi parking a inda ake ajiyar motoci ya gaisa da mai'gadi sannan ya shiga gidan,dakin Ali ya shiga ya iske shi zaune bisa sofa yana kallan resline,amman gaba daya hankalinshi baya wajan kallan kawai dai yake.

   Dafa kafadarshi yayi yace"Ali mai mata hudu,kaddai kaci man ka fara shiga tension din mata tun yanzo?"

      "Ka bari kawai"

"Hmmm Adai yi kukarin yin adalci saboda Allah zai tambayika ya ka tafiyar da iyalinka,kayi adalci ko bakayi ba ,duk zai tambayika wallahi bana san Allah ya jarrabika da abubuwan rayuwa shiyasa kaga ina maka magana"

    "Nafa gode abokina"

"A haka kamar kana jin magana,amman kamar kun hada iri da karuna,wajan taurin kai"

     "Am kadan masu magana Alhaji yace zuwa 3:00pm zai zo suyi man rakiya zuwa airport,to ni bana iya yi masu magana dan Allah ka fada ma kanwarka su zama ready"

     "Kai mai yasa bazaka fada masu ba"

    "Saboda bansan ya zan fara magana dasu ba,bama zan iya ba"

    "Ne kuma bazan ma kuwacce magana ba, tunda ba ne ki auran su ba,idan bazaka yi masu ba sai dai a barshi"

     "Please ai naci maka"

     "Baka da hankali wallahi,kaifa ne mijinsu ba neba"

     _"Just do it for the last time"_

    Waya ya dauku ya danna ma Azeema kira da sauri ta dauka tace"yaya kayi mai magana"

    "Plese ki daina wannan maganar,ina dakin Ali ina jiranki"

     "Inajin tsoron zuwa yaya Ommar"

     "Kizo mana,ba ni naci kizo ba"

   "Ok ina zuwa"

  Tsaki yayi yace"yaushi zaka gyara rayuwar gidanka ,matanka na aure suna tsoranka kamar mala'ikan mutuwa,saboda muguwar tsanar da kai masu"

     "Ka dinga duba yanda abubuwan suke ,kafin idawo _please work hard"_

     _"Never mind,_za'ayi bakin iyawa"

             220


      Azeema ce tayi sallama daga bakin kofa,Ommar ya amsa mata ta shigo cikin sanyin jiki ta gaida shi, da kyar ya amsa shima dan Ommar yana wajan ne.

    "Da fatan kun gama shirin?"

    Inda Ali yake ta kalla ,kamar ma baisan da wasu halitta ba a wajan,haka yayi yana danna wayarshi,hawayanta ta goge tace"nidai na gama"

    "Ita 'yar uwar taki fa?"

  "Bansan wani hali take ciki ba"

    "Ki shaida mata kada ta bata lokaci 4:00pm dai dai zaku fita daga gidan nan"

    "To,bari in fada mata"

    "Babu damuwa Matar Ali"

    Jikinta babu 'kwari haka ta fita tana share hawayan da suka dadi suna bin fuskarta,dakin Munnera ta shiga ta iske ta ta gama shiri tana hakimci saman kujira tana waya,ganin shiguwar Azeeman ne yasa tayi sauri ta katse tana maida dubanta a kanta tace"lafiya kika shigo man daki babu sallama?"

    "Macen da ta amsa sunanta mace ita ake ma sallama,bawai muna mata ba"

    "To yanzo mai ya kawo na ki gaya mani magana?"

    "Angaya maki maganar idan da abinda zakeyi bismillah"

    Tsaki tayi tace"fice mun a daki tun kan ki raina kanki"

    "Banza kawai,ki zama cikin shiri zaki bi mijinki wanda baisan darajarki ba,inda za'a saita maki 'kwa'kwalwa"

    "Idan kin gama haukan naki kina iya tafiya"

    Tsaki tayi ta fito ta buga murfin kofan da karfi,tana masifa ita kadai,horn din mota akai ma maigadi, dubawa yayi ta wata 'yar kofa,ai da sauri ya wangame gate din,motocine na alfarma masu masifar kyau da kudi bakake wulik guda ukku duk iri daya suka shigo gidan,basu shiga _parking space_ ba sai dai suka parka cikin filin daki tsakiyar gidan,Alhaji Ahmad ya fito daga gefinshi Hajja ce rike da hannun Aminatu,daya motar kuma driver ne shi kadai,sai dayar kuma wani kyarkyawan saurayi ne ya fito da ka ganshi kaga jinin Ali,sai dai Ali ya fishi kyau da kamewa,yanayin fuskarshi kawai idan ka kalla zaka san yana da kiriki da rashin kamewa.

     Gaba dayansu suka rugunduma suka shiga gidan,amman banda driva,palon shiru kamar babu kuwa a cikin gidan,sallama suka karayi,Aminatu ce ta tashi ta nufi dakin Ali ta kwan_kwasa daga ciki aka bata izinin shigowa,Ommar ta iske zaune saman duguwar kojera,yana kallan resline koerar kusa dashi ta zauna tana kasa_kasa da idanuwa tace"Ya Ommar kana nan ashi?"

     "Ina nan wallahi mutumin sai nuku nuku yake kamar mace,ya kasa gama shirin har yanzo"

    "Yana ina wai?"

  "Yana daki mana,bansan abinda yake ba yafi minti goma ciki"

    "Bari in gani"

  "K!zonan kika san a halin da zaki iske shi,ki bari kawai ya fito ya gama duk wani iskancin shi ya fito,tunda ji yake yau kamar anci zai tafiya da shahararun makiyanshi"

    "Tare fa muke da duka family munzo rakiya kasan kuma Alhaji baya san jira"

    "Da gaski kike My D?"

    "Allah kuwa suna falo,muna ta sallama ne ma mukaji shiru shine naci bari inzo ingane"

    "Wayar shi ya dauku yayi dialing no' Ali ,amman sai ya jita kusa da inda yake,tsaki yayi ya tashi ya nufi beedroom dinshi,ga mamakin shi sai ya hange shi tsakiyar gado yayi rufda ciki,da mamaki ya isa kusa dashi ya taba shi" wai kai wani irin iskanci ne haka?"

     Tsaki Ali yayi ya juyu,idanuwanshi da Ommar ya gani sun rine sun kuma jajir,shi ya bashi tsoro a hankali yace"mai ke faruwa Ali?"

    _"Noting bad"_

 "Wani irin _noting bad,_kana cikin wannan _stuation_ haka"

    "Kai na yake man cewo kamar in jifar dashi haka nake ji wallahi"

    _"You see,_abinda nake gudar maka kinan amman baka sauraran ma duk wanda zai baka baki akan ka kwantar da hankalinka,yanzo gasu Alhaji can palo suna jiranka,kasan bazai taba jin dadi ba ya ganka cikin wannan hali"

    "Tun yaushi suka iso?"

     "Yanzo meena ta shigo taci suna ta sallama babu kuwa shine tayu _site_ dinka"

     _"Please_ bani medicine dina insha sai mu fita"

      "Yana ina?"

   "Cikin _bag_ din can" ya nuna mashi wata jikka ya dauku magungunan sunfi kala goma,mika mashi sannan ya dauku mashi ruwa a _bedsite friage_ dinshi ya zuba ya mika mashi haka ya dinga shan magungunan kamar wanda cutar duk duniya ta kamashi.

     Shiru Aminatu taji sai ta biyu bayansu,waje ta samu bakin gado ta zauna tana tambayar Lafiya kuwa.

     Ommar yace"kanshi ke ciwo kadan amman yasha magani yanzo zai tashi mu fita"

     "Ya Ali _please be a man,_dan Allah kada ka bari wadannan matan su kwantar man da kai ,bazan taba yafi ma ku daya daga cikinsu ba"

    Hannunta ya kamu yace _"be a good girl,_kuda bana nan nasan zaki kula da kanki bana san yawan fita babu dalili sannan ki maida hankali akan karatunki kada inzo in samu wani abunda bai mani ba kinsan zamu bata sosai daki "

      _"As you wish big bro"_

     _"Ya Ali to smile for me"_

      "Dariya ya saka yace" zanfi _missing_ dinki feyi da kuwa kida _my best friend for ever"_

      "Muma zamuyi naka sosai ku ya Ommar?"

      "Fadin wannan duk wani abune na bata lokaci,tunda munsan wani abu ne tabbatacci dole sai ya faru"

       Ali yace _"love y'all"_ 

Ya kama hannun Ommar ya hada da na Aminatu yace"ga amanar kanwata nan na baka kada ta nemi wani abu ta rasa indai anyi shi a duran duniya,nasan baka da matsaka _you love her soo much,_ban da matsala da kai, _and you sele girl,_kada inji kada inzo in gani wani abunda zaisa inji ina jin haushin ki, _all ready_ nasan kinsan like dina da dislike dina,babu wani dalili da zaisa in kara maki bayani akai, _be a good girl"_

      "I will insha Allahu"

 ta fada tana goge hawayan fuskarta.

    Handcacheirf Ommar ya dauku ya mika mata yace"ban yadda kuda digon hawaye ya kara zuba a kyarkyawar fuskar nan taki ba,idan kuma ba haka ba zan ma yayanki wulakanci"

      Dariya suka saka gaba dayansu tace"na daina sweet D"

    "Tunda kun fara ku fita ina zuwa,tantabaro sarakan soyayya"

             225




    Gaba d'aya matan gidan sun hallara tsakiyar falon kuwa yana kwasar gaisuwa wajan Alhaji Ahmad surukinsu da Hajja,'yar nasiha Alhaji ya karayi masu da lallashin Munayya da Deji akan da yayi wata ukku zai dawo da Munnera da Azeema ya tafi dasu,kada su damu su kuma tada hankalinsu,ya kara da shi masu albarka.

    Babu kunya Munnay tace"Alhaji ai tafiyarshi ta feyi mana alkairi saboda tunda aka kawo mashi mu gidan nan bamu taba minti biyar cikin kwanciyar hankali ba,daga shikaran jiya zuwa yau huran yunwa yake mana"

    Karaf a kunnuwan Ali wanda fituwarshi kinan daga dakinshi yana daura agogon hannu,yaji maganarta ta karshi _da yunwa yake barinmu_ wani mugun kallo suka jefi juna dashi.

    Ran Alhaji idan yayi dubu a bace yake,a fadace yakai dubanshi akan fuskar Ali yace"maganar da ta fada gaskiya ne ku karya ne?"

    Shiru yayi saboda baida bakin magana amman yasan daga jiya ya fara yi masu haka kuma ai akwai kumai aje girka masu ne dai yace an daina.

    "Karya take Baffa ku yau ma abinci da yawa aka dafa har barinshi akayi, sai dai masu aiki suka dauka suka cinyi,bai barinmu da yunwa"

    Ranshi yaji yayi sanyi da yaji abinda Deji ta fada amman yana san ya gasgata abinda ita Dejan ta fada ya dubi Azeema yace"abinda ta fada gaskiya ne ku ya abin yake"

     "Ina azumine shiyasa ban sauku kasa ba kwata kwata yau sai da zamu tafi din nan"

      "Maganar kwana biyu da ya wuce fa?"

     "Yana bamu,kuma akwai wadatacci a cikin store duk mai so sai ya girka"

     "Ku kuke girka abinku tunda kuka zo kinan"

   Da sauri Deji tace"wannan christan ne yake dafawa kullum daga safi zuwa rana hadda ma na dari"

     Masha Allahu bana fatan a kafa rashin adalci a gidan nan duk mai wata matsala kada yake fada,kumai yayi maku a sanar dani"

     Ido Hajja ta kura ma Deji saboda ta gama fahimtar duk abinda Munnayya ta fada haka yake,amman Deji tayi saurin kari laifinshi irin wannan matan sune ake alfahari dasu,kuma kuwace uwa take san danta ya aura.

    Waje Ali ya samu ya zauna ranshi na kuna yace"Alhaji barka da yamma"

     "Barka dai,da fatan kun gama shiri?"

      Kamar baya so yace"Eh,mungama"

    "To muna iya tafiya ai ko?"

    Gaba daya suka tashi,domin tafiya har sun kai bakin kofa Alhaji yace"ku bazaju ji rakiyan mijinku ba?"

    Ba kunya Munayya tace"Allah ya raka taki gona,zan huta da jaraba"bata taba tunanin cewa gaban sirikinta take ba,saboda kunya Deji kasa tayi da idonta saboda bata saba ganin wannan rashin kunyar ba.

    Ran Aminatu bace tace"ke wai uban waye ubanki da kike san kiyima mutane rashin kunya"

     Tsawa Alhaji ya daka mata yana yi mata gargadi da cewa matar yayanta ce.

     Duk wannan abin da yake faruwa Ali suna can har sun shiga mota shida Ommar,wata ya hanga tana kukarin shigowa cikin gidan suna magana da maigadi sai yaga ta mika mashi leda ya amsa,ya kara maida hankalinshi sai ya ga slimy ce daman yasan tantiriyar yar iska ce,mamaki ya cika shi da tambayar kanshi _mai ya hada maigadinshi da mata irin su slimy,_sai yaga gaba daya sun fito sun dusu wajan motocin Munnera yaga ta tsaya tana waya kamar minti biyu sai yaga ta nufi wajan mai gadi ta amshi ledar da yaga slimy ta bashi, _mai ya hada wannan yarinyar da slimy?defnetly i have to findout_

     Aminatu da Ommar da Ali a mota daya,Hajja da Alhaji a mota daya,Munnera da Azeema a mota daya,horn suka yima maigadi ya wangame masu motar suka tafi,airport din max air dake kano.

     Mari munayya ta wanki Deji dashi tace"uban wa ke maki karya,naci uban wa ke maki karya ,da na fadi magana kika karyata ne"

     Kuncinta ta dafi sannan ta tsorama Munayya idanuwanta da suke zubar da hawaye tace"Innata ta hurine da fadin laifin mijina duk rintsi duk wahala,kuma tace kada in taba kai kararshi wajan iyayanshi ku wajan wani,idan yayi fushi dani in duka in bashi hakuri"

     "Banza sakara uwar kankanba da shirshige,Innarkin banza,sakara aljana"

    "Kada ki kara zagarmani Inna saboda bata san abinda akeyi ba"

    "An zagita din,akwai abinda zakiyi?"

             230



Kuka Deji ta saka tana ma Munayya Allah ya isa da ta zagar mata Inna.

   Munayya tace"kada ki sake ki zagan saboda yanzo daga ni sai ki a gidan nan in maki bugun tsiya babu uban da zai amshiki,idan kuma kina ganin wasa ki gwada ki gani"

    Shiru Deji tayi saboda bata da mafita sai dai ta barma Allah ikonshi.

     Max air dake airport din kano,suka isa,kuwa saukuwa yayi suka nufi wasu kujeru,wadan da daman an tanar dasune saboda idan ana bukatar hakan, suka zauna,Alhaji ya maida hankalin shi akan Ali yace"Ali zunan wajan mana"

    Yasan tatsoniyar gizo bata wuce ta koke,kafadar shi Alhaji ya dafa yace"ban aminci maka ka dinga cutar yaran mutane ba,kuma kada ka manta amana na baka,idan ka wulakanta su kana ganin bana kusa ni ka wulakanta saboda haka kayi hakuri ka zauna dasu cikin hakuri da lumana,kada ka zargi kuwa cikin wannan lamari kafi kuwa kamashu saboda abubuwan da ka dinga shukawa rayuwarka na mu'amala da mata,shiyasa na nema maka auran nata hudu kila a samu sauki ku  kuma ka daina gaba daya,to Alhamdulillhi a duk iyakar idan da na saka maka ban kara kamaka da wani laifin ba,saboda haka dan Allah kada ka canza hali idan kaje can"

    Zuciyarshi na 'kuna ya kada kai yace"insha Allahu"

    "Yauwa ,Allah yayi maka albarka"

    Can kasan makushi yace"ameen nagode"

     Itama Hajja kara ja mashi kunne tayi ta kuma kara bashi hakuri da duk abinda zai fuskanta daga matanshi,sai yayi hakuri ya kauda kai,itama godiya yayi mata ya kuma jaddada mata bazata samishi da wani abu makamancin wannan ba.

    Sanarwa aka farayi akan saura minti biyar 5 a tashi,can nesa dasu kadan ya hango Aminatu tana kuka ta'ke karasuwa inda suke,wajanta ya nufa ya kamo hannunta yace _"stop crying_ mana _big girl_ kada ki badani mana"

    _"Yaya is not joking_ zanyi missing dinka da yawa kuma zaka tafi da matanka zasu dinga bata maka rai, bana san ka shiga cikin wani _tension_ wallahi _please can i go with you?"_

    _"Never mind Menata i have to handle everthing_ kada ki d'auka zasu wani tada mani hankali,no"

    _"Are you sure?"_

  _"Very much sure_ ma kuwa" ya fada yana rungumeta sannan ya kamu hannunta suka nufi inda sauran abokan tafiyar shi suke.

    Mahmu ya zo kusa dashi yace _"Broda please take care of ur self"_

   _"Never mind,i will, the stobborn guy in the Alhaji Ahmad estate"_

    "Haba ya Ali kumai ya zama tarihi insha Allahu"

    Kafadar shi ya dafa yace"Mahmud zanyi matukar alfahari dakai idan har ka zama na kwarai"

    "Baka da matsala dani _the must silent and beautiful young millonia in Alhaji Ahmad estate"_

     Gaba dayansu suka saka dariya banda Ali wanda shi murmushi kawai yayi,baki da hanci Munnera ta saki tana kallan yanda Ali keta raha da 'yan uwanshi daman haka yake mata da ta malla ka mashi dan sauran raguwar budurcin da bata idasa barbadarwa ba,gaban tane ya bada mummunan faduwa da tayi tunanin idan Ali yazo kusantar ta yajita an taba shigarta ya zatayi,ai tasan ranar sai ya kasheta,ai kuma Slimy ta tabbatar mata da cewa boka mai gani har naji yace mata bazai gane ba,sai taji hankalinta ya kwanta duk tana tsurama hallittarshi kallo take wannan tunani,kamar anci ya kalle inda take yaga ta tsurama gaban wandan shi ido,kamar mayya ku kiftawa batayi.

    Sanarwa aka farayi na su matsu kusa kuwa yaji sunanshi a fara shiga jirge,Ali ya kama hannun Ommar yace _"the best friend ever in my life_,na bar maka amanar kumai nawa ciki hadda Menata,nasan banda matsala dakai"

   Tsaki Ommar yayi yace"kai banza ne ai yima kaini kula da abinda nafi so a rayuwata,saboda haka kada ka dinga yiman shirshige akan abinda ya zama tamkar ibada a wajena kula da _my D_ ta"

     Duka Ali ya kaimashi yana dariya yace"wai kai gidan uban wa ka iya soyayya haka bayan kuwani lokaci muna tari da kai ku bacci tare muke kuma banga kana mu'amala da 'yan mata ba"

     Kusa dashi ya kuma ya rada mashi dai dai saitin kunanshi wanda sai shi kadai zaiji yace"an gaya maka ni irinka ne sai dai kaci yaran mutane kuma ka kuda masu mari"

     Dariya suka saka gaba daya Ali yace"da rashin mutunci zamu rabu kinan?"

     Kara shagala Munnera tayi wajan kallan Ali yana dariya yayi mata masifar kyau bakinshi take kallo tana _imagining_ yanda zaiyi _franch kiss_ da ita,ai kuwa sai taji tsigar jikinta ta mutsa,wata sha'awarshi ta mutsa mata,bata an kara ba sai taji an take mata yan yatsu hadda murjesu wata siririyar kara ta saki,da sauri ta dagu kanta taga waye yayi mata wannan cin mutuncin sai taga Ali daf da ita kamar zai fada mata yace"banza mayya uban me kike kallona"

     Ta bude baki zatayi magana yasa hannu guda biyu ya d'alle mata baki yace"kikai maga sai naci uwar mamanki,daga ganinki harijace ki,inba haka ba uban wa ya baki damar kallan mani wando"

   Zata kara bude baki domin ta kare kanta ya kara murza takalminshi kasan kafarta ba shiri hawaye suka fara sintiri bisa fuskarta yace"kinsan wannan abun yafi karfinki sai mace mai aji zata samu damar da zan iya ahigarta amman ba kazama zaka irinki ba,maza goge hawayanki so kike aga kina kuka aci na cutar dake ku,ai idan kin kwantar da hankalinki zakiyi kuka ina da tabbacin hadda na jini ma sai kinyi karewar na ruwa"

    Ba shiri ta goge fuskarta daga nisa zaka zata  wasu muggan couples ne masu ji da soyayya nan kuwa basu san muguntar Ali bace ta mutsa.

   Azeemace rike da hannun Ommar tana kuka,hankalinta bai kara tashi ba sai da taji an fara kiran sunan masu shiga cikin jirge.

    Ommar yace"sai kinyi hakuri da halin Ali,abinda kawai zakiyi kada ki dinga tursasa ma kanki sai kin shiga rayuwarshi,amman duk wani abu da kikasan ya rataya a kanki na matsayinki na mata ki tabbatar kina aiwatar mashi amman kada ki sama ranki zaki samu Ali saboda bafa sanku yake ba,amman kada ki manta da addu'a"

    Kai kawai ta iya gyadawa,tana sharce hawayan fuskarta saboda duk yanda tayi tunanin zama *_MATAR ALI_* abin yafi gaban nan,sunayansu aka fara kira daya bayan daya suka shiga Ommar na daga ma kanwarshi hannu yana tausaya mata zama da mai hali irin na Ali  wanda idan ya nuna baisan abu to baifa san nashi,amman ta dage sai ta aureshi gashi tunma zaman bai fara ba ta fara ganin ba dai dai ba, daman Ommar ya fada mata abinda zata fuskanta tace mashi ai itama mace ce,zata iya jawo hankalinshi wajanta,baisan abinda zata iske ba hankalin shi idan yayi dubu tashi yake ji yake kamar ya hana tafiyar da ita,dan _weaving_ yayi ma Ali wanda,shi Ali ya gane damuwar dake bisa fuskar Ommar murmushi yayi mashi na babu gudu babu ja da baya tsuntsun da yaja ruwa shi ruwa zai doka,suka shige cikin jirge kuwa da abinda ke nukurkusar shi cikin zuciyarshi.


   *****

Zama tsakanin Munayya da Deji babu yabu babu fallasa dan ma tana raga mata saboda duk tsiyarta bata manta alkairin da mutum yayi mata,bata manta taimakon da Deji tayi mata ba lokacin da Ali yaci ubanta,dalili kinan da yasa take dan raga mata bata cika takura taba sosai take da fredom kadan a gidan,da aci Munnera aka barsu da ita tana da tabbacin daya sai ya kashi daya saboda ta tsanita feyida kuwacce halitta a duran duniya,yau ma kamar kullum ta fito tana jin yunwa amman babu abincin da zata watsa ma cikinta gashi kuma Ali yayi ma peter kashidin kuda wasa idan ya shiga gidan da sunan yi masu girki abakin aikin shi,zama tayi bisa  _onesiter_ na kujeron falon tana tunanin hanyar da ya kamata tabi,kamar an mintsineta ta nufi _compound_ din su peter,ta tsaya ta kwala mashi kira jiki na rawa ya fito ya duka kasa yace"gani ma"

     "Wanda ya baka umarnin ka daina dafa mana abinci baya nan, saboda haka wuce katai ka fara aikin ka saboda babu ubanda zai zauna gidan nan ba tari da yana aikin da aka daukushi domin shi ba"

     "Ma,sir yace kada in saki in shiga site dinku idan na shiga abakin aikina"

    "Haka kuma idan baka girka man abinda zanci ba yau sai dai ka kwana gidan ubanka,dan na rantsi da Allah kurar kare zan maka"

     "Ma _am sorry but_ bazan iya shiga in dafa maki abinci ba, wanda ya kawoni ya bani umarni kinga bazan iya karya mashi doka ba"

     "Kai da ogan naka kunci ubanku saboda haka maza ka fara hada kayanka kafin in fito yanzon nan zanga ubanda zai hana ka tafi daga gidan nan idan ogan naka zai dawo sai mugani, _get ready or else"_ ta nuna shi da yatsa.

    Ta juya ta nufi cikin gidan nasu ta dauku kudi ta nufi bakin gate tace ma maigadi ya samu wani ya bashi yayi mata takeaway a *Al'AMEER RESTAURANTE* ya amsa cikin girmama wa.

    Waya peter ya daga ya kira Ali yana kuka ya fada mashi abinda ke faruwa da kurar da Munayya tayi mashi,ran Ali idan yayi dubu ya baci lallai zaiyi maganin wannan yarinyar saboda tana son ta dinga karya mashi dokar da ya sanya" kumai zata yi maka kada ka tafi _give me 10m"_

     _"Ok sir"_

 Waya ya kira meena ya fada mata cewa kume take ta hada kayanta ta kuma gidan shi,da zama kafin ya dawo next weak,tambayar shi tayi dalili yace _"just do as i say_"jin da tayi cikin fada yake magana sai ta tashi da sauri ta shirya kayanta a cikin yar jikka wadda zata ci kala biyar ta sauku kasa ta iske hajja a cikin kicin tana girki tace"Hajja big bros yace in kuma gidanshi da zama wai zai zo next weak"

    "Lafiya ko?" Hajja ta bukata.

    "Shine dai ban sani ba wallahi,bari inyi sauri yace yanzon nan kila wani abun ke faruwa a gidan"

     "Kada kiyi dreving kisa a kaiki kinji?"

     "Ok Hajjana, _as you wish"_

    "Idan kin isa kin kira inji yanda akai"

     "To,na tafi"

"Allah ya tsarimin Auta na"
