Audi mai gadi tayi ma magana da sauri ya aje wanken kayan shi da yake,yazo inda take yace"Hajiya 'karama lafiya dai ko?"

   "Ah lafiya lau baba maigadi ina driver yake?"

    "Inajin yana sashinsu,kina da bukatar shi ne?"

    "Da za'a taimaka man da nayi farin ciki da hakan"

    Bata amsa ba tadan ja baya ta kuma gefi daya ta rungume hannuwa tana jiran ya gyara rebas din ta shiga su tafi,wayanta ce tayi kara da sauri ta daga,daga bangaranta tace "Ya Ali na fito wallahi,Hajja ta hana wai inyi driving da kaina sai dai wai a kaini amman gashi nan yana gyara rebas zamu tafi"

    Daga can bangaran nashi yace _"you have to be hurry please"_

    _"What the mattar_ ne wai?"

    _"Just go there_,zaki sani idan kinji _pease be fast"_

    _"On my way"_

Motan ta shiga suka kama hanya sai gidan Ali mai mata hudu _the beautiful young millonia_

  A can gidan Ali kuwa,Munayya ta kara kumawa site din su peter tana mashi tejara akan sai yabar gidan,tunda ba gidan ubanshi bane,hakuri ya dinga bata amman fir ta kiya da kanta ta shiga dakin nashi ta dinga wurgu mashi da kaya tana zaginshi,duk ma'aikatan tsaye sukayi suna mamakin tejara irin ta Munayya,horn akayi da sauri maigadi ya tashi ya bude saboda yaga motar kuma ya gane ta,parking space akayi parking Aminatu ce ta fito,minti daya bayan fituwarta wayarta ta fara ringing dagawa tayi tace"Ya Ali ina gidan yanzo muka shigo"

    "Ke neme peter ya fada maki abinda ke faruwa,ki dau hukunci dai dai da yanda tunaninki ya baki, _and dont go easy for any body,_ wannan umarnine na baki"

    "Ya Ali ai gaya can daya daga cikin matanka tana watso mashi da kaya waje,wai mai ke faruwa?"

    _"Take care of everthing"_

   _"Ok sweet broda"_dittt ya kashe waya.

  Da takunta na kasaita ta idasa wajan tana isa da sauri peter yayu kanta ya durkusa har kasa yace _"small Hajiya please help me,_ zata kuran"

    _"Get up_,akan wani dalili zata kureka"

    Sai ga Munayya ta fito da akwatin kayan peter a hannu ta wurgushi sai a gaban Aminatu yayi burki,kallan Aminatu tayi sama da kasa sannan ta maida dubanta akan peter tace"kafin in kirga biyar kayi maza ka hada wadannan tsummukaran naka ka kama gabanka,idan na kara ganin maikama dakai ma kaja mashi musiba"

 "Fuck you" ta daga danyatsa sama."ma din uwarka"

     "Babu Small Hajiya"

   "Akwai shegiyar da take biyanka albashi tunda ka fara aiki a gidan nan?"

     "Babu"

 "To babu wata matsiyaciya irin tsiya wadda bata iya dibar arziki ba,da ta isa tace ka fita dan uban babanta,idan kuma akwaita bismilla duk uban da ya fasa yaci kaza kazan shi"

    Kallan ido cikin ido sukeyi ita da Munayya,Munayya bata hango cewa zataci bulus ba sai ha hau burin kunya"ai daman kannan miji matsiyata ne,ina ruwanki da harkar gidanmu"

    "Banza matsiyaciya kefa babu wanda ya taba maki shaidar mutunci,bansan inda ki kayo gado ba,wajen tsohu ku wajan tsohuwa oho,kuda yake ubanki baida masifa sai dai barawo ne,kila uwar kika biyu saboda tun bamu kai haka ba mukeji ana mijin hajiya"

   Ai kuwa a firgece Munayya ta zuba ma Aminatu na mujiya idanuwanta sunyi jajir saboda tsabar tijara,amman kuma ta gagara furta kumai,idan ma ta furtan bata san abinda zata furtan ba,jin zuciyarta take kamar zata fito waje,lallai ga tabiyun Ali kuda yake Ali mugunta kawai ya iya amman ita wannan tantiriyar marakunya ce,nuna peter tayi da hannu tae"wallahi idan bakabar gidan nan ba nice ajalinka,ku kuma ka dinga dafa mana abinci kamar yanda ya zama aikinka"

    "Bazai dafa ba,
duk wacce bazata dafa da kanta ba sai dai ta tafi gidan ubanta,daga yau ma an daina girki gidan nan sai dai kuwacce ta dafa da kanta"

    Munayya ta nuna Aminatu da hannu tace"kada in kara zartar da hukunci ki kawo man rashin kunya cikin hidimata,in ba haka ba zan maki azabarban bugu "

    "Nafi karfin bugu wajanki,kuma Allah ya tsaran da hada jiki da matsiyaciya irinki,amman kinga wadan nan su zan saka suyiman dai dai da kasusuwan jikinki idan kuma kina ganin wasa bismillah"

Duk yanda Munayya tasu tijara a wajan taga babu damar hakan,kwafa tayi tace ma peter zasu hadu, _next meet_,zai raina kanshi.

   "Sai dai idan Ya Ali ya dawo,amman indai ina gidan nan babu wannan maganar"

   Tsaki tayi tace"sa'idawa masu zuwa lahira da kukun dambu,banza kalarki kalar azzalumin wanki gadan jaraba"

    Dariya Aminatu tayi tace"oho dai kwadayi yasa aka aure yayana, munga yanda za'ayi handama ai,marasa wadatar zuciya,diyar tsohun barawo"

    Tsaki tayi ta kuma cikin gida saboda ga alama wannan ta fita rashin mutunci, _saukinta ma Allah yasuta Munnera bata nan da taga lagwanta yanda ta matsa mata,Allah yasa dai ba zama zatayi dasu ba aikuwa da,da akwai matsala._

    Bayan ta Aminatu ta ruf, itama ta shigo cikin gidan,duguwar kujera ta samu ta zauna,sannan ta kira Ali take fad'a mashi yanda sukayi da Munayya,dariya yayi yace"kada ki sake ki raga masu,sannan kada peter ya kara shigowa da sunan dafa abinci,kuwacce ta dafa da kanta,ki dai nasan baki da matsala saboda huran Hajja ce"

     "Eh gaskiya,amman Ya Ali wannan matar taka batada mutunci kaga yanda take zagin ma'aikatan gidan nan kuwa"

   "Eh mana,ai kinga ga ranarta tazo kuh"

   ABROAD.... 
   LONDON


    Gidan da suka sauka maikyau ne dai dai misalin zaman mace hudu,kuma feye da haka,amman kuma falone babba sai 5bedroom a cikinsa, sai kichen guda biyu, sai wajan daining table shima babba wanda zai iya cin mutum goma,manyan kujeru ne seatbiyu aka kawata falon dasu,gefe daya kalar black and orage ne,gefi dayan kuma Ash and black,sai dankaririyar plasma wadda ta kusa cinye bango daya manne jikin bango,kumai na falon gwanin ban sha'awa,Azeema ta dauki daki daya,itama Munayya ta dauki daki daya,sai kuma daman Ali akwai dakinshi a gidan saboda nan ne gidanshi.

   Yau kwana biyu da saukarsu amman har yau basu saka Ali a ido ba,tunda ya shigo ya ajiye kayan shi,wani abokinshi yazo ya dauke shi basu kara saka shi a ido ba,ba kuma wai dan ba gidan yake kwana ba,sai dan sanda zai shigo gida sun dade da bacci sanda zai fita basu riga sun tashi ba,duk da yana bala'in takura da hakan,ji yake kamar ya bar masu gidan ya sake gida,saboda yayi imani idan har zasu cigaba da zama inuwa guda babu abinda zai hana basu ga tasku ba.

    Yauma kamar kullum Munnera ta fito daga ita sai wata shigeyar riga jikinta wadda bata da maraba da zindir,wata mahaukaciyar mika tayi sannan ta wurga kanta saman kujera tana fadin "wash Allah na"

     Tashi yayi ya isa inda take ya chakumuta ya mikar da ita tsaye yace"wannan abin da kikeyi mai hakan ke nufi?"

     rukwan da yayi mata yasa gaba daya hankalin ta ya tashi, daman gashi ansha kayan mata saboda tunanin ta idan suka kebi zai nemi wani abu wajanta,bata san gogan naka sai idan kin mashi,ma yake jin yana da sha'awar hada jiki dake,cikin wata irin kissa tace"ne ban ma san kana nan ba,wallahi"

   Wani mugun kallo ya watsa mata yace"ku tantama banayi kin taba karuwanci"

     Tsaki yaja ya wuce abinshi amman yanajin har yanzo kamar nonowanta suna manne da kirjinshi,ku wai gota baiyi ba,saboda tsabar miskilanci da nuna baki isa ba,hawaye taji suna zubu mata wai yaushi zai sakku su fara rayuwa kamar ta mata da miji ne,anya bazata zubda ajinta ba taje ta kara _insisiting_ akan shi ba,lallai idan zata biye mashi bai ke su dauwama cikin wanna halin ba babu ruwanshi.

 Hannuwanshi ya kai bisa bobbs dinta ya dan matsa yace " _and also i miss this stuffs"_

             255



      Kallan mazaunan ta ya karayi yana hango idan ya damke ta wahalar da zata sha tunda yana sani da gayya take juyasu,saboda ya dadi da sanin Janet geridin shakiyyace,ta iya sarrafashi yanda yake so,yake samun ta, amman yau kham idan tazo hannun shi yasan ma zata sha wahala,duk da yasan bata kula kuwane namiji sai shi,amman bai da tabbas din hakan,saboda kasu da yawa na cikin mata basu da gaskiya,kuma indai munafunci kake so,to da yawa mata sun kware a wannan harkar ,shiyasa mace take da hadari.

    Exotic ta d'auku mashi,sannan ta dura saman _plate_ da _cup_ ta tahu kawo mashi,gaba daya nonuwanta sama suke suna kasa,baki ya lashe yana ayyana tanayi ne kawai saboda tayi _atracting_ dinshi, kuma tayin,saboda yakai kululuwar matsuwa da yaji shi a babban birninta,dukawa tayi ta zuba mashi,sai gaba daya nunuwan sukayu waje,da yake _have vest_ ce kuma babban wuya gareta,gaba daya dauriyar shi ta kare,amsar _cup_ din _juice_ din yayi ya aje gefi,sannan ya fizgota ta fada saman shi,ya chakumu mamunanta ya fara _soking_ dinsu yana shafawa,ita kuma ta saka hannuwan ta,cikin gashin kanshi tana yamutsawa wani wahalallan numfashi yake saki cikin sauri sauri,bobbs dinta ya sake ya kuma saman bakinta ya dinga tsotsu,ita kuma ta saka hannunta ta cire mashi botiran gaban rigarshi,lallausan gashin kirjinshi ta fara shafawa,gaba daya ya kacame,baiji bai gani,kunnuwan shi ta hura daga bisani cikin mayaudariyar murya tace _"lest go inside baby"_

    Minti biyar tsakane, sannan ya sarara mata ya tashi suka shiga _bedroom_ nasan akwai aika aika yau da gudu na fito saboda bazan ga wannan shidancin ba dan da alama yau Ali a namijin shi yake,na dawo falo na zauna ina bakin cikin yanda bazan sha kallo ba.






    "Kinsan Allah, wannan Ali dan rainin hankali ne,amman kuma sai in dinga tunanin anya lafiyaryine kuwa,kiga mata har mata cikin gidanshi amman kamar an aji maza jinsinshi,nifa na gaji ina ganin sai na biyu mai ta inda bazai taba tsammani ba,duk wayan shi sai na testa shi"

     "Ai naga alamar haka wallahi"

    "Ke nifa naga kasadarki da kika yadda kika fada gidan ukuba,gidan azaba, dan ku a club idan ya shigo kuwace mace ji take kamar ta suma, amman kuma babu damar kuda kallan shi kiyi,da kun hada ido dashi ma wani mugun kallon da zaki samu sai kin raina kanki,shiyasa duk maita ta da Ali ban taba gigin bin hanyar da ya biyu ba ma"

    "Ke ai naga kaskanci yau da safe, kinga yanda ya hankadani,ya kuma buge mani kumata da wani azababban mari,wai daga na shafa cikinshi da na hangi wani gashi kwanci na kasa hakuri"

    "Kawata akwai jarabar tsiya,ke daman baki da hakuri"

     "Kinga laifina kuwa,yanzo dai ya keki ganin ya kamata inyi dan in samu kudan awa biyu yayi man"

     "Kawai ki zuba mashi magani a lemo yasha kiyi _reaping_ dinshi kawai"

     "Tab, baki da hankali slimy idan ya dawo hankalin shi fa?ya fahimci abinda na aikata ya zakiga zamu kare,ai kashina ma ya bushi"

     "Ai daman kwadan shi kike yi kawai tunda kudinshi dai yaki sakar maku aci ba sai ki kara gaba ba"

     "Baki da hankali,ai yanzo bana iya rayuwa sai da Ali"

     "To kawai sai kiyi hakuri da yanda zaki samishi,har zuwa sanda zai sauku,sai kuma kin gyara hali"

     "Zan dai ga yanda za'ayi kawai _i have to think big"_



    Kuka na dinga jeyu wa daga can dakin da Janet da Ali suka shiga,tagume nayi ina tunanin anya lafiya,kuka naji yana kara sauti ana mayi kamar wani abu take so,da sauri na tashi naji bakin kofar nadan leka ta abin kofan na hanyi Janet gurfani gaban Ali ta kama kafafuwan shi tana 
magiya wanda bansan mai ya hadasu ba,zauni Ali yake ya dafe kai zufa kawai yake yana fidda numfashi dakyar ya iya furta _"please Janet i can do this"_

     _"Please Baby_
 bansan yazan yi ba idan ka barni,mutuwa zanyi na shirya kumai dominka _i prepare things elot only for you"_

    "Ai bazai san muna tare ba baby"

 shafarshi ta farayi tana kukarin saka hannunta ta cire mashi belt din wando,da sauri ya rike hannunta yace _"you have to understand what am try to tell you_,nayi mashi alkawarin na daina kuma idan na saba kinga a addinina Allah zai kamane da laifi saboda haka _please sorry"_

    Ihu ta fasa ta fara kai mashi doka saboda tama manta dawa take tare,ta manta zai iya bata kashin tsiya,saboda kishi idanuwanta sun rufe.

      Duka take kai mashi babu kakkau tawa,duk ta rude ta fita hayyacin ta,barinta yayi ta gama duk iya abida zatayi,kuka take kamar na fitar rayuwa,ganin abun nata ba na kare bane yasa ya ri'ke hannuwanta sannan ya zaunar da ita akan kojera sannan yace mata _"baby look at me"_

     Kauda kai tayi,saboda ji take kamar ta kashe kanta ta huta takeji a zuciyarta,kamar an zabure ta haka ta damkeshi tace"please Aliyu kaci da wasa kake mani,wallahi zan iya mutuwa saboda rashinka,bazan iya hadaka da kuwacce mace ba,ni kadai zan mallake ka"

    Zauna wa yayi saman kujera,sannan ya daukuta gaba daya ya durata saman kafafunshi,ya roke fuskarta yace" _come known baby Janet_,aurena ba yana nufin _the friendship is over_ ba"

    Idanuwa ta fiddo tace" _friendship_ kamar ya?"

    "Kamar yanda kika sani,na sani cewa bazan aureki ba sai dai mun dan kasanci na wani lokaci,kinsan daman nasha fada maki bazan aure kiba, kuma _anytime we have to breakup"_

    Tunawa tayi da duk lokacin da yaga tana zurfafa kishinta a kanshi,sai ya fada mata cewa zai iya rabuwa da ita daga yanzo zuwa kuwane lokaci,jikinta ne yayi sanyi a hankali ta kwanta saman kirjinshi tace"yanzo dai kada ka bari matanka su rabani da kai plaese"

    _"I know,_to yanzo dai ya zakayi dani saboda gaba daya ka kaini sama"

    Baida wata mafita sai na yanda zaiyi ya dan ragi mata zafi,saboda yasan shine yake da kasu mafi tsoka na lalacewar Janet, farkon haduwarsu _she is vagin_ shi ya anshi _vaginity_ dinta,shi yasa yake _respecting_ dinta _the most_,kuma idan zai shikara baya nan bazata taba ba ma wani namiji kanta ba, har sai ya dawo,ya mutsata ya cigaba dayi ya samu ya dan lallabata ta hanyar yi mata _fingaring_ ya bar mata gidan,da sharadin gobe zai zo ya _speanding_ duk yinin a wajanta ta shirya mashi abincin da tasan yana so,har ya tafi ya dawo ya zagaye hannuwan shi bisa kugunta ya rada mata a kunne yace"ban baki damar shan kumai ba,kuyin wani abu da zai tada maki da sha'awa dan akwai yewuwar bazan kara _having sex_ dake ba kinji?"

    Cikin sanyin jiki tace"mai yasa?"

    _"It is promise between me and my father,_na daina _having sex with any girl accept my wifes,so you have to avoid_ kumai da zai sa ki ji kina so"

    Kai ta gyada mashi tace _"i will"_

    Dan kumatun ta ya kaima _kiss_ yayi murnushi yace _"i have to take a leave,always love you my first love"_

    Wadan nan kalaman sun taka muhimmiyar rawa wajan kwantar mata da hankali,har da fituwa ta raka shi wajan motar shi,a cikin motar ma sun dadi suna musayar kalamai na tsantsar soyayya da kaunar juna ita dashi,karshe tace sai ta sha bakin shi na tsawon minti biyu,kamar yanda suka saba yin hakan a matsayin sallamar karshe.

     Baida wani zabi sai na biye mata suyi hakan,saboda shine babban wanda ya lalata masu yarinya, duk wani abu da take shiya kuyar da ita yanda yake san ta kasanci,sun dauki tsawan shikara takwas suna tare,tun bayan da ya tabbatar shine namiji na farko da ya farkata sai ya dauke nauyinta akan kumai na rayuwa,ya kuma killacita saboda ita din tashi ce.

   Dakyar ya yakice ta, daboda ba tasan halin da yake ciki ba,ji yake kamar ya kwalla kara,saboda cikin shi da yake kukarin fashiwa,hanashi tafiya tayi ta cigaba da bada kasu mafi tsoka wajan kara ruruwar wutar sha'awar shi,sai da taga bacin rai a fuskarshi sannan ta,barshi ya wuce _a ziciyata nake ayyana yanda wata mace take ma Ali sangarta san ranta amman baya iya yi mata jan ido,to me hakan ke nufi?tana da babban matsayi a zuciyarshi kinan,Janet you are taking spacial part a zuciyar Ali zaki._
 
   Dakyar yake murza sityaring motan da hannu daya,hannu daya kuma yana dafi da cikin shi,horn yayi maigadi ya bude mashi gate,yayi _parkin,g_ bai ma idasa rufe marfin motar ba ya wuce cikin gida yana dafe da cikin shi,da bin bango ya samu ya idasa cikin falon,saman kujera ya fada yana dafe da cikin shi yana fidda wani irin nishin azaba.

     Azeema da fituwarta kinan daga dakinta da ta gaji da kwanciya,tafito tadan kashi kwarkwatar ido ta hanyar yin kallo,gari yadan fara duhu tunda wajan karfi 7:00pm na yamma ne,kuma glob din falon ba irin maihasken nan bane, saboda _nature_ din Ali ne bai san haskin lantarki sosai,shi yasa yake amfani da mai colour saboda ragewar yawaitar haske,sai da ta iso tsakiyar falon sosai sannan ta hange shi saman kujera kwanci yana dafe da ciki,idanuwa ta tsira mashi tana kallanshi,azaba ta isheshi da kyar ya tashi yana niyyar tafiya _bedroom_ dinshi,ai kuwa sai gaba daya ya kara kumawa saman kujerar ya zube dakyar ya iya furta "wayyo Allah na marata"

    Da gudu Azeema ta kara sa inda yake ta rike hannunshi tace"Ya Ali lafiya?"

    Hannunta ya kamu ya dura saman marar shi yace"marata zata fashi"

    Kuka ta saka tace"a kira doctor mana yaya,ina wayan in kirashi"

     "Ina da magani a daki _take me to my bedroom please"_

  Kama hannuwanta yayi da karfi suka dinga bin bango, har suka samu suka shiga  dakin shi,gaba daya nauyin shi ya sakar mata numfashin ta har daukewa yake saboda nauyin da Ali yake dashi bana wasa bane,da taimakon Allah suka isa bakin gadon shi ya fada ya kwanta rub da ciki ya cigaba da juye juye yana dafe da ciki.

   "Ya Ali ina maganin yake?"

    Bedsite duruwan gado ya nuna mata,da sauri ta bude taga maganin ta dauku sannan ta kuma wajan friage din da tagani a dakin ta tsiyaya ruwa a cup ta kawo mashi tace"gashi kasha maganin"

     Dakyar ya dan daskwana ya tashi ta mika mashi maganin ya amsa yasha,zama tayi kusa dashi yana ta juye juye cikin mintin da baifi ukku ba bacci ya kwashe shi amman da kaga yanayin shi zaka san baccin bamai dadi bane,kwanciya ta gyara mashi ta saka blnket ta rufe shi sannan ta kunna mashi AC ta tafi dakinta.

    Bakin gado ta samu ta zauna tana tunanin yanda jikinta ya hadu da na abinda yafi suyuwar mata a rayuwa,dariya ta saka tace _"i love you soo much Ya Ali"_

  Wanka ta fada ta wanke duk jikinta tayi brush ta fito gaban madubi ta tsaya ta shafa mayukanta masu kanshi,sannan ta saka rigar baccin ta mai kyau,mara nauyi,ta nemi gadonta ta haye har bacci ya fara kwasarta sai ta tuna ya kamata taje ta duba Ali taga wani hali yake ciki,dakin shi ta nufa tun a bkin kofa take jeyu nishin shi da sauri ta bude ta shiga, ta isa inda yake ta kamashi tace"ya Ali ka bari in kira doctor mana kada kaje wani abun ya samika"

    Bebiyu yake ganinta,sama sama yake jin maganarta amman bai iya fadin abinda take cewa,surarta ta dinga wanzuwa a saman idanuwan shi zindir yake kallan kumai na jikinta,wata muguwar fizga taji am mata ihu ta saka tana,sarrafata Ali ya farayi cikin sauri kamar bajemin zaki,kuka ta farayi saboda bata gane inda ya dusa ba,batai aune ba sai taji bakinta saman nashi ya fara tsotsarta bakinta yake tsosta kamar ya samu alawa,numfashi yake fiddawa dakyar,da kyar ya ya raba  bakinta ya dawo saman mamunanta wata damka da yayi masu sai da ta saki kara,shafarsu ya fara yana lagudawa,bata ankara ba sai taji bakinshi saman su,jikinta gaba daya ya fara rawa,daman Momcy ta gyara diyarta da haddun magungunan mata,bata san sanda ta fara maida mashi martani ba,bakin shi ta lalubu itama ta fara sha,daman Ali gwanin shan baki ai sai suka fara wani salon kiss,kayan jikinta gaba daya ya cire,da taimakonta ya cire kayan jikinshi,kasanta ya kai hannu yaji danshi,nan ya kara kaime wajan sarrafata ,da gudu na fito ina tausaya ma Azeema da wannan zakewa da take,palo na nemi waje na zauna,da niyyar zan shiga idan ya gama  kashe boss din in  taimaka mata ta gyara jikinta,dan nasan babu mai kwatarta yau a wajan Ali har sai ya fanshi wahalar da yake sha na tsawan kwanakin da ya dauka bai shige kuwacce mace ba,gashi kuma shida Janet,yanda ta kaya dasu.

    Ihu na ji anayi da sauri na isa bakin kofar na tura naji gam da key,Munayya wadda tashin ta kinan daga baccin asara taji ihu na tashi sama sama,amman bata kawo kumai a ranta ba, ta kara mirginawa ta kuma ta kwanta,jin ihun take kamar a cikin gidan a keyin shi, da sauri ta tashi ta fito falo,sai taji ihun kamar a dakin Ali yake fetuwa,kuma ihu na mace,bakin kofar dakin ta isa tadan taba taji kofan rufe,dakin Azeema ta nufa ta shiga ta iske wayam,babu ita babu alamarta, kiranta ta dinga yi ku zataji ta amsa atoilet amman taji shiru,rudewa tayi ta fito waje kaddai ace Ali jima'i yake da Azeema da kuwa ta bani ta lalace,kamar an tsikarita ta nufi bakin kofar dakin ta kara murzawa taji datse da makulle,kuma ana ta kuka kunne ta kasa taji ku zata iya jin abinda ke faruwa,ji tayi Azeema tana cewa"Ya Ali dan Allah ka barni haka wallahi mutuwa zanyi,na gaji ka dagani,wayyo zafi, wayyo zan mutu, wayyo Yaya Ommar,wayyo Momcyna,babu wanda bata kira sunan shi ba, wanda ta sani amman bata daina kukan ba.

   Kuka Munayya ta saka tana ma Azeema Allah ya isa da ita Ali ya fara sani kafin ita, da ta shirya daukar maganar slimy na saka mashi magani a lemo yau saboda tayi _reaping_ dinshi amman gashi can da wata suna hulewa,hannu ta dura akai ta dinga kuka tana sharce majina,kamar wadda aka cema uwata ta mutu.



  _wai mace ki tunanin reaping din namiji kai duniya ina zaki damu?_

      Bayan sun gama cin abinci sai Aminatu ta kama hanun Deji tace"zu mutafi dakina musa labule Aunty Deji"

   Deji tayi dariya tace "Haba Aminatu ni banci Aunty ba sai ki zaki ceman Aunty" 

     "ai ki matar yayana ce,dole inci maki Aunty" suna tafiya suna magani. 

    Bayan sun isa dakin da Aminatu ta sauka a matsayin dakina, ta kama hannun Deji suka zauna bakin gado sannan ta kama hannunta tace"Aunty Deji kina son yaya Ali?"

     Deji tace"bafa na san shi, aradu Aminatu"
 
    "idan baki sanshi, mai yasa kike San kallan photon shi?"

   "Kawai yana burgeni saboda yana kama da turawa sosai kuma yana da kyau"

    "To mai yasa kike kasa bacci har sai kin kalla photon shi"

    "To nima ban sani ba,kuma kinsan tsoranshi nake ji, dana ganshi sai inji gabana yana faduwa, ku idan na tunashi"

  "Sai kuma me kikeji game dashi? "

   "su kenan na kuma San duk inajin hakan saboda yana da mugunta sosai, nifa kinsan daya dawo zan gudu gida Allah"

   " kina San baffanki yayi fushi dake kinan? "

   " bani da wani zabi sai na guduwar saboda bafa a duka na a gida,amman da nazo nan bansan iyakar marin da yayi mani ba"

    "to yanzo duk ba wanna ba,bani hankali ki nan wajan in baki labarin abinda yake damunki" 

     "to maiki damuna inba zullumi ba,kinga har nayi kiba da baya nan" 

     "kinsan mai ki danunki ciwan so ke dawainiya dake,wallahi kuma kina son yaya Ali, daki yarda da kada ki yarda hakan take"

      "kina da aure kina fadin sunan wani a waje?" 

    "ai na manta kuma fa,malamin mu na makarantar arabi yace babu kyau, Allah na tuba"

    " jinan Deji idan kin yadda kina son yaya Ali"

    "idan inason shi yaya kike san inyi, tunda ya tsaneni kuma ince gaba dayanmu ya tsanemu" 

    Dayan hannunta ta kama tace "idan kin yadda zan taimaka maki ki samu soyayyar shi"

    " a'a bani so wallah, bazan sa kaina a wahala ba"

    "to shikinan yanzo dai ya kamata ki waye kafin ya dawo yaga kin tashi daga Deji kin kuma Khadija beautiful" 

    Dariya sukayi gaba dayansu, sannan Aminatu tace"anjima zamu fita inyu maki sayarya kuma a gyara maki wannan gashin nake mai tsawan tsiya"

    "to da wani kudin, nidai bani da kudi wallahi" 

    "kina matar young millonia kice baki da kudi?" 

    "ai kudin shi ne ba nawa ba" 

    "kudin miji ai na mata ne, ki bari kawai lokaci zaiyi halinsa, sai yanda kikayi da yaya Ali"




    LONDON

      zazzabi mai zafi ya rufe ta,gaba daya ta rasa inda zata saka kanta, da kyar take tafiya saboda yanda taji jiki a daran jiya a hannun Ali kamar baisan cewa ita budurwa ce wadda bata san da namiji ba, amman babu tausayi a tattare dashi,gashi babu wani maganin data sha wanda zata samu sauki, bata da wani zabi saina neman shi ta fada mashi tana bukatar magani kuda paracetamol ne.da kyar ta dinga bin bango har ta isa dakin da take tunanin nan ne nashi,da sallama ta tura kofar ta shiga kwanci ta iske shi da waya a hannunshi yana magana,da ganin yanayin wayar kasan da mace yakeyin wayar, gefi ta tsaya tana sauraran yanda yake kashi murya yana kwantar da kai yana kalaman kauna, shida Janet ne saboda ta uzura mashi da tambayoyin yana San ya rabu da ita ne, shine yake kwantar mata da hankali da alkawarin zai zo zuwa anjima yaji matsalarta. 

    Ido ya tsora mata da alamar tambaya, akan dalilin da yasa ta shigo mashi daki. 

    "Bani da lafiya, ka taimaka ka siya mani magani"

    kamar bazai amsa ba yace"maike damunki"

     Tambayar rainin wayau,kwalla ta sharce tace"zazzabi"

     "akwai first aid box a falo ki dauka magani kisha" 

   Juyawa tayi ta fara bin bango,Ido ya tsora mata tausayi ta bashi a karo na farko da ya tuna daran jiya yanda ya sameta a cikakkiyar macen mutunci yace "wait"

   Daga shi sai boxes da singlet ya iso ya sungumeta kamar 'yar baby suka fito falo,dai dai lokaci Munnera itama ta fito daga daki.

     Idanuwa ta tsora masu tanajin kamar zata mutu a zuciyarta take tunanin _mai hakan ke nufi?ya fara Santa kenan._hawaye ya zubu mata ta kuma dikinta,ita fada saman gado ta sake kuka mai sauti.

     Saman kujera ya a jeye ta sannan ya kuma ya dauku mata magani ya zauna kusa da ita,ya balla mata magani ya bata tasha,sanan yace mata"kince bakida lafiya to mai ya same ki baki da lafiya?"

     Kai ta kauda sannan tace" kawai tashi nayi da zazzabi"

    Baki ya taba yace"matsalar auran yara kinan kuya ka tabasu sai suce ka cutar da su,kida ba wani abu akai maki ba,na azu a gani ba,shiyasa nasu in aure babbar mace,ba kwaila ba"

   Tashi yayi yana mita abinda kawai taji a maganarshi _bai masan tsautsan da ya kaishi aikata hakan ba_hawaye ya zubu mata,ya biya ma kanshi bukata amman babu wata  kulawa.

    Dakin shi ya shiga ya cire kayan jikinshi ya fada toilet domin yin wanka,ya tafi wajan janet.

    A tsanaki ya dinga shirinshi har ya gama, yayi kyau sosai na tada ma yanmata akwatin hankali,keyn motanshi ya dauku ya fito falo, can ya hangota kudundune saman kujera har ya kauda kanshi zai tafiyarshi sai ya tuna kuba darajar aure kanwar babban abokinshi ce, dan tsaki yayi cikin isa ya iso ya tsaya saman kanta yace"ba kinci bakida lafiya ba"

  A hankali ta gyada kai.

  Tsaki ya karayi sannan yace"na tsani mutumin da zan ma magana bazai amsa Mani ba,saboda haka ki keyayi kuda gaba"

   Kamar bata so tace"kayi hakuri"

  Hanyarshi ya kama zai tafi sai ya tsinci maganar Munayya wadda fitowarta kinan daga dakinta"my man ina zaka ka dauki wannan wankan haka"

  Ranshi a matukar baci ya fuskancita yace"yaushe kika fara saka Mani ido a abinda ya shafi rayuwata"

   Fari tayi da ido sannan ta gyara yanayin tsayuwarta duk a salanta nawai taja hankalinshi race"banga aibu ba dan mata ta tambayi al'amuran mijinta,ina ganin hakan bazai zama matsala ba ko?"

   Nuna ta yayi da dan yatsa yace"babban abinda ya cuce rayuwarki rashin zuciya da kin San daukar gaskiya,kada tunaninki ya kawo cewa duk wani sanabinki bazai taba tasiri akaina ba,ya kamata ki fahimci na tsaneki _the most_,kuma ki fita harkata tun kafin in kara tsanarki a rayuwata"

    Hannu ta dura akai ta kwarma wani uban ihu"amman mai na maka haka ka tsaneni haka?"

   "Sakara kawai, uban wa kike tambaya,ledar pure water"ya tsalake yayi tafiyarshi yana tafiyar takama.

    Da kyar Azeema ta iya tashi tana tafiya kamar bata su,tazo dai dai saitin Munayya tace" Allah wadaran naka ya lalace"

    Kallo Munayya ta bita dashi saboda Ali ya kashi bakin magana gaba daya,ji take kamar ta mutu saboda wulakancin da yayi mata,mai yake nufi da kazar kuwa?lallai zata kuya mashi hankali.

   Horn yayi maigadin gidan su Janet ya wangame mashi ya shiga,parking yayi sannan ya haura saman tun kafin yayi mata norking tasan da zuwanshi ta bude,runguma ta kai mashi da dabara ya kwace ya kama hannunta suka shiga ciki

           *280*



      A gida Nageria kuwa zaman Munnera da Aminatu ya canza salo saboda babu mai raga ma wani cikinsu,dan ma Aminatun ita ma fitinar nar kanta ce ta iya da ita da sun kwashi kashinsu a hannu ita da Deji,yau ma kamar kullum Munnera ta fito tayi sashin yan aiki ta kwala ma Audi mai wanki kira da kyar ya fito saboda rashin lafiyar dake damunshi,kwana biyu da suka wuce.

     Kullen kaya ta cilla mashi tace"ka tabbatar ka wanki su da kyau,kuma bana san wannan gogar wadda bata guguwa idan basuyi ba kuma zaka kara mai maita wa, kuma idan ka gama wankin ka kirani in gani idan basu fita ba ka kara wanki wa tunda ai biyanka ake ba kyauta kake ba,kuma sau biyar zaka saba su"
  
     "Hajiya dan Allah kiyi hakure zuwa gobe sai in wanki bani lafiya sosai ban ma iya wanken"

     "ai kuwa baka isa ba,in dai ba albashin wannan watan baka dashi ba"

     "maza dalla malam kaje ka fara abinda ya kawo ka tun ban dai-dai ta maka kwakwalwa ba,ba kauyan banza kawai dulu"

      Peter ne ya fito saboda duk abinda yake faruwa yana jinsu,yace"Aunty kiyi hakure wallahi jiya baiyi bacci ba cikinshi ke cewo ga zazzabi da amai da yayi tayi"
 
      "ba matsala ta bace, kuma ma ban haneka shiga harka ta ba,ubn wa ya sanya bakin ka a cikin magana ta?"

   "sorry ma"

   "sorry ma din ubanka,bace man da gani shege kare"

      Ranshi bace ya tashi daga durkusun da yayi yana tunanin _wace irin nace oganshi ya auro mara imani mafadaciya tijararrah haka wadda bata san darajar dan adam ba_

       "kai kuma yau ku mutuwa zakayi sai ka wanki kayan nan wallahi in ba haka ba kuma a kuma kauye a cigaba da cin tuwan dawa miyar kuka"

      Cikin dauriya ya fara wanken tun yana iya ganin abinda yake har ya fara ganin duhu duhu daga bisani kuma ya fadi a sume,da gudu peter yayi kanshi ya jijjega shi amman bai mutsa ba ya shafa mashi ruwa sannan ya farfadu,ya kamashi da kyar ya iya kaishi  daki ya kwantar dashi ya dawo da niyyar karasa mashi wankin,sai gata ta fito kawo nashi wani dankwali da ta manta dashi,saukar mare peter yaji a kuncin shi sannan tace"uban wa ya baka damar yin aikin da ba naka ba,maza aji ka kama gabanka dan akuya"

      Daga saman beni Aminatu ta leko ta taga domin tashin ta daga bacci kinan tana hangin farfajiyar gidan ta hango sanda Munnera ta ke kukarin kara kaima peter mari,amman ya goce,da sauri ta sanya hijabi da zane ta sauku kasa ta nufi sashin su,tun kafin ta isa take karu da bama baman da munnera take dura ma peter na rashin mutunci

      "to diyar maguzawa irin jaraba uban mai kuma yau ma yayi maki da kike dura mashi wadannan zagin haka kamar kin haifeshi"

        Kankanci ido munnera tayi saboda yau Aminatu ta sameta a dai dai yanda zata mata rashin arziki san rai"ubanki yayi mani shegeya wadda tayi kwantai ta kasa auruwa sai kankanba da girman kai"

    Wani mahaukacin mari taji ya sauka a kuncinta daga bisani kuma taga jini nabin hancinta,sannan Aminatu ta nunata da hannu tace"kul dinki da neman gaya man maagana dan zan iya sawa a sauki man kanki ba wata tsiya bace,matsiyaciya tinkiya"

      Ta kuma sai tin da peter yake tace"mai ke faruwa?"

    Peter ya gaya mata kumai da ya faru,sannan ta maida dubanta ga Munnera tace"daga yau shima ya daina wanken da gogar tunda ba wannan tsohun dan daudun ya kawo shiba,kuma duk wanda kika kara zage zai rama, in bugunsu kikayi zasu rama,wannan dole ne saboda haka ki keyayi gaba saboda bacin rana idan kin ji ki tsira da mutuncin ki idan kuma ba haka ba kumai zai iya faruwa hadda wanda ma baki tunani"

    "Ina Audin yake?"

    "yana ciki small hajiya"

   "ku shirya driver ya kai ku asibity yaga likita,kazo ka karbi kudi in an rubuta magani sai ku tsaya ku saya"

       Rissinawa ya karayi yace"mun gode small hajiya,Allah ya saka maki da alkairi"

    Kallan munnera yayi wadda take dafe da kunci yayi dan murmushi daga gani na rainin hankali ne,wanda hakan na nufin raini ya fara shiga kinan tsakaninsu,wani mugun kallo ta wurga mashi sannan ta ja kafafuwanta ta nufi hanyar shiga cikin gidan gaba daya duk masifarta tayi lakwaf saboda gaba da gabanta wai aljani yataka wuta.

       Bayan ta shiga sukayi arba da Deji wadda take kukarin shiga kicin,da sauri ta nufi wajanta tace jini na zuba a hancinki,mai ya sameki?faduwa ki..... Sai taji gilmawar hannu daga bisani kuma taji kamar kunama ta cijeta a kumatu,wata kara ta sake,mai karfi tana tsalle tana kunama ta cije ta a kumatu,hankade ta munnera tayi sannan ta haura sama,dai dai lokacin Aminatu da peter suka shigo da gudu saboda sunji karar kukan Deji,a kasa suka samita dafe da gun da munnera ta kwada mata mahaukacin marin da tayi tunanin cewa kunama ta cije ta,da dauri Aminatu ta daga ta tace"kunama a gidan nan,ina ta cije ki?

    Kumatun ta Deji ta nuna sawun hannun kwanci a kyarkyawar fuskar Deji wadda ta fara jin gyara da kalolin mayuka,ran Aminatu ya baci a ranta tace _watau shine tazo ta rama a kanta kinan lallaizamu Kwashi yan kallo da wannan matar bata san hali na bane zan kuya mata hankali_

   *LONDON, DUNIYAR ALI DA MATANSHI.*


      Waje ta fito tana neman wanda zai nuna mata inda zata samu chemist.

      "kin iya hausa,na dauka ma bakya jin hausar"

      "yazan iya kasa fadin yarena"

       "oh kina nufin kima bahausa ce kamar ne?"

      "kina mamaki ne?"

       "sosai ma kuwa,idan nai la'akari da yanayin shigarki ku dankwali babu kanki"

       Kaita kauda cikin kusawa tace"idan akwai taimakun da zan iya maki 'ke fada ina da abin yene?

      "ye hakuri dan Allah, wai chemist nake nema,ina da bukatar magani"

       "sai kinji zaki gane"

      Friage ta bude taga sauran kwalin hollandia din biyu,daya saura rabin kwalin,daya kuma ba'ayi amfani dashi ba,godiya taima Allah sannan ta nufi dakinta ta dauku maganinta wanda ta sawo a chemist,na kara karfin sha'awa (Bega) ta sanya guda biyu a ciki ta maida a cikin frage din ta rufe,sannan ta nufi dakinta ta fada saman gado tunda idan Allah ya taimaki ta yasha tasan yau kanta zai nufu,addu'a ta farayi na Allah ya taimake ta,dan in ba haka ba bata san inda zata nemu wanda zai debe mataa kewa ba.

      Katan feli ne da ya sha flawowe,kida ke tashi mai dadi da kwantar da hankalin masoya,babu kojera ku daya a wajan, samari da 'yan matan duk tsaye suke,Janet tana ta hangen ta inda Ali zai bullu amman har ya kara wajan awa guda a saman yanda aka tsara za'ayi partyn na bathday din kawarta,da motarshi ya shigo wajan,bayan yayi parking ya dadi bai fito ba,minti biyar tsakani sai gashi ya fito,kallo gaba daya ya kuma kanshi musamman yanda ya hadu ga wani kyau na musamman da yake badawa,fuska ya yamutsa sannan ya taku kamar wani zaki dakakken namiji mai aji da wayewa cikin isa yake tafiya,wata class mate dinshi ta tareshi suka gaisa sannan tace" _you look so muahh"_

     _"tank you"_

  _"and you look so sweet,please can i hug you?"_

    _"please Savitri,idon't like all this"_

    "ne da kike gani nafi ubanki matsayi da daraja,shi uban nake wanene shi,banza sakarai,zan iya ballaki idan kika shiga rayuwa ta"

    Gaba yayi zai tafi sannan kuma ya dawo kusa da ita yace"bari ma kije idan duk duniyar nan danginki ne babu wanda ya isa yasa in soki,mata masu aji ake so ba karya irinki ba,saboda haka kada in kara ganin mai halitta irin taki a duk inda kika san ina bayyana,in kuma ba haka ba rai zai baci"sannan yasa kafarshi ya tadeyita ta fadi kasa.

      Tafiyarshi yake cikin isa babu kuma wata damuwa saman fuskarshi,can wajan wani dan block ya samu ya zauna sannan ya dan hanga ku zai hangeJanet can ya hangota ita ma ta harde hannuwa akirji tana kallanshi da tuhuma,fuskarta kamar anyi gobara.

      Baki ya taba,sannan ya kauda kai kamar ma bai ganta ba ya dauku waya yayi dailing number,daga daya bangaran aka amsa da.

     "shegen kasa,kaci ganye sai kaga dama kake kirana ko?

     "kana da matsala,kai kullum kana kurafi ban faye kiranka ba,kai an fara kwamuwa a skul ne fa"
    
     "To kana ina yanzo haka?"

     "ina inda ka aiki ne"

     "banza katan wofi an girma ba'asan an girma ba,dan kaga baka aje yara ba shiyasa kake ganin kanka har yanzo a saurayi kake"

    "wai kai har yanzo babu mai ciki cikin matanka,ku ba ma wadda kake kulawa sai wannan arniyar?"

    "duk nai masu ciki mana,da mun dawo zaka ga suna janshi sun kusa haihuwa ma,kuma bari kawai a dawo maka da yaran,nawa kake su?

     Dariya Ommar ya saka yace"wallahi baka da mutunci?ka cika raina man hankali"

     "banza, daman wani labari zan baka aman tunda ka bata man rai na fasa,sai kuma na huce"

     ket ya kashi, dan yasan halinshi idan ya tasu shi da mageya zai iya fada mashi,yama kashe wayan gaba daya.

      An fara gabatar da bathday patyn,mata da samari sun fara chashe wa,Ali yana gefi yana kallan abinda ke gudana bai kara be takan Janet ba haka ita ma mulkin nataya mutsa a zuwan har sai yaje inda take sannan,bata kuma san da cewa Ali sai dai ta mutu da jiranta ma bazai kalle inda take ba,tabarshi ya fiddo ya dauku lighter ya banka mata wuta ya fara zuka cikin kwarewa da iya duniyanci,sanin waye Ali yasa da yawan mata duk yanda ya kaiga burgesu suna tsoran zuwa wajanshi,sai dai kallo ta wutsiyar ido,in kika cika kallanshi nan ma baki tsira ba,silent bullet kinan.

     Aunty yasmen ta gama danwake ta bama,Yazeed ya kai ma Auntyn gidan uncle Ali,bayan ya shiga yayi sallama sai ya iske Munayya tana zaune a falo har kasa ya durkusa ya gaidata yace "gashi inji Ummata tace akawo"

    "wacece umman taka, kuma ina ne gidanku da har zaka kawo mana abinci"

     "Uncle Ali yaje yace ayi a kawo ma Aunty tana so"

    _Anan ta ganu rainin wayan da ake mata har ya kai haka,da zatace tana san abu anfi karfin a bada umarni mai aikin gidan ya dafa sai dai yaje wani gida yace a dafa a kawo ma yar iskar yarinyar da ta shiga gabanta_

    "babu damuwa maza gida"

     jetai cikinta na yamutsawa kirjinta ma fat fat saboda tashin hankali dole kuwa tasan duk yanda zatayi ta amayushi dan inba haka ba yau sunanta gawa a gidan nan"

   daukuta yayi kamar yar baby ya nufu falo da ita baiga Munayya a wajan ba yasan ta nufi daki ta boye,naman ya tura mata yace _"start with this"_

     "saboda daman kin gajeyunwa da kwadai ku?"

       *294*



     Gaba dayansu suka sunkuyar da kai kasa,saboda sun san gamansu da ita idan har suka sake suka hada ido da ita, akwai matsala babba a garesu dan sai sun gwammaci kida da karatu.

    "sanin babu mai kwaba maku shiyasa kuka maida mana gida kamar gidan yan gada,har'ana jeyo shiwarku ku?saboda haka yau yine zanyi a gidan nan ,kuma ku tabbatar kunyi abinda na saka ku,in ba haka ba zakuyi a bakin aikinku daman ya kamata ace kunbar aiki a gidan nan zuwa yanzo"

     Sun kwana da sanin kadan daga cikin aikinta ne,zata iya abinda yafi haka ma,saboda bata hada hanya da mutunci ba yayi gabas tayi amma.

     Ciki ta shiga bayan ta kara jaddada masu abinda zasuyi,kawai dan ta kunta ta masu, bawai dan abinda ta fada gaskiya bane na gidan yana wari.

    A hakimce ta iske mahaifiyarta cikin shagwaba ta isa wajanta ta fada mata ta saka kuka,hankalin Momy tashi take tambayarta.

    "ki! lafiya kika shigo kamar an maki mutuwa?"

     Kara rushewa tayi da kuka,lallashi Momy ta fara sannan tace maike faruwa ne wai,Alin yace ya sake ki ku wane aka ce make ya mutu?"

     Kukan ta dan tsagaita sannan tace"Momy bazan iya zama wannan gidan ba,bai da mutunci, baya kaunata,kuma baya da wadda ya tsana a duk cikinmu sai ni,dukana yake kamar jakka,kwana biyu da ya wuce na tashi daga jinyar dukan da yayi man,dakyar masu gadi da yan aiki suka amsan a hannunshi"

     A hargetse Momy take sauraran maganganun da 'yarta ta take shirya mata na karya,hankalin ta ya gama tashi da duk bayanin da yarinyarta ta tayi mata"bazan yadda ba wallahi aje a kashe man ke,wallahi wannan ai aikin banza ne aikin wofi,bari Dadyn naki ya dawo, gaskiya bazaki kuma ba,idan ya kiya kuwa sai dai aye wacce za'ayi,dan ba jaka muka aura mashi ba"

     "Momy wallahi kada ki bari ace zan kuma gidan nan,dan sai dai a kawo maku gawata dan azzalumi ne"

     Hannun momy ta kama tace _"momy please save my life"_

     "kada ki damu,zan san yanda zanyi"

      Kara rukunkume momy tayi tace _"Tnx you soo much"_

    Key din motan Momy ta amsa,tace _mata zata dan shiga tayi shopping abubuwan da zatayi amfani dasu,tafiya tayi wajan ajiyar motoci,bayan ta gama jaddada ma 'yan aikin cewa kada ta dawo ta iske basu gama ba,_horn tayi ma maigadi,da gudu ya wangale mata gate ta fito,wani maifacen keke dake can karshan katangar gidansu taima horn da sauri ya iso yace"Hajiya gane, mai keke so?"

     " shiga muje"

     Mamaki ya tsaya yana yi tace"idan kagama wangale bakin kana iya shiga muje sakarai kazami kawai"

     Babu kuzari ya shiga motar,saboda duk ya raina kanshi,a ranshi kuwa yake ayyana anya akwai alkairi a wannan gayyata da tai mashi,sai ya tsinci kanshi wajan tambayarta inda zasu nufa.

      Cikin halin ko in kula tace"sai da ka zanyi in amsu kudin"

     "ina da iyali, kuma suna bukatar taimaku na,kada kisalwantar da rayuwata dan Allah"

      Dariya ta danyi a karu na farko tana Allah wadai da rashin ilimi a rayuwar wasu mutane,baki ta taba tace"ka taba makaranta kuwa?"

     "ban taba ba hajiya"

    "shiyasa kake aiki irin na jahilan farko,ya kake tunanin zan iya sai da wannan banzan kan naka inci na saida me?"

     bayanin abinda take so take mashi,sannan tace"yaje yakira mata daya daga cikinsu,zata jirashi a nan wajan data rigata tsaya"Hajiya mai ya hada ke da wadannan yaran, dan Allah ki fidda kanki daga cikin hatsarin yaran da suka addabi alumma da yawa"

    "zakayi abinda na saka ku zaka tsaya raina man hankali,ubana! tunda ni bansan dai dai ba ai gwara ka kuya man"

     "yi hauri ba nufina kinan ba"

    "to maza ka aiwatar da abinda na umurceka yanzo,kuma katabbatar kun tahu tare"

    tafiya yake yana tunanin jarumta da rashin hankalin wannan matashiyar yarinya wadda take neman jefa kanta cikin musiba da tashin hankali,dan ba tasan suwaye yaran bayan gidan sarki bane, da bata ku kusa da shiga shirginsu ba,amman zai kirawo mata su,shima yana mu'a mala da sune saboda da yawa cikinsu sun tasu cikin unguwar abokan juna ne,sun daina abota ne da yaga irin rayuwarsu shi kuma bazai iya ba,sai yakama sana'ar facin keke,a ganinshi hakan zai iya zaunar dashi lafiya,suna ta shaye shayensu ya iske su yace"shegu kundai ke shiga hankalinku ku?"

     wani daga cikinsu yace"malam ware tun bamu sauke maka hannun dama ba"

     Dariya Auwalu maifaci yayi yace"kai banza ne,sholin ke gaya maka karya"

     Adda ya zare yayu kan Auwalu gadan gadan daga can gefe wani da yake saman dakali zaune,yace"kai kure dakata mana"

    cak wanda aka kira da kure ya tsaya,ga alama wanda yayi maganar yana iko da dukkan wanda ke wajan"dawo ka zauna"

    ku mawa yayi yaci gaba da shan sholesho dinshi

    "kai!Auwal mai ya kawo ka nan,bayan munsan kana daya daga cikin masu gulmarmu mun zama yan iska,kuda mu yan iska ne munsan mutunci tunda gashi mun barka kana shan iskar duniya,duk hakan ya faru ne ta sanadiyyar munyi wasa tare, zuwa makarnta da cin abinci cikin kwano daya,wannan shine wani shudaddan lokaci ka zama abokina kuma dan uwana,amman duk da haka ka kiyayye shiga rayuwarmu,dan shiga rayuwarmu bazai maka kyau ba,saboda haka bace mana da gani,d'an gyaran keke"

     "wallahi ban sani ba,kana iya tasuwa muje sai kuyi magana"

      "muje dai muji kawai dan nima bata fada man ba"

     Sun isa inda motar Munnera take parke,cikin isa shamo ya tsaya daga gefi yace ta fito daga motar sai ta fadi abinda ya kawo ta.

     Bata da wani zabi sai na fituwar tunda ita ke nema"sannu ko"

    Fuskar shamo a daure yace"kima haka"

    "aiki nake san saku in babu damuwa"

    "in kuma akwai damuwar fa"

    Murmushi tayi tadan kwantar da murya cikin yanayin lallami tace"nasan ma bazaku watsar da tayi na ba"
 
    Fuskar shi ya sassauta daya ga ta kwanar da kanta yace"ina sauraranki"

   _Bayani tai mashi na tanasan su shiga gidansu suyi ma Aminatu bara zana,su kuma zane mata ita sosai yanda zata Shiga taitayinta a matsayinta na kanwar mijinta ta hanata shakatawa,karshi har marin ta tayi wanda wannan marin shine sanadiyar da ta zuciya ta badakwangilar a hura mata ita_

    Kai ya kauda yace idan aka kama mu fa,rayuwarmu ta tafi a banza saboda yan kudi da zaki bamu,ku zaki dauki duk wani abu da ya tasu?"

     "zan tabbatar da hakan bai faru ba"

    "idan ya farun fa"

    "zan dauki alhakin kumai"

   "babu damuwa ban adress din gidan,da yanda zamu shiga babu matsala"

     Kwatancan gidan taimashi sannan tace"shiga gidan dai akwai matsala gaskiya dan bazai shigon maku ba ta katanga,sai dai kuna iya daure maigadin idan kuka fito ku kwanci shi"

     "Idan kuma bamu samu damar daure shin bafa?"

     "inaga zan aikishi kawai kafin ya dawo sai ku aiwatar da abinda kukaje yi"

    Jikkarata ta fiddo dauku dubu dari tace"dubu dari ta wadatar da ku ai"

    "sai na ganku,Allah yasa kada ku bada matsala"

     Auwalu mai face ta dauka ta maidashi wajan sana'arshi sannan ta wurga mashi naira dubu biyar tace"gashi nan mai kashin tsiya,daga faci  baka iya kumai ba,zuciya ta mutu,kaga inda jarumai suke can"

   *LONDON*
 

      Kan gadon shi ya fada yana jin wani bakon yanayi a jikinshi,wayan shi tayi ringin harta tsinki baya da wani kuzarin da zai iya daga wayar,wani sabon kukan ta fara, tana bukatar a kai mata agaji,kamar bazai dauka ba kuma sai yayi tunanin kila kiran mai mahimmanci ne,ba tare da ya duba ba ya kanga saman kunne yayi shiru yaji wake magana,sansanyar muryar da baya mantawa  Itace tace _"i never expect this from you baby"_

   Banza yayi da ita,amman kuma bai katse mata wayar ba,tasan kuma bazai maganar ba inba yaga dama ba tace _"baby please don't break my heart_ mana"

   Banzan dai ya kara yi da ita babu wata alama da ta nuna mata zaiyi maganar ma,kuka ta saka mashi,je yayi bai kyauta mata ba, tunda ta karyu da ajinta ta neme shiri sai ya hakura,kamar mai maganar rada yace"zaki kashi man dodon kunne ne kike man kuka a cikin kunne?"

     "ai kaine kayi banza dani kake amsa ni"

    "ok ya isa,stop crying and mai ke damuwarki?

    Ji tayi kamar ta hadeyi zuciya ta mutu,saboda bacin ran da Ali ke bata,bai masan matsalarta ba kinan,bai masan ya wulakanta ta wajan paty ba kinan,lallai Ali baida imani bai kuma da tausayin zuciyarta data mace wajan kaunarshi,amman yana tambayarta maye matsalarta,kuka ta kara sakawa tace"wallahi nasan ka daina sona baby,yanxo har ka manta abinda kayi mani a wajan bathday patyn..."

    "wait?'yan mata kada ki bata ma kanki lokaci,kifa kinsan dan ki nazo wannan wajan kazaman yaran, kinsan bana hudda dasu, amman da kika ganne sai kika basar to a tunaninki zan iya san wannan yarinyar, _after all_ kinga yanda muka kwashi da ita amman kinje kin cuge waje daya kina _expecting_ dina inzo in baki hakuri ko in lallashi ke akan abinda gaban idanki kumai ya faru,to ba sai na maki bayanin kune waye ba,kin riga kinsani"

    "to am sorry,kazo yanzo,ku inzo ne?"

    mararshi ce ta wani murda,ga kanshi da yake juya mashi,wani irin amai na tasu mashi,yanayin da yake ji a jikinshi bai taba riskar kanshi a ciki ba,harwani  zazzabi zazzabi ya sakku mashi cikin lokaci kalilan,da kyar ya iya ce mata"kada kizo,nima bazan zo ba,muyi waya later"

   Kawai ya kashi wayanshi gaba daya saboda zata iya taimakawa wajan kara mashi rashin lafiyar da baisan musabbabin afkuwarta ba.

      Wata irin muguwar sha'awa yake ji wadda hankalin shi ya gagara misalta irinta,dakyar ya tashi ya fito falo ya shiga kicin ya samu lemon samiya matsa cikin ruwan lipton ya kwankwada ya fito ya samu saman kujera ya kwanta,babu wani cigaba da ya samu,sai ma kara hauhawa da abin yake,mata ne a gidan shi amman ba na kusanta ba,kanshi yaji yayi mashi wani irin nauyi ga amai na tahu mashi ga wata irin juwa na dibarshi,a kwancin da yake.

     Daga ita sai wata rigar bacci wadda tsirara take,ta fito falon,daya daga cikin kujerun ta samu ta zauna sannan ta wara kafafuwanta yanda zai iya ganu kalar pant din da ta sanya,duk a kukarinta najan hankalinshi,amman bata san bama ta ita yake ba,ta yanda zai i da wannan bakon yanayi yake ,dan bai masan da ita a wajan ba,amai ya dinga kwarawa babu kakkautawa,a rude ta tasu,ta isa wajanshi ta kama shi tace"man sannu,mai ya same ka kake amai haka"

    Dakyar ya iya fizge hannunshi daga nata,ya fara bin bango domin ya isa daki amman hakan ya gagara,dole sai da taimakon ta ya sada kanshi da dakin baccin shi.

      Saman gado ya fada,sanna ya dafe kanshi da mararshi dake barazanar fashiwa,toilet ta shiga ta hada mashi ruwan wanka domin ya  gasa jikinsh, tana jin tsoron kada ace sanadiyyar maganin da ta saka mashi ya sanya shi wannan ciwan,ai kuwa da ta shiga ukku idan har ya gano ta,bayan ta fito tazo dai dai saitin fuskarshi da tayi jawur saboda azabar da yake sha tace"na hada maka ruwa ka shiga kadan watsa,zaka samu kwari a jikin ka"

     Mugun kallo ya watsa mata sannan yace _"get out"_

      tashi tayi ta kama hanyar fita daga dakin,bayan ta fita ne da sannu da bin bango ya shiga dakin ya amfani da ruwan,domin ruwan data hada mashi,sosai yaji dadin shi, daman yanayin akwai dan sanyi amman ba mai irin cutar wa bane,ya fito ya dan mutsika mai a jikinshi,sannan ya kuma ya kwanta,ji yayi kamar ana kara ingizashi wajan aikata abinda ku kusa baya da burin yin hakan,amman ya, ya iya da yanayin da yake jin kanshi in za'a taramashi mata goma zai gama masu aiki batare da ya gaji ba,yana mamakin yanda ya tsinci kanshi a wannan yanayin mara amfanuwa a gareshi saboda zai jama kanshi raini da wulakanci,wajan yaran da ya tsana kamar mutuwa,tsakiyayi ya kara tamke mararshi ku zaiji wani dan sauki amman ina.

      da sallama ta shigo dakin dan nesa dashi ta samu kujera ta zauna tace"sannu Man,ku a samu maka magani kasha"

    banzan kallo ya kara mata yace"ba nace ki fita man a daki ba"

    daman ta kudura a ranta kumai za'ayi sai dai ayi dan wallahi yau ta kai makura gashi ta kara cusa magungunan da boka mai gani har hanji ya bata,babu wani tsoro tattre da ita kawai ta isa wajanshi tadan shafa gashin kirjinshi tace"sorry Man"

    gezo ta fara mashi da wata irin hallita wadda baisan ya zai misalta ta ba,daga bisani kumaya nemi hankalinshi yaje ya gushi a wata duniyar,sarrafashi ta dinga yi yanda ranta ke so,ta dinga kai mashi sako inda ta san dole ya bada kai buri ya hau,a cikin yanayin gushiwar hankalin da yake ciki ya tsinci kanshi da maida mata da martanin abinda take mashi,a wata duniya suka tsincikansu wadda saida Munayya ta gwammace da bata zo duniya ba,tun anayin abun na mutunci da shauki har aka kai fagen da za'a fara kusawa,aka kama hanyar dauriya da cije baki,duk yanda ta dauki Alin yafi gaban nan sai gashi anzo matakin da ba dan gidan yana da nisa tsakaninshi da farfajiyar gidan ba,da babu abinda zai hana ba'a kawo mata taimakun gaggawa ba,kuka take babu mai taimakonta,kuma bata ga Alamar abin zai tsaya ba,tun tana iya mutse har tazo sai dai ta dinga zubda hawaye,iya jegata ta jegata,sannu a  hankali ya fara dawo wa cikin duniyarshi, bayan ya dauki sama da awa takwas yana yada manufarshi a wata mahada ta Munayya,da kyar ya iya jan jiki shi gefi yana maida numfashi, ga wani ciwan kai da yake barazanar tarwatsa mashi kai,nannauyan bacci yayi awan gaba dashi mai dadi da nishadi.

     Kuka take mara sauti,duk da tasan ta dan raba ma alhazawa a waje, amman bata zace za'ayi mata irin wannan aika aikar ba,dakyar ta iya tashi da taimakun bango ta mike sai taga inda ta tashi jini face face, ta daga kafa daya da niyyar tayi tafiya amman abun ya faskara wata kara ta saki,wadda ta tada Ali daga nannuyan baccin da ya kamashi, bayan ya gama aykawa da sakkone,da sauri ya tashi tsaye sannan ya kalleta yace"waya kawoki dakina a wannan lokacin"ya daga kanshi wajan agogo 4:58pm,ganin babu kumai jikinta yaji kanshi ya sara ya waiwaya ya maida kallanshi saman gadon shi, yaga jini kamar an yanka karamar dabba,a hankali ya zauna bakin gadon ya dafi kai yana ambatun sunayan Allah,wani irin aika aika ya yi ma yarinyar mutane haka,yayi imanin baya cikin hankalinshi ya ma iya kusantar yarinyar da itace ta biyu a jerin wadan da ya tsana a gidanshi,ya akai haka ta faru dashi,wannan azal ce ta hau kanshi,waigawa yayi ya kara kallanta yaga yanda gaba daya tayi wani irin yanayi ita bata mutu ba ita bata wani mutsinkirki ga gadon shi yayi face face da jini,salati ya dinga maimaita wa a cikin zuciyarshi,garas yake jinshi duk wani yanayi da ya tsinci kanshi yaji ya samu natsuwa,tashi yayi ya kankanci ido yace"burinki ya cika kin samu abinda kike so,saboda haka sai ki baci man da gani tunkan in idasa ki yanzon nan"
    
    "ban iya kuda mutsa hannuna ma wallahi"

     "kin zabi mutuwar kinan"

     "duk yanda kai dani ba damuwa bace"

     "ban taba ganin mayya irinki mai kaima namiji kanta ba sai ke,ai an bari in taya"

    wayarshi ce tayi kara,ya dauku da sauri ya dauka da yaga sunan mai kiran a saman wayar, cikin girmama wa ya rissina yace"Alhaji barkada asubah"

    daga daya bangaran aka amsa da "kun tashi lafiya?"

    "lafiya lau Alhamdullhi?

    "yaushi kake shirin dawo wa"

    "sati biyu masu zuwa insha Allahu"

     "Babu damuwa, Allah ya kai mana rai"

    "Ameen, ya su Hajja?"

    "gata nan kusa tana gaishika,kumatana fatan ka natso kayi hankali"

    dariya yayi,sannan Alhja ya kara da cewa"kaji tsoran Allah, yaran da ka tafi dasu ka dauka tamkar amana Allah ya danka maka idan kayi fada dasu da Allah kayi haka, idan ka cutar dasu ka cutar da wanda ya baka amanarsu,saboda haka ka guje haduwarka da ubangijinka,haka kuma idan kayi adalci kaji dadin rayuwa idan kuma bakayi ba kai ka sani da yanda Allah zai fushi da kai,sai munkara gaisawa "ya kashiwayar ranshi baci.

      297



    Karu na farko da matan gidanshi suka sa mahaifinshi ya fara jin haushinsa,shiyasa babu rage babu raguwa tsakaninsu,ai kuma ya kara tunasar dashi akan mahimmancin amana da adalci,tashi yayi ya shiga bayi ya hada ruwan mai zafin gaske,wanda a tunaninshi zai iya kuna mata gaban ta ya hada,sungumarta yayi kamar ya dauki kashi,haka yake ya mutsa fuska,ya jefa ta cikin ruwan,wata kara ta saki mai wahala,saboda a tunaninta ma ta kune,da sauri ya dawo ya kara dan nata cikin kwamin wankan.

     "Dalla malama kada ki daukan sakarai,bayan na hada maki ruwan  zaki fita,dan kin raina man wayau"

    "ai ruwan yayi zafi Man"

    Bakinta ya dalle sannan yace"wai ke wace irin mayya ce,da baki jin kashedi, ina ga kun hada iri da fir'auna saboda taurin kanki,kada in kara jin kin kirani da Man,inba haka ba kuma rai zai baci"

       Kuka take tana musul musul domin ta kwaci kanta daga wannan mugunta ta Ali,amman ta kasa,bata da wani zabi sai na hakura idan ma kasheta zai sai ya bada himma,kusan minti biyar yana danne da ita cikin ruwan,sannan yace"da fatan zafin ruwan yayi maki yanda ya kamata"

     Zuge wajan ke mata,amman bai hana ta matse ba, ta fito dakyar tace"eh babu laifi harma yasu yayi sanyi ma,zafinshi bai kai yanda nake so ba"

    Rankwashi ya aza mata bisa kai yace"mayya kawai,ai su nai ya babbake kawai,a huta da jaraba,ni bansan wace kaddara take bibiyata ba da har tsautsai yasa nayi wani abu dake,duk ma yanda akai akwai wani abu,za kuma ki bane harma ki lalace in dai akwai hannunki a tursasa ne ga kusanarki da nayi"

    Tawel dinshi ta daura sannan ta nufi gado zata yaye bedshet din yace mata"malama kama hanyar dakin ki kawai kada in kara ganin gilmawarki cikin gidan nan,dan zan iya karya ki,saboda bakin cikin na hada jiki dake"

     Dariya ta saka tace"ai baza ma ka ganne ba, saboda baccin gajiya zanje in kwasa,gwarzan namiji yayi aiki a kaina,ai sai na hada da magunguna ma"

   "ai sai ki zauna shiririta,sauran suyi maki zarra,ki zama yar kallo,yar aiki"

     "Ai Hajja babu wata shegeya wallahi,sai yanda mukayi da Ya Ali,kidan bamu lokaci,dan ma mutuniyar tawa kamar kura take wajan tsoro,kuma tana sane da zakunan gidan bayan kuwacce a sama take"

    "a to,kaddai ayi babu ku,aji kunya mace gidan miji bayyi da ita ai anyi asara kinan,kuma anci baya?"

      Sanna ta tafi tana kara jaddada masu kada su bata lokaci karfi biyar za'a hallara gidan Ali Zaki.

     Bayan fitar ta Aminatu tace"kinsan Allah kamar yanda na fada maki kada kiji tsoran Ya Ali,idan har kika kuskura kika dasa ma kanki tsoranshi, wallahi sunanki sorry,kinsan dai da yayan banza kike zaune"

    Deji tace"ke  bari kawai Mena,inajin son wannan yayan naki fa,amman kuma ina tsoran shi"

    "to ni dai ki fara shirin kuma wa gidan aure,Malama kada Hajja ta iske munafira bamu fara kumai ba"

    "ok,bari in tashi,amman fa zanyi missing Hajja fa"

    "au Hajja ma ba ,ne ba kinan?"

    "keda tare zamu kuma gidan,ai babu mai rabani da ke Allah,sai mutuwa"

    "ai kuwa gaskiya, kinasan Alhaji yayi kasa kasa dani kinan,zaman ma da nayi gidanku ba dan ranshi ya su ba,dakyar Hajja ta taushe shi,da cewa yayi sai na dawo gida"

    "wai da gaske kike Mena?"

    "wallahi ke daya zaki kuma,zan dai maki rakiya dakin mijinki"

    Kai Deji ta langabe tace"kice zan kuma akurki inda sai yanda akayi dani"

    "idan kinsu ba,in kuma kika genu akan turbar dana duraki ai sai yanda kikayi da Yaya Ali wallahi, tunda kidin kumai yaji,kala ce ke,ta musamman"

    "hmmm kayya dai,yayan nan nake fa baida dama,wajan rainin hankali,da hantara"

    Suna fira suna shirya kayan Deji da suka jedo daga kasuwa, kama daga kananun kaya, rigunan bacci da sabbin dinkuna da Hajja tasa akayi mata, saboda kayan nata ba wasu masu kyau bane,ba kuma na zamani bane.
 
    Amenatu tace"sai ki fidda daya daga cikin wanda aka karbu yau wajan dinki ki saka ko?"

    "haka ya kama ayi ko?"

    "ban sani ba yar rainin hankali"

    Ayya daga tambaya kuma,daman nasan kin gaji da gane na a gefin ki"

    "eh naji dai,a tafi inda aka fe wayau"

    "Zaki sani ne kima,bari a kaiki gidan Ya Ommar ku tayaki zama bazan ba kafin angwaye suzo"

   "maganar banza kinan Hajiyata"

    Wata lemon green din super ta fidda wadda mai dinki ya dadi yana lalata lokaci wajan ganin ya tsara dinkin yanda ya kamata,sannan ta dauku black hijab,ta aji kusa da kayan,kallo Ameenatu ta watsa mata tace"malama gyale zaki saka tunda mudin motar gida zamu shiga,ba ta haya ba,so nake kallan farko da yaya Ali zai maki yasan ba wai Deji bace,Khadeja ce wanna"

    Dauke shi tayi ta maida cikin shirgin kayan da suka hadawa,sannan ta dauku lemon grean din gyalan da yayi shege da kayan,ta dura mata saman kayan,takuma ta dauku hill sosai na takalmi ta aji mata gefe tace ga kayan da nayadda ki saka nan wajan"

   Dariya Deji tayi tace"Allah yasa wanda ake dominshi ya kyasa"

   "ai dai kinsan nasan zabinshi,hatta kalar kayan da kika saka yana san kalar saboda haka sai ki shirya ma tarbar maigidanki"

   Towel ta daura ta fada toilet tana fadin" kada Hajja tazo ta iske muna surutu bamuyi wanka ba"

A hankali motocin nasu suka tsaya a harabar gidan nashi,kusan a tare suka fito daga motar Hajja da Alhaju suna cikin daya,Deji da Aminatu suma tasu daban take,hmmm masu karatu tsaya fada muku irin haduwar da Deji tayi abun bazai fadu ba, amma ba karya Deji ta hadu dan babu yadda za'ayi ka yarda cewa wannan Deji ce 'Yar kauye wadda ka Sani,mai surutun tsiya,da sallamar su suka Shiga falon nasu, babu kowa a falon dan haka suka zauna akan kujerun kawai Alhaji ya dauko wayar shi ya kirawo Ali.

    Umarni Alhaji yayi ma Ameenatu da ta kira mashi duk wat mace da take mallakin Alin,kofar dakin kowa ta bubbuga atare suka bude Munayya da Azeema ita kuwa yar mulkin sai da kuwa yazo sannan ta bayyana,cikin isa ta dan samu waje ta zauna tace "barka da yamma"

   Dattaku irin na Alhaji bai nuna mata kumai ba ya amsa daga irin gaisuwar rainin hankalin da tayi mashi.

     Bayan ya fito ya kai gaisuwa wajan Alhaji sannan yaje kusa da Hajja yace"Hajja na nayi missing dinki sosai"

    Hannunshi ta kama tace "nima haka Babana amman kuma anya zan yadda da kai,saboda naga kayi kiba kamar ba kaiba"

    Dariya yayi yace "mun sameku lafiya"

 Tace "lafiya kalau,ya turan kuma"

    Alhamdullilahi"

     Aminatu ce tace"au ni bakayi missing dina ba kinan"

     Hannu ya mika mata yace"haba Nena Isa sweet sister,ai kina daya daga cikin abinda ke raina"

      Kamar saukar aradu yaji anci"ina yine,Ya Ali"

     Kallo ya tsare ta da shi,yakasa sanin wacece wannan a cikin family dinshi,ya dai san Aminatu bata da kawa,basarwayayiyace"sannu ko"

   Bayan yan gaishe gaishe da akayi Alhaji yace dukkansu su bashi hankalinsu nan yana da mahimmiyar magana,Alhaji ne ya fara bude taron da addu'a, sannan yace abunda ya taramu anan ba komai bani sai domin ina san inji  dalilin daya saka ke  ya nuna Munayya kikayi yunkurin kisan kai,dan haka sai ki fada mana ke muke saurare"


    Bata tabakawowa cewa asirinta zai tunu ba cikin hura hance tayi kamar baza tayi magana ba sannan tace"ban san abinda ake magana akai ba fa"

    "duk wani binciki da mukayi ya tabbatar mana da kidin kika haddasa,wadandakika saka sunahannun hukuma yanzo haka" 

    Bata da wani zabi sai fadin gaskiya tace"rashin kunya sukayi min shi yasa ni kuma nayi maganin su"

    Alhaji yakulu yace"yanzo kinqnufin yqn sara suka su zasuyi maki maganin abinda sukayi maki,na dauka idan kika fada man zan dauki mataki a kansu"
 
     Kai ta kauda irin kanka ake jidin nan"to shi kinan babu kumai wannan shi ya nuna cewa zaki iya bada dama a kashi mutum baki da damuwa"

    Babu wata alamar nadama tattare da ita a zahiranci ma kunkune take,wata kila zagin Alhajin take oho.

   "Saboda haka a wannan gaba duk hukuncin da ya ga damar dauka akanki bani da wani zabi tunda na baki dama aman baki amfani da ita ba"


    Gyaran murya Ali yayi yace"Alhaji daman ya kamata kasan cewa wannan yarinyar bata da mutunci ku miskala zarratin idan kayi duba da daman mahaifinta bai bada ta dan wata manufa ba sai dan biyan bukaar kanshi na kara kusanci damu,wannan kinan duk baka san cewa wannan yarinyar bata da mutunci ba sai idan ka tuntubi wanda ya santa yasan ainahin wacece ita,saboda haka na yanke igiyar auren da ke tsakanina da ita guda biyu idan ta samu miji tayi aure"

   
       Ali ya nisa sannan"haka zalika kuma zama da ita hadari ne Alhaji, duk wadda tayi haka zata kuma yin abunda yafi haka dan haka ina Neman umarnin ka hukuncin da nayanke kada ya bata maka rai"

     "matarka ce baburuwana kuma a wannan gabar"

    "Nagode" abinda ya fito bakinta kinan sanna ta haye sama wani salon taunar cingum take tafiyar takama take da isa,kamar irin Allah ya rabata da kaya din nan.



      Gabaki dayan su ragowar matan jikin su yayi sanyi bare ma munnir wadda idanta ya raina fata da ta ankara da irin duban da Ali yake mata.

   Tsam Ali ya mike yaisa wajan da Alhaji yake zaune yace"Alhaji gawani shaida da zai tabbatar da wani hukunci da zanyanke yanzo,shima idankaban izini"

    WayarAlhaji ya Amsa,abindaya fara gane ne ya tada mashi da hankalida saure ya mika ma Aliwayar yanasalati da nemantsaria wajan mahalicinshi,wanikallo ya wurga ma Munayyayace"amman kinyi asara zina da auranki"

    Ihu ta saka tace"wallahikarya yake man dan kawai nem ya sakan ne"

    Tsaki Alhaji yayi ya dafe kai yace"Allah ya kyauta tashi mu tafi Rumasa'u"



*_Edited by Ummour Aleesha_*


   Bilkisu Bilyamin


MATAN ALI.
(the most lovely story of nice family)

  
 BILKISU BILYAMIN

 

     *1  END*



    Ya kasa yanda zai mayi shari'ar duk wani tunaninshi ya tsaya,harwani sashi na zuciyarshi yana tunanin baima shi Alin Adalci ba, da ya kasa nema mashi yara masu tarbiyya,bayan Ali ya dawo daga rakiyarsu ya iske Munayya har lokacin tana rusar uban kuka,har inda take ya karasa ya dan duka dai dai yanda zata iya jin duk wani harafi da zai furta,sannan ya bude dakakkiyar muryarshi yace"ban taba ganin asararra ba a duniyarnan irinki,wai ke anya jikin ki na aiki da hankali,to bari in fada maki bazan taba barinki kibar gidan nan ba har sai na tabbatar da na ladaftar dake akan wahalar da kika bani,saboda haka tun ban lalata maki kamanne ba ki tashi maza ki zagaye falan nan da tsallan kwadi,ki maya sau goma"

     "wallahi baka isa ba,bazanyi ba, ka sake nin kuma sai kayi man mugunta"

   "wallahi sai kinyi,ba kuma shi kadai zakiyi ba,akwai wani da zai zo bayan kin gama wannan,na dauka kinsan wa kika aura?"

    "na sani mana, mugu tsautsayi yasa na aura"

    Ai kuwa bata gama rufe baki ba ya daura mata mahaukacin mari a saman bakinta,kamar cizan kunama taji saukar marin,wata kara ta saki sanna ta dafe bakinta,daga bisani kuma jini ya wanke mata hannu,belt ya cire ya dinga nasarta kamar daman an halitta mashi ita danya jibga ne,bayan yayi mata iyakar bugun da yaga ya gamsar dashi,sannan ya dakata,ya fiddo wata farar takarda ya wurga mata yace"wannan bugun na wasa da hankalina da kikayi har kika samu nasarar da na kusance jakka,kazama,kilaki irinki ne,wannan kuma takardar saki ukku ce wadda sanadiyar bin mazanki ya jaza maki,minti biyu na ba ki dauku duk wani abu da kika san naki ne ayi gaba,kika dara haka kuda da sakan daya ne,zaki dana sani a gaba daya duniyarki ta yan iska"

    Da kayr ta iya tashi saboda mugun bugun da yayi mata,duk ya fasa mata jiki ta nufi dakin da ta zauna ta debu yan abubuwan data san zasuyi mata amfani,ta zo ta wuce ta gabanshi ya ci gaba da aika mata zagin da duk ya iya.

   Duk abinda yake faruwa Deji da Aminatu suna kallo ta sakalan kofa,kuka Deji take kamar anci uwarta ta mutu,dariya ita kuwa Ameenatu take tace"saura Waccan mai kama da dangin karunar,nasan ita nata daban ne"

    Kumai tana kallo daga kofar dakinta ganin yanda ya keme Munayya yasa ta kuma daki ta saka key dan bata shirya cin na jaki ba a wajan Ali,zata jira har zuwa sanda zai bar gidan sannan ta tafi,bugun kofarta yake kamar zai balla amman kememe tayi banza dashi ta'ki budewa, tasan idan har ta bude kofar nan tabbas mutuwarta ce ta karatu,saboda haka bata shirya ma,ban kwana da kumai nata ba.

    Jin an daina buga kofar yasa ta tashi da niyyar tafiya,sai ta tuna mugunta irin ta Ali,dakyar inba safayn makulle yaji daukuwa ba ya dawo,ai kuwa da sauri ta kara tura makullen cikin gidanshi,ai kuwa sai taji ana amfani da wani makullen ta baya,jin akwai makulle yasa ya kara buga kofar daga can taji yace"samun saukin whalarki ki fito yanzo,idan kika bari nayi yanda nayi na fiddo ke to abin bazai maki kyau ba,dan billahil lazi sai nayi fatafata da naman jikin ki"

    Da yake ta iya tauren kai da,ke fadi, girman kai sai tace"An gaya maka kuwa banza ne da zai tsaya ka dinga cutarshi,ni ba sakara baci,saboda haka bazan bude ba,idan yasu ka sauke ginin gidan ka fiddo ne"

     Ranshi ya gama baci da duk harafinta daya, wani mugun bugu ya kaima kofar dakin da hannunshi,sai ga jini ya na fitowa amman ku a jikinshi,da saure Aminatu ta fito daga dakin Deji tayi saure ta rike hannunshi tace"please kayi hakuri Ya Ali"

     Wani mugun kallo ya watsa mata wanda da sauri ta sake hannunshi,da saure ta kuma daki ta dauki wayanta ta buga ma Ommar wanda shigowarshi garin kinan daga wata semina da bankinsu aka turashi,ku ida sa shiga gida baiba ya samu wannan waya daga wajan Ameenatu,minti biyar cikakkiya ta sadashi da gidan Alin.

    Gaba daya hannunshi ya kumbura ga jini nata fita,aman bai damu ba ya samu kujera cikin falon ya zauna yace babu inda zaije har sai ta fito,ya nuna mata ita karamar yar iska ce,Mena kuka kawai take tana kukarin kama hannunshi ta daure domin jinin ya daina zuba,aman ya hana ta,karshi ma wurgi yayi da ita da kafa,ran maza ya baci kinan,a lokuta da dama shi yasa basa san bacin ranshi, idanuwanshi sukan rufe ya kasa gane gabanshi ballantana bayanshi,zai dinga abu cikin duhu,abune mai matukar wahala shawo kanshi,indai ba abinda ya kesu ya aiwatar ba.

     Kusa dashi Ommar ya zauna amman baice mashi kumai ba,ya cigaba da shafa bayanshi dayan hannunshi kuma yana rike da hannuwanshi da suke ta rawa kamar mazari,ajiyar zuciya ya fara,wata kan wata,hannunshi Ommar ya kama ya daga shi suka nufi dakinshi,duk wannan abun babu wanda ya tanka ma wani cikinsu,alama yayi ma Aminatu da hannu akan ta iske su dakin Alin,kai ta girgiza saboda idan yana cikin irin wannan yanayin bata ma ku kusa da rabarshi, dan yana iya illata mutum,ruwa mai sanyi Ommar ya kawo mashi ya tsiyaya ya bashi ya sha,sannan ya kara mashi ya kara shanyewa a hankali numfashin shi yake fita,sannan ajiyar zuciya tana biyuwa baya,agajin gaggawa Ommar ya ba hannun nashi,ya nade mashi da bandeji,da yake gidan bai rabu da kayan gyaran rauni da magunguna irin haka,ta tasu.

       Idanuwanshi ya lumshi sannan ya dura hannunshi saman kanshi yana jin kamar zai tarwatse saboda hayaniyar da yayi yau,kallan Ommar yayi yace"zan dan huta"

    Kai Ommar ya daga mashi yace"nima yanzo na shigo garin,zuwa anjima sai mu hadu"

    Hannu ya daga mashi Alamar sai sun hade din,fita yayi ya ja mashi kofan sannan ya karasa wajan Ameenatu da take dakwan jiran fitowarshi,da saure ta tareshi tace"Ya Ommar bana cikin natsuwa,ya Ya Alin yake?"

    "lafiya lau,ya na bukatar hutu ne"

    "kasan Allah yau matarnan sai dai kune ku ita, zan mata shegen bugu"

   "wace mata kuma?"

   Nan ta bashi labarin abubuwan da suka faru,bayan dawowar Alin zuwa yanzo da ya iske wannan tashin hankalin.

    "kada ki mata kumai and kuma yanzo zan sa ta bar gidan,saboda kada Ali yazo yayi wani abun dana sani,kin san idan ranshin nan ya baci,kinsan zai iya kumai"

    "shiyasa zanci ubanta kafin ya tashi daga baccin in yasu in kurata gidan ubanta"

   Saukuwa take daga saman binan, janye da trolly dinta, ta sauku kasa tace"babban kuskuran da zaki karayi a rayuwarki taba lafiyar jikina,kila abinda nai maki bai gamsar da ke ba,to zan canza salo"

   Kukan kura Ameenatu tayi ta kwalfa mata mari sannan ta chakumuta ta makureta,duk iya yanda Ommar yayi ya amsheta ya kasa,jibgarta Ameenatu take kamar Allah ya aikuta duk wata hanya da zata kare kanta bata samuba saboda Ameenatu ta mata kofar rago.

    Azeeema jin hayaniya take kamar anayi a cikin kanta,bayan tafarka tace gaba da jin hayaniya jikinta babu kwari ta bude kofa,sai tayi tozali da bura ubar da ake cikin gidan wanda Ommar ya gagara rabawa yama rasa yanda zaiyi,da saure ta sauku ta nufi inda suke tana "lafiya Meena dan Allah ki daina"

    Deji da tun sanda taga ana suburbudar Munayya ta shige kuryar daki bata fito ba sai yanzo da taji ana Ameenatu,da gudu ta dinga tsallakematakalar binan,ta iso inda ake yakin badar,ankasa kwatar Munnera daga hannun Ameenatu saboda tace sai ta tuge gashin kanta gaba daya,sannan zata kyaleta.

   Da karfin tsiya ta danki karin gashin da tayi ta dinga fizga, kururuwa Munnera take, wannan kururuwa da take shi yayi sandiyyar tashin Ali daga bacci,da saure ya fito daga dakin,abinda ke faruwa yake kallo hannu ya nade bisa kirjisa ya  zuba masu ido,wani mugun kallo Ommar ya jefa mashi yace"kai mata magana mana,tak'i sauraran kuwa please"

     Cikin dakakkiyar murya yace"Meena kyaleta haka"

     Sai da ta kaima bakinta nushi sannan ta daga ta tana huce amman duk da haka jikinta kyarma yake ji take kamar ta kara kai mata chafka,rungumeta Ommar yayi yana shafa bayanta yana bubbugawa,cikin izza Ali ya taku kusa da Munnera yace"daman na fada maki bazaki bargidan nan ba har sai na illata ke,kinga kuwa ku a haka aka barki an maki illa,tunda antuge maki gashi guda biyar, ba tamutunci ba"

     Ommar yace"ku barta ta tafi mana tunda ai kun kuya mata hankali"

    "ta dai kuya mata bakin yake,ni yanzo zanyi nawa"

     Azeema ya kalla yace"inda reza ina so,right known"

     "babu Ya Ali, please mana"

    "akwai saman mirron dakina kawo man yanzo"

    Tasan babu gaddama haka taje ta dauku rezar ta kawo mashi.

     Ommar yace"wai kai wani irin dan iska ne ,anci ka kyale yarinyar mutane ta tafi amman ka kiya"

    Tureshi Mena tayi daga rikon da yayi mata tace"a kyaleta taci bulus, kalle kaina yanda tasa aka mana aski nida Khadeja"

    Da sauri taje ta cizge dankwalin kan Deji tace"kagani,saboda haka ka bari ya daukar mana fansa ne kanwar shice, ita kuma matarshi ce,kai kuwa abokin shine,saboda haka wallahi sai an mata askin itama"

    Ran Ommar ya baci da maganganun da take fada,daman yana tsoran fushinta tana da fushi kuma bata da control idan ranta ya baci,amman kuma baikama ta fada mashi wanann maganar ba,ranshi bace shima yace"ku kashe ta mana, matsalarku ce, tunda kunfi kuwa iya jin zafi"

    Ya kada kanshi yayi tafiyarshi,ranshi a masifar bace,da maganganun da Ameenatu tayi mashi na rashin fahimta.

      Kwal a kwabu Ameenatu tayi ma Munnera bayan ta lafta mata wata katuwar yanka a saman kai,kuma duk akan umarnin da Ali ya bata ne,sannan ta kara da nata,ta dadi tana tanajin gashinta saboda Ommar dinta yana san mace mai gashi,tasa an aski mata shi bata san ranar da zai kara fituwa ba.


       *_SHAFIN MASOYA DEJI DA ALI_*


    Yau itace rana ta farko da zata fara gwada sa'arta akan Ali,a gurguje ta shiga kicin ta hada mashi tuwan shinkafa miyar kubewa danya tasha nama zuku zuku,ta jere bisa daining table, sannan ta kara kumawa kicin ta hada mashi kunun aya mai dadi wanda yaji kayan kamshi da madara da dabino,ta maida cikin friage,da uku uku take hayewa binan sauri take taje ta tsala wanka,kalolin man da Ameenatu ta zabamata take amfani dasu ta dinga shafa ma jikinta,riga da skart din hollan blue ta saka gaba daya jikinta ya dame,dukiya fulaninta ta fito,das a rigar mazaunanta sun zauna das a skrt din,gaban madobi  ta kuma da dan juya ,sannan ta dan gwada yanda zata dinga aika mashi da kallan kauna,sannan ta danyi gaba ta dawo,sannan ta juya wata yar kara ta sake "woooo yaro da kudi abokin tafiyar manya zaka zo hannu zaka biya bashin duk abinda ka aikata"

    Wayarta ta dauku ta dan kashe photo guda biyar ta turama Meena ta whatapp "kinga wankan yau,sai na gasa yayanki a ovan dina"

    Ta rufe data ta wullar da wayar saman gado,sannan ta kuma gaban madubi tana kara lailayi fuskarta da kayan kwaliya.

    Sheguwarshi falon kinan da wata irin gajiya da yunwa,da kyar ya iya tashi ya shiga ya watsa ruwa ya saka kaya masu saukin nauyi ya fito ya zauna saman daining table,kai ya dafe da yana da gata da yaci abinda ranshi ke so,to sai dai peter bai iya kumai ba sai dafa kayan turanci,shi kuma yana san tuwo da sauran dangin kayan hatse,kulolin ya buda sai yayi arba da abinda zuciyarshi ke so,saving kanshi yafara.

    Ta jima da jin shigowarshi amman ta tsaya lalata lokaci wajan feshi jikinta da kalolin OUD din turarukan da take amfani dasu bayan ta tashi daga Deji ta kuma Khadeja,ta kalmi ta dauku mai tsinin gaski,ta saka ma kyarkyawar kafarta,sannan ta kara juyawa a saman madubi "yes"tace sanna ta fito das das das..... Din da takalmanta ki bada amo shi yaja hankalin Ali wajan kallan abinda ke faruwa,kallan cikin ido suke ma juna sosai Deji ta tsunduma idanuwanta cikin na Ali,sakwanni take aika mashi masu wuyar dauka a wajanshi,duk jarumtarshi naganin bai mata kallan kurilla ba, amman ya kasa dauke idan shi daga nata,cigaba da saukuwa take gaba daya jikinta kadashi take,ku ina tsalle yake,har ta iso tsakiyar falon,cikin sanyin murya da kissa tace"barka da dawo wa Ya Ali"

    Cikin karfin hali ya kauda kai ba tare da ya tanka ta ba ya cigaba da tsakorar abincin,kafa daya ta dura saman daya sannan ta fuskanci shi sosai ta kuma kafa mashi idanuwa,zuciyarshi ta matsa mashida sankara kallan halittar da ke barazanar tarwatsa lagon shi,karaf suka hada idanuwa luu.. Tayi da idanuwanta,kwarewa yayi ya fara tari da sauri ta nufi inda yake,ta dinga bubbuga bayanshi sannan ta bashi ruwa ya amsa ya sha,sai da ya dawo dai dai daga shi har ita suka ankara da hannunta dake bisan bayanshi,da sauri ta janye sannan ta dan rankwafu har sunajin hucen juna,turaranta da nashi suka hadu suka bada wani irin kamshi mai saukar da kasala,gaba daya kirjinta ya bayyana abu kalilan ne bai ida fituwa ba,baki ya lashi,yakara kai masu chafka ta hanyar kallo,jikinshi ne ya fara rawa,cikin muryar kissa da wani yanayi na wahala tace"bari in zuba maka kunun ayar"

    Kanshi ya dauki daga kallan abinda ke barazanar zidda mashi aji,ya dauki spon da niyyar kara diban tuwan amman abin ya gagara saboda kumai ya kwanci mashi,tsaki yayi sannan yayi wurgi da spon din ya tashi ya wuce daki,dariya Deji tayi tace"zaka sani ne,yau duk masifarka baka taba ni ba na dauka zan sha maruka"

     Daki ya kuma ya zauna saman kujera ya dafi kai yana tunanin wace halittace cikin gidanshi ke barazanar tarwatsa mashi duniyar jin dadin shi,wacece ita? Kuma daga ina take?mai take gidanshi?kuma wacece?oho ita ta sani.

    Wayarshi ce tayi kara daga daya bangaran akace"kana gida ne?"

   "eh,daman ina san ganinka mutumina"

   "da kake san ganina mai zan maka?"

   Dariya ya saka yace"sai kazo zaka ga abinda zaka man"

      Tsaki Ommar yayi ya kashi waya.

      "nasan bairasa wani bayani akan wannan aljanar yarinyar ta gidanshi ba,tuna wa yayi da tunda ya dawo basu kara haduwa da Azeema ba,yasan kuma bai kyauta ba kudan cikin jikinta ya kamata ace yadan raga mata inda hali ma ya fara kuya ma kanshi sakewa da ita,tashi yayi ya haura saman ya nufi dakinta noking yayi daga ciki tace"waye?"

   "malama bude dalla"

   Da sauri ta dauki hijabi ta dura bisa wata yar mitsilar rigar da ta saka saboda zafin daya addabeta, gashi kuma tasha yaji sosai,zafi ya tasu mata,bude kufar tayi,rabata yayi ya wuce, daya daga cikin kujeron dakin ya nema Ya zauna,bakin gado ta zauna,tace"ya Ali barka da yamma"

   "kima haka,ya jikin naki?"

   "inajin sauki sosai ma"

   "yanzo zaki iya zagaye gidan nan da gudu?"

    "zan iya, saboda yanzo inajin karfi a jiki na"

    "ah to yayi,ya zan gane kina jin karfi a jikinki?"

   "idan kaci inyi gudun zan iya"

   "akwai abinda yafi gudu kara lafiya ma,ku a kuya maki shi ne?"

   "eh ka kuya man"

       Damarda ta samu Ali na duguwar fira da ita shi ya bata damar shagwabe mai fuska,kumai na jikinta ya cuku na shanunta sunyi wani tsaye bisa hijabi,ga bakinta yayi wani jaja,rabanshi da mace tun artabunsu da Munayya,wannan kuma babu wani armashi sai wahala tunda babu natsuwa,daga inda yake ya tashi ya isa bakin gadan ya daga hijabin ya cireshi,tsammmmmmm ya ji tsigar jikinshi ta mutsa,sosai yake muradin mace a wannan gabar,zama yayi kusa da ita har jikinshi na gogar nata yace"kinyi kiba kumai naki ya cuku,wai duk cikin ne haka?"

   Hannunshi ta dan guce sannan ta yayemu hijabin ta tana kare kirjinta,tace"ina jin mafa kumburi nake,yau da kyar na tashi ga zazzabi,dan ma nasha magani"

   Fuska ya tsoke yace"bana san gaddama,sannan kuma bana san karya,yanzu kika gama man gaddama dan na cire hijabinki, haka kuma kinman karya kinci baki lafiya bayan kuma kinci kina jin karfi jikinki"

      "kayi hakuri,na bari"

   "bawai hakurin ba, bana san gaddama da karya da munafunci dan nasan mata kunkwari a wannan sigar"

   Jikinshi ya jawota yace"wadan nan abubuwan sunyi girma kamar balo"shafa su yayi sannan ya matsa,kara ta saki tace"akwai zafi wallahi"

   Kunnanta ya lalubu ya rada mata"anya ba raguwa bace ke kuwa?"

   "akwai zafi ne Ya Ali"

   "ki natso bansan shirme,kuma wallahi kada kiyi man kuka"

   Kai ta gyada tana maida kukan da ya tahu mata,saboda a tunanin ta za'a kara maimaita ta ranar farko,bakinshi ya hada da nata,sosai yake kaimata Sakunne ta ku wace gaba,bayan ya jegata ta iyakar iyawarshi,sannan ya lalubu kunnanta yace"kin shirya?"

   Kai ta gyada tace"Amman a hankali za'ayi"

   "babu zafi kamar wancan,amman fa sai kin jure dan taurin rai ne dani, ba lallai in iya kammalawa kusa ba,be strong"

     Kai ta daga,sarrafata ya dinga yi yanda yakesu ta dauka abin zai zuda tangarda amman sai ta samu akasin haka,babu laifi ta dinga maida mashi martani gwargwadan iyawarta,wata duniya suka fada mai ciki da nishadi da annashuwa,bayan kumai ya kammala ya jawota ya durata bisa kirjinshi yace"kin yi kukari na dauka raguwa ce ai"

   Kara narkiwa tayi saman kirjinshi sannan tace"ai baka kulani sai in kana da bukata,sannan kaki duguwar magana dani"

   Kamar bazai magana ba sai kuma yace"kin san dai ai duk kanku ba zabina bane,amman zanyi kukarin ganin na kwatanta adalci,kuma kulaki ya danganta daga yanda zaki ban kulawa,ki sani ina da yawan bukata,ban kuma san in nuna neki da bukata ni daya,ina san nema a dinga nema na"

MATAN ALI
(the most lovely story of nice family)
