     Yana bakin iya warshi wajan ganin bai nuna ma Azeema tsana ba,babu laifi yana kulawa da ita,kila duk tana samaun hakan ne saboda cikin dake jikinta,sannan gashi yana isar ta da bukatunshi,tunda yanzo babu na waje,sosai take juriya Akan shi.

      A bangaran Deji kuwa abubuwa sun fara tafiya dai dai saboda yanzo Ali yana binta da kallo,sannan yayi saukin dizge da hantara da kai mari da kwallo da mutum.

    Yau ma kamar kullum bayan ta Shiga daki ta zauna akan Gado sai tunanin abunda ya faru takeyi tana murmushi, wayar tace taji tana kara alamun kira dubawa tayi taga Aminatu ce,daga bangaran Aminatun tayi dariya tace"kaga matar yayana da fatan kin fara bamu kashi,in kuma fasaha ta kare sai ai man bayani"

    "in kuma bana so fa?"
   
   "zaki iya rubutaaramin littafi a kaina da halina da yanda zanyi daki"

     "a da kinan banda yanzo,duba ka gani jiya ba yau bace"

     Juye tayi sosai gashi kumai nata ya fito cikin style din dinkin da tela ya  dadi yana bata lokaci wajan ganin ya zauma jikinta das,

    Numfashin shi ke barazanar daukiwa,kallan cikin ido sukai ma juna,Sannan ya rabata gefanta yayi wucewarshi daboda ya gagara maida martani,amman yana jinjina yanda zasu fara samun kusanci da wannan yarinyar tunda takasa manta karansu na lokutan da suka wuce.

Kai ya dafi yana jin wani amai na tahu mashi,daman idan bacin rai yayi mashi yawa sai yaji yana san amayar da yan hanjin cikinshi,da sallama ta shigo dakin kusa dashi ta zauna ta dafa kafadar shi tace"Ya Ali maike damunka daga dawowarka haka?"

   Jajayan idanuwanshi ya dago ya watsa mata so,ba ita yake bukatar gani ba a yanzo,ita din dai wadda ta gama dasa mashi maganganu yanzo yake muradin kara gani kusa da shi ya gaya mata yana santa, zai iya rasa ranshi saboda ita,yayi dakon santa tun sanda ya fara gane cewa ita ma mutunci kamar sauran kuwacce mace mai ji da kanta da aji,wai bata san yana santa bane lallai dole tasan yana santa.

   Hannun Azeema ya janye daga dafawar da tai mashi yace"get out"

    Tsoro taji da taga ya dawo mata Alin da data sani mara tausayi,da sauri ta tashi ta nufi dakinta tana shiga ta fada saman gado ta saka kuka tana addua'ar Allah yaSa ba wancan Alin bane ya dawo data shiga ukku,yanda ta dauki rayuwarta da duniyarta gaba daya ta kuma cikin tashi yanzo ace zai wurguta aikuwa ya tasamma kasheta daranta.

    Ranta fes a karu na farko sunyi musayar yawo da Ali bai lakada mata na jakki ba,kinan ya fara shiga tarkon ta kinan,tsalle tayi sannan tace"yes sai kuma anjima zan kara zuwa maka da wani salon"

   Ta kwabi kayan jikinta ta fada toilet ta sakar ma kanta ruwa,bakinta ta dirza sosai sannan ta wanke duk wani lungu na jinkinta wanda ke tara kazanta,sannan ta daura towel ta fito,gaban madubi ta kuma sannan ta shafa mai masu kamshi ta dan shafa man lebe a saman bakinta,wata riga ta dauku daga cikin wadanda Ameenatu ta kawo mata,baki ta rike tace"ni kam bazan iya saka wannan rigar ba"

    Kara dibawa tayi sai taga wannan ce mai damar ma,sakawa tayi sannan ta haye gado ta dannan ma Ameenatu kira,suka sha hira sannan take fada mata yanda sukayi dazon da Ali,dariya suka sha sannan daga ban garan Ameenatu tace"kice Allah ya suke ba'a canza maki kamanne ba"

    "sai ma anjima idan naji kila In samu tsarabar mari ku kura,idan ya barni zanzo goben in bai bari ba kinga dole kizo ki"

   "dayake ce maki akai dan ban aure ba sai indinga fita babu izinin nawa mijin"

   "maida wukar mutuniya ta"

   Kitt ta kashi wayar bayan sunyi sallama ta dinga juye Saman gado tana jira karfi 9:00pm ta idasa taji wajan mijinta Ali.

    Juye yake saman gado ya rasa tudun dafawa, wannan yarinyar tana san karasa shi wallahi,so kawai yake ya ganshi tare da ita amman abun yaci tura,karar bude dakinshi yaji anyi da wata siririyar murya take sallama,amman yake amsawa cikin bed room dinshi ta shiga taga babu hasken fitela,sallama take kwadawa amman yana kallanta amman yake tanka ta.

   "ya Ali kana ina?"

    Bango ta fara bi tana neman makwanne,batayiaune ba sai taji ta buge wani abu.

   "wash kafana"

 Ji tayi an fizgota, ihu zata saka taji an tushi bakinta,jitayi ana tsotsar harshanta mutsu mutsu take tana dukan abinda ke lashi bakinta amman an ki barinta,zi tayi an sa'ko hannu cikin jikinta,hannu yake kaiwa ku ina duk inda rudewa ta kai Deji ta rude kuka take amman bai fita saboda bakinsu dake hadi dana juna,su take ta cizai amman rukun da yayi ma bakin bana wasa bane,ya bata wahala dai dai gwargwadan yanda yaga zai iya,ya ya mutsa ta yanda yaji ya gamsu,yana san ya wuce haka amman sai da Amincewarta,sakinta yayi yana maida numfashi wani kan wani,dakyar ya iya tashi ya kunna fitilar,dakin .

    "lafiya?mai ya kawoki nan dakin"
 
     Dagudu ta tashi ta rungumeshi tana kuka tace"dan Allah ka taimaka man wani ya kamani zai cire man baki, kuma yana ta taba mani nan"

    ta nuna kirjinta,kanta yakara mannawa da kirjinshi, amman ya gagara furta mata kumai, ajiyar zuciya take kawai,dariya yake san yayi mata amman baya san tasan cewa shine yayi mata haka,tunda bata san shidin bani a tafia haka.

    Tsaki yayi a ranshi yace _har yanzo yarinya ce ke_

MATAN ALI
(the most lovely story of nice family)

   BILKISU BILYAMIN.


   3 END.


    Sun dadi a haka yana rungume da ita tana maida ajiyar zuciya shikuma daman haka yakeso,kasanciwarta tare dashi a yanzo yafi mashi kumai dadi,lallai shi dan gatane a wajan Allah tunda abinda yake tunani yazai sada kanshi da ita,sai gashi tazo inda yake da kanta.

     "Kina iya tafiya dakin ki tunda kin gama abinda ya kawo ki"

   "Dan Allah to kayi hakuri ka kaini dakin saboda inajin tsoro wallahi"

   "Ai ba Nina kawo ki ba ko,bukatar kanki ce ta kawoki, kuma kikayi gamu da abinda kika zo nema"

    Duk bata fuskanci inda ya dusa ba ta dai kara rukun-kumeshi tace"Dan Allah ya Ali ka kaini dakina bazan iya wuce wannan katan falon ba ,zai iya kara biyune,ya illatani, yanzo ma bacin kazo da bansan iyakar abinda zai kara aika tawa a garine ba"

    Dariya yake san yayi amman kuma baisan ta raina shi.

     "dalla malama sakar man jiki mana"

    Dan sakinshi tayi amman ta rike hannunshi gam tace" wallahi na daina maka rashin kunya"

    "Ai ni daman Baki isa kiman rashin kunya ba yarinya,duk abinda kike kyaleki nake saboda bana san shiga shirgin yara irinki,saboda haka wuce dakin ki tun ban sa wanda ki neman tabaki ba ya kara kugwaigwai ta man ki"
    Kuka ta saka mashi,jin kukan yaki har cikin ranshi daga bisani yace"matsalar a aura maka kwaila kinan,tazo ta hanaka zaman lafiya"
    _Magan-ganunshi sun bata mata rai,amman bata da zabi sai abinda ya zabar mata a wannan lokacin, amman tana da kudurin sai ya maimaita fa kuda da yaran kuda, sannan zata fahimci abunda yake nufi,zai san kwaila_

    "muje in kaiki dakin kada ki daman a dakina,wai ma mai ya kawo ki ne"

    Banza tai dashi,saboda tana da haushin kwailar da yace mata"kurmace kinan?"

    "daman inasan inji gida gobe"

    "kauyan"

  "a'a Wajan Hajjata da Mena"

   "ina ta zama Hajjarki,ki ai gidanku na kauye,anzo birni anyi clean"

    Shiru tayi bata tanka shi ba step din suka fara hawa,yanayin makurewar da tai mashi suna tafiya yana ya'ba mata magana sai ka dauka wasu sabbin masoyane,Azeema ta fito neman abinda zata saka ma cikinta saboda ya addabeta da motsun kiran abinci, ta gansu a haka,wani malolon bakin ciki ya tukareta, _wai mai hakan ke nufi?ya saki jikinshi da ita ne har yana rakota dakinta, kuma suna makale da juna ga kuma yanayin kayan daki jikinta babu mutunci a garisu kumai ya bayyana_ rabasu tayi zata wuce yasa hannunshi na hagu wanda Deji bata kama ba ya tallafo kugun Azeema yace""mai ya fito daki waje yanzo?"

    Kai ta kauda ita a dole an mata laifi tace"abinci zanci"

    Cikin ya shafu yace"babyna kana ba momynka wahala ko,ta zama achichi"

   Sakin hannunshi Deji tayi zata wuce abunta saboda bazata dauki rainin hankalin ba,yasa hannu daya ya tallafo kugunta ita ma,sannan ya mannaa da gefin jikinshi.

    Yanayin Azeema da ya kuma kamar zata mutu dan bakin ciki da Deji ta gane sai ta kwantar da kanta saman kafadarshi sannan dukka hannuwanta biyu ta maidasu ta zagaye kugunshi.

     Iyakar kululuwar kishi Azeema ta kai,hannun Ali ta ture tayi gaba da katan cikinta wanda duk lokacin da ta haihu d'ane,kallan Deji Ali yayi yace"mai hakan ke nufi kikayi yanzo"

    "mai fa,ai kwaila bata iya kumai ba" 

    Dariya ya saka,sosai ta saki baki tana kallanshi wannan shine karu na farko da ta taba ganun dariyarshi mai kyau da ibganci,a iyakar zaman da sukayi bata tunanin tamasan yanda yanayin hakuranshi suke,baki yasa ya hura mata idanuwa yace"yan mata yadai"

    "a'a kwaila dai,ina ne ina yan mata"

Daure fuska yayi ya kawar da kansa ya kalli wani waje. "zancen banza zakara ya taka wuka. Mene rabarku da kwaila. Abu kamar na babe."  bude baki tayi zata yi magana sai kuma ta fasa. Zuciyarta na tafarfasa tana bugawa kamar guduma. Tayi gaba cikin dacin rai. 

Dariya yayi sannan yace "Ashe kalmar ta bata miki rai"

    Kai ta dan juya irin bata damun nan ba tace"ina fa ta bata min rai nida kirjin babu kumai ai na cahn cancanci kwaila"tana fadar haka ta Shige cikin dakin nata tabar shi nan tsaye,yana mamakin yanda ta kuye gaya magana kai tsayi,wai tama daina jin tsoranshi kinan.

     Washegari da yamma kasancewar daman tune Ali ya zaunar dasu ya gindaya masu yanda yake san a tafiyar mashi da gida,sannan ya shirya masu akan cewa kwana biyu biyu zasu dinga karbar girki,sannan bai amince wani kato ya dinga girki ba inba su dakansu dinba,wanki da sauran abubuwan da ya kamata sai masu aikin suyi amman bai aminci ma shigowa cikin iyakar inda matanshi suke rayuwa ba, _kishi ba'ayinka da wasa._
  
    To yau ma girkin na Deji ne, ta gama shirya komai sai ta,kuma daki ta dauki wankan mutunci,muggan kayan shan iskar cikin gidan da ta saka ma jikinta sun dauki hankalina ballantana kuma wanda akayi dominshi,sannan ta baza kalolin turarauka masu tada hankalin masoyi,hakan bai mata bama ta raba gashinta wanda ya dadi da tuhuwa gida ukku duk ta kamashi da bant masu kalar bunshort din da ta saka,sosai tayi masifar kyau da daukar hankali,sannan ta fito ta neme kujira ta harde,tana jiran shigowar gadanga usar yaki.

    Bata dadi da zaman ba sai ga Azeema ta fito itama da katon cikin ta dan wata tara tana cikin doguwar rigar ta irin ta masu ciki,wanda zuwa yanzo babu abinda take amfani dashi irin wadan nan rigunan saboda,bama ta iya saka kayan da ba duguwar rigar ba, zama tayi sannan tayi tsaki tana hararar Deji, ita kuwa deji ganin haka sai ta dauke kanta ma daga kallon gefen data ke kamar bata san Allah yayi halittarta ba a wajan,a zuciyarta tace _ban neman jaraba da fitinar mace mai ciki_

      Turo kofar yayi ahankali ya shigo hadi da sallama,amsawa sukayi gaba dayan su tukunna Deji ta tashi ta karbi jakar shi zata wuce,riko ta yayi ya hada da jikin shi sannan ya rada mata a kunne "baki iya tarbar mijinki ba kinan"

    Kasa kasa tayi da murya sannan tace"kwaila ai bata iya tarbar miji ba"

   Kugunta ya kara danka har sai da tayi wata yar kara,sannan  ya karasa gurin da Azeema take ya duka ya shafu cikin ta yace"ya baby na yake Yau,dafatan bai wahalar man da ki ba ko"

     Kai ta kauda saboda bata iya kallan wannan barikin ba,dauke kai tana hura hance sannan tace"gashi nan dai cikin ciki,wahala kuwa ai ina shan ta tunda bani samun kulawa"

    Tsayawa yayi kallon ta sannan yace"ban san kuma wace irin kulawaa zan bashi ba"

    Zafin kishi yasa tayi kukarin kawo mashi raini,bayan yana yi mata magana tai banza dashi saboda ya kama Deji zata mutu dan haushi,buga mata tsawa yayi yace"ina maki gargadi a karu nakarahi ki shiga taitayinki sannan kisan abinda kike binki yanda kikeso ba yana nufin zaki kawo man raini in kyaleki bane"

     Duk abinda ke faruwa Deji tanamakale dashi yake sakinta,itama ta kara narkewa cikin kirarran jikin Ali,wanda idan ka lafi har wata kasala ki saukar maka ta jin dadi da annashuwa,janta yayi suka wuce,baki ta yamutsa tace _jarabbbi kawai_inji Deji.
  
      Suna Shiga dakin ta ajiye masa jakar zata fito cikin fada yace"ke zunan, waye zai hada miki ruwan wankan,ku dan bakibsan darajar aure ba sai rashin kunya,da sa mutane a shiga ukku"

     Baki ta zumbura sannan ta fada toliet din dan hada masa ruwan wankan,ta zuba maahi kalolin mayukan wanka da duk wani abu da tasan zai bukata, tana gama hada masa ta juyo zata fita daga toliet din taga mutum a bakin kofa daga shi sai towel a jikinshi dauke kai tayi da sauri zata fita ya riko hannun ta ya dawo da ita yace" ke ban fa san isakanci,wannan bakin da kike turuwa fa?"

     Kara turashi tayi sannan tace"ai na gamahada ruwan wankan"

    "wankan kuma waye zai yiman ko haka ki kaga Zeema nayi sai ta tabbatar nayi kumai sannan zata barni amman da yake ke yar kauye ce deef"

     Jin ya ambaci Azeema sai taji wani malulu ya tsaya mata a rai a fusace ta tura shi cikin bahon wanka a ranta tace _yau zaka ga karuwance,da bariki an gaya maka Dejin da ka sani dace wannan,ta yanzo ba'a kawo mata raini a sha lallai_

     Baki ya bude ya tsaya kallon ta ganin yadda ta tura shi cikin abin wankan,dauke kanta tayi kamar batayi komai ba ta fara yi masa wankan sai data gama masa tas,bayan ta tabbatar ta kaiahi sama wajan bukatuwa da ita da gayya taje lakatu maahiwani wajan haka da gayya ta lashi cikinkunnaahi bayan ta fada maahi cewa akwai cumfa cikin kunnan,haka kan na shanunshita murza san rai a cewarta matattara dauda ce,duk wani waje da tasan idan kajiyarce shi namiji zai bada kai buri ya hau taji wajan da niyyar tana kwakulo dauda tunda wajan aji datti ne,sannan ta mika masa towel ya saka shi kuwa gogan ko magana ya kasa yi dan mamaki suna fita nan ma ta shafa masa mai hadi da,kara ya mutsa shi san ranta,shirya shi cikin kananun kayan tana gamawa ta jawu hannun shi,ai kuwa kamar walkiya haka ya fizgota ta fada jikinshi sannan ya lalubu kunnata yace"wallahi baki isa ki tada man hankali ba kice wai in fita waje ba tari da na rama dukkan abindakika yiman ba"

     "amman dai ai kasan matarka tanabukatar abinci akan lokaci"

    "wannan kuma damuwarki ce"
    Saman gado ya wullata sannan yabi ya mayi kumai ya faru na daga wasanne tsakanin mata da miji,duk sakonin ya kaisu inda yake da muradin kaiwa,sosai taji jiki saboda sosai ta kadashi kuma ya kadu,haka itama sai da ta gani shayi ruwa ne,sannan ya saurara mata,kalolin wasannin da yayi mata ta dadi tana jinsu a kuwane sassa na jikinta,luf tayi saman jikinshi kamar ba yanzo ta gama mashi rashin kunya ba,kunnanta ya lalubu sannan yace"gaskiya wannan kirjin ya wuce na kwaila,ya kai na yan mata ajin karshe"

    Kunya ce ta kamata ta kara jan bargo tarufe so,bakinshi ya kai saman nata ya cigaba da aika mata sakwanni masu wuyar bari,kunnashi ta lalubuita ma ta rada mashi.

    "zumana ya isa haka muje kada matarka tayi fushi da kai fa,dan bata san abinda zai rabika"

    Shafar fatar jikinta ya karayi yace"wai yaushi zan zama angon ne,kiban dama in nuna maki masayinki a wajena mana"

    "ba yanzo ba sai na kara girma yanzo ai kwaila ce"

    Kunnanta ya ciza ta saka kara,da sauri ya kara maseta cikin jikinshi yana bata hakuri,tashi yayi ya maida mata kayan jikinta sannan ya kama gaahinta ya raba gida biyu ya saka mata bant din,amman baiyi dai dai ba kamar yanda tayi shi farko,rungumarta ya karayi sannan yace"a taimaka yau dai in dan dandana wannan abun"

     ya shafu inda yake san kai sakonshi,tureahi tayi sannan ta kamahanyar da zata sada ta da falon da sauri ya cimmata ya sunkuceta kamar yar baby suka fito,Azeema tana nan inda suka barta sai dai ga alama zafi ya adabita ta cire rigarta daga ita sai wata yar karamar vest waddata fidso da ainahin shafi dincikinta,zaune take kasa dirahan tana shan mangoro,kallon banza ta watsa masu sannan ta dauki kanta.

    bisa kujira ya Aji Deji sannan ya kuma wajan Azeema ya sunkuya ya daukuta chak ita maya aji saman kujira,sannN ya sumbaci kuncinta.

    Yace"sorry baby mun bata maki lokaci ko?"

    Dadi taji a ranta kuba kumai dai bai wulakanta ta ba,saving dinshi Deji tayi sannan ta daga kai tace ma Azeema "mai zan zuba maki?"
   
    Baki ta yamutsa tace"barshi kawai zan zuba abinda nake bukata"
   
    Ali yace"ki ina naki abincin?"
   
    Tashi tayi daga kujirarta ta kuma sana cinyarahi ta zauna sannan ta fara yamutsa abincin tace"ga namu nan"
    
     Sai da ta tabbatar ya kushi sannan ta fara cin nata,suna gaddamar cin na karshi dai ya zuba a cikin kwarmin havebest din jikinta,gaba dYa Ali ya saka hannuwanshi biyu ya kama kuwanne na shanunta ya kafa baki yana cinye abincin,bayan ya gama ya fara lsarsu,zata kwace yace"akwai miya bari a lshi mki in kuma yayi maki zafi babu ruwana"

*MATAN ALI*
(the most lovely story of nice family)

   
   *BILKISU BILYAMINU*



*4 END*



       Sosai Azeema ta shiga tsananin damuwa da mamakin ganin yadda suke yi da Dije. Ta cika da mamaki da tunanin wai wannan shi ne Alin da ta sani wanda a baya yake masu wulakanci da tsananin riko da cusguna masu. Wai ko dai ita ce dama ta ke rudin kanta na cewa tana sonshi amma shi bai fara sonta ba. Lallai namiji sai a barshi. Namji kai aljani, namiji kwandon zawo.

     Ta cigaba da nanata maganar cikin ranta. Ta rasa abin da ke mata dadi a duniya. Ta ji kamar ta je ta shake su lokacin da suke rungume da juna suna aiwatar da abubuwan da ta jima tana mafarkin ya yi mata irinsa,amman bata samu kuda kusa da hakan bane. Wani sashi na zuciyarta kuma na raya mata cewa ta shiga ita ma a dama da ita irin yadda ta ga Ali na murza sassan girma a tare da Dije tana wani shisshidewa tana wata ‘yar murya kissa da jan hankalin namiji kuda dutse ne tana cewa,“I love you my dear.”

    Yace, “you are mine.” Yayi dariya kasa kasa yana mai cewa, “yan mata da gaski dai ba kwailar bace”

    Idanuwa ta juya,sannan ta watsasu, yamutsa fuska tayi tace, "a barni dai a mataki kwailar kawai,dan bana tunanin ina da abin mura.”

     Fuska yayi ya kakaro murmushi daidai lokacin da ya cika hannunsa da dangin fulaninta ya ce, _ja’ira kema dai kina so ne. In kuma kin yi musu na rabu da ke na je ga wata daga cikinku_yana ayyanawa a cikin zuciyarshi,a zahiri kuma yace"Allah na tuba da kiran wannan kalma"

    Murmushi tayi sannan ta sake shafar kumatunsa,ta rungumi kwibinsa tana mai ajiyar zuciya ta shafa gadon bayansa. A daidai lokacin ya ji kamar ana masa tsikari domin kuzarinsa ya sake da wowa, nishadinsa ya zaburar da shi da madubin biyan bukatarsa ya ji roko na musamman take mashi abubuwan suna kai mashi yanda yake bukata,lallai zai zama mijin hajiya idan wannan tafiya haka za'a dinga yinta,duk wani jan idanshi zai iya zama tarihin da ya riga ya shude.

    Ya sake dora lebbanshi akan nata yana murza matsikaranta yayin da harshensa ke shiga nata da wani irin salo mai wuyar bayyanawa,duk wani kukarinshi na ganin bai shagaltu ba amman ina! abubuwan bana wasa bani take aika mashi,Babu jimawa suka mance a irin duniyar da suke ciki suka lula zuwa wata mai cike da abubuwan al’ajabi da mantar da zuciyar dake cikin kunci sai nishadi,da kwanciyar hankali sosai suka lula duniyar da su kadau suka san abinda ki cikinta.

    Tsakin da Azeema tayi maikara shi ya ankara dasu daga duniyarsu ta masoya,peck Deji ta sakar mashi a kunci sannan ta tashi daga saman cinyarshi ta kama hanyata hayi saman steps din,gaba daya kada ku'ina na jikinta take,binta Ali yayi da kallo yana kissima yanda za'ayi artabu duk ranar da ya kama wancan kayan,basar wa yayi sannan ya dan dubi inda Azeema take yace"ke mayi matsalarki wai?"

    "ban sani ba nima"

    Dariya yayi ya tashi daga inda yake ya kuma kusa da ita ya kwantar da kanshi saman cikin yace"babyna ya dai?da fatan kana lafiya baka wahalar man da uwargida na"
   
    Tureshi tayi sannan ta taba baki tace"ai fa sai uwargida tunda daman an samu an lika man wannan cikin da yake hana azu kusa dani ai dole ace man haka"

    Fuska ya tsuke yace"ban kuma san hauka,kin sani"

    Baki ta zumbura tana kunkuni,sannan ya kara kamuta yana shafa cikin yace"cikin nawa ne kaya a wajanki?, to ban ma kara kusantarki ballan tana inhadaki da kaya"

   Kara turashi tayi tace "da dai yafi, kada ka karan mana,yanzo ka gama soyayya da waccan yarinyar amman ku kusa da abinda kake mata baka taba man ba"

   Tashi tayi tana goge kwallar dake fito mata daga idanuwanta,dakyar take haye saman binan sabida nauyin da cikinta yayi,sosai ta bashi tausayi da sauri ya cimmata ya daga ta chak,kuka ta saka mashi tana bugunshi,tana ya ajeta babu ruwanshi da ita tunda baya sonta,ku ta ita baibi ba ya dinga haura binan da ita,dakinta ya direta saman gado sannan ya haye shima,lalubarta ya farayi,kuka ta kara saka mashi tace"wallahi bazan yadda ba,sai in kana san wani abu sai ka tahu waje na"

   "dan na isa ne,kuma dan ina da iko"

    Bakinshi ya hade da nata, yake bata damar da zatayi kurafin da take niyyar yin,sosai yaki bata sakon da dakyar take amsarshi,saboda tayi nauyi sosai,sai daya gama jigata ta,sannan yace"kin shirya amsar dose dinki?"

   "wallahi ban shirya ba,ban ma su ni"

   "wannan kinsan dolane, kudan dan daki cikin cikinki nawa"

    Kuka ta kara saka mashi tace"ai daman ba ni kake so ba,dan naka kake so shiyasa kake nuna ma waccan yar kauyan soyayya amman ni ka barni sakara"

    "ashe matsalarki ba zata kare ba,idan har bazaki daina kurafi ba,kinga ita bata taba maganarki ba"

    "ai daman tunda ita ana nuna ana santa"

    "to yanzo ki shirya zan ba dana magani,in yasu sai ki cigaba da magan ganunki marasa tushi ballantana madafa"

    "nifa babu wani shiri da zanyi wallahi"

    "to bari kiji wallahi sai nayi"

    Kuka ta kara sakawa tana bugunshi tana tureshi,abubuwan da ya fahimci tana so su ya fara aika mata dasu,luf kake jin malama Azeema sai kukan matan da suka san kan duniya take kasa kasa,ita a dole an mata dole, an takura ta,sai da kumai ya kammala sannan Ali ya kara rungumarta ya lalibu kunnata yace"kina da babban matsayi a wajena,Zeema bana san kurafi idan kika kiyaye zaki saman da saukin kai"

    Lafewa tayi a jikshi amman kuma bata tankashi ba,sanin ba zata tanka ba yasa ya cigaba da fadin"kina da matsayin da Ita khadeejar bata da shi a wajena"

    Cikinta ya shafu,sannan ya manna mata kiss a kumatu yace"zaki fara kawo man farin ciki a rayuwata,da dukkan zuri'armu,kinga zamuyi alfahari daki kinan"

   Gyara mata kwanciyar yayi yanda cikin bazai dameta ba, sannan ya cigaba da cewa"har ila yau kice macen da na kusanta sama da sau goma a rayuwata, kaf gaba daya,kinga shima wani abun alfaharin ne a wajanki"

    Gashinta wanda ya tarwatse ya maida baya ya gyara mata yace"kina jina ku har yanzo fushin ne?"

    Shiru yaji, dubawa yayi yaga baccinta take hankalinta kwance,kwanciyar ya kara gyara mata,yana shafata yana lallabata.

    Kacukan ya dauki tunaninshi ya kuma duniyarsu shida Deji,da yanda zai sha rumanta, yanzo yadan rage hanya ne,a wajan Azeema amman ji yake kamar bai amfana da kumai ba,saboda inba haka ba yasan zai iya aikata ba dai dai ba,amman yana san ya kusanci Deji saboda itace abinda ke matsalarshi yanzo,tana cikin hankalinta da kuma aminciwarta,zai fisamun gamsuwa da natsuwar da yake muradin samu,dari bisa dari.

      Wayar take juyawa tana san ta kirashi tana kuma tsoran zubda ajinta,kasada tayi ta dialing number daga can bangaran aka dauka akayi shiru.

   Cikin sarkiwar murya tace"kayi man shiru ina magana"

   "mai kike san in fada bayan wanda na fadan"

   Baki ta turo sanna tace"tu kayi hakuri,raina ne ya baci na fada haka"

    "eh kin kyauta tunda zuwa yanzo baki san yanda zaki fadi magana mai dadi ga mutane ba"

    "nidai kayi hakuri mana"

   "daman dan kiban hakuri keka nemi ki zagan"

   Kuka Ameenatu ta saka tace"please mana ya Ommar,bazan iya jure fushinka ba,Allah na tuba"

   Har cikin ranshi yake jin kukanta na shiga,kamar bazai magana ba sannan yace"ya ishike haka zubda hawayan,idan na huta zuwa yamma zan shigo inga babyna"

    Dariya ta saka tace"kayi hakuri kinan?"

    "kinsan bana fushi daki,ai"

    "tnx mijin Ameenatu"

    Soyayyarsu suka ci gaba da aika ma juna mai tsafta da tsantsar kulawa da juna,soyayyarsu abin burgewa ce da kuyi ga sauran masoyan da basu san mai ake nufida kulawa ba, da nuna soyayya ga abokan tarayyarsu.

     Gaba dayansu ya tara suna zaune sun saka shi tsakiya gyaran murya yayi yace"Zeema daki zan fara,cikinki yayi girma kin shiga watan haihu,kina da zabin abinda yayi ma rayuwarki,zaki barma wannan girkin ki saboda ki samu sauki,amman maye ra'ayinki?"

    "lafiyata kalau zan iya dukkan abinda na saba yi"

    Ba dan ranshi yasu ba yace"good haka ake so"

    "saboda haka,an canza kwanakin daga kwana biyu zuwa kwana dai dai,kuwacce zata dinga kwana daya daya da mijinta"

    Ya nuna Azeema yace"saboda kidan samu saukin aikin,tunda kinci zaki iya"

   Wani mugun kallo Deji ta watsa mashi sannan ta dauke kai tace"ai da kabar kwana bebiyun tunda banga amfanin daya dayan ba,in ma da hali a maida wata wata"

   Fuska ya daure yace" Lallai baki da kunya dan kunga na fara sake maku shine har kuke mani magana yanda kuka ga dama,tu wallahi kada wadda ta nemi kawo man raini ku tashi kuban waje sakarkaru kawai,ku zaku tsara man yanda zanyi ku ni zan tsara?"


*_Nagarta Writters Association_*



*MATAN ALI*
_(the most lovely story of nice family)_


*BILKISU BILYAMINU*



 *_Godiya mai dinbin yawa ga dukkanin masoyan littafin MATAN ALI,naji dad'in yanda kuka nuna Kauna a garene da soyayya Allah ya biyaku da mafificin alkairinsa,wannan ne shafin karshe,sai mun hadu a littafi nagaba  MIJINA HASKEN RAYUWATA_*



       *5 END*


    Kai ya dafe yana tunanin sufa mata duk yanda zakai masu sai sun kawo maka raini da izgilanci, musamman idan suka ga abinda kake takama dashi,to ne kham bazan yadda da raini ba, _a zuciyata naci ina sauran raini tunda kuma anga idi zundir._

    Dakinshi ya kuma ya dauki keys dinshi ya nufi gidan Hajjarshi kwana daya baije ya duba ta ba,sai yake jin duk bai kyauta ba,parking space ya nufa ya fada motarshi ya danna horn mai gadi ya bude nashi ya nufi family house dinsu.

   A falo ya iske su gaba dayansu Mahmud,Ameenatu,Hajja suna fira,da sallama ya idasa shiga falon, ya samu kujerar dake kusa da Hajja ya zauna yace"Hajjana barka da yamma"

   "sannu dai Babana"

    "yawwa,nayi missing dinki fa"

    "ai naga alama tunda gaka tunda sassafi"

    Kai ya shafa yace"kaina na dan cewo ne sai nasha magane,nadan kwanta"

    Karaf Ameenatu tayi tace"Allah Hajja ya Ali tunda ya samu ya kura wadannan kadangarun barikin hankalinshi ya kwanta yake mana asha ruwan tsuntsaye a gidan nan"

    Harara ya watsa mata,kai ta dauke tana dariya ciki ciki,Mahmud ne yace"big broda ai wallai kasan kai aure,gaba daya matanka na bugawa a duniya ne,suna da kyau,wannan yar kauyan ma naga yanzo ta wani waye kamar yar shugaban kasa,ni wallahi ranar nan ban gane ta ba,sai da na tsaya nayi mata kallan kurilla"

    Tsaki Ali yayi ya kara tamke fuska yace"Ke tashi ki kawo man ruwa mai sanyi,kuda baku iya kumai ba sai surutu da fadi ba'a tambayi kuba"

   Ganin babu wasa yasa ta tashi da sauri ta nufi kicin,shi kuma Mahmud ankara yayi da katubarar da yayi wajan furta kallan kurilla yasa ya dan sosa kiya yace"Hajja bari in dan fita gari"

   "a dawo lafiya, amman kada ka kuskura ka dadi"

   "to bazan dadi ba,Ya Ali na barka lafiya"

   Banza yayi dashi bai tankashi ba,Hajja tace"yanzu daga zuwanka ka tarwatsa man yara muna fira"

    Dariya yayi ya tashi daga kujirar da yake ya kuma bisa tata ya dura kanshi saman kafadarta yace"ai sai kiyi firar dani ai"

   "kai din da kake miskili mutum in zai karace surutunshi baka wani responding mashi sosai"

   Dariya ya karayi yace"to yi hakuri ina jinki,zanyi sosai"

    Ita ma dariyar tayi ta shafa kanshi tace"ya zamanka da iyalin naka yanzo,da fatan dai a wajan ka an samu cigaba"

    Kafada ya daga yace"not much dai"

   "ban gani not much dai ba,kana nufin har yanzo jiya e'yau"

   "no,an samu cigaba kasu 70 daga cikin 100"

   Duka ta kaimashi tace"ansamu salama zakace, bawai not much ba,kuma nasan daga wajanka ne Allah"

   "Babu ruwana Hajja wannan diyar take yar kauye ce da matsalar"

   "bana tsammanin Khadeeja tana da matsala gaskiya, yarinya mai hakali da tunani ni kham ban yadda ba"

    "ai na dadi da sanin kinfi san yaran nan dani yanzo"

    "eh din,aikai nake ma kara Babana"

    Ommar ne yayi sallama ya shigo ya zauna kujirar da take fuskantar. wadda suke zaune,ya gaida Hajja sannan yace"zuwa yanzo wannan mutmin bai girma ba Hajja?"

    "ai kasan shi baya girma,Auta bata wannan san jikin nashi wallahi"

    "kuma gashi duk sanda lokaci ya bada kin zama kaka ba"

   "ka bari kawai Ommaru,ai nagani idan Azeemar ta haihu jikana zan dauka ku shi"

   Gaba daya suka saka dariya, tashi  tayi tace"ne kham na tafe nabarku da sheriritar ku"

   Ommar ya kalleshi yace"wai kai yanzo wuyar gani ma kake,man"

    "to mai yafi raina gani da kayan hutu a gidana"

    "matsala ta da kai dan iska ne kai wallahi"

    "tu uban me yasa kake mani shirshige kana saka man ido,kake gane bana gano"

    Dariya sosai Ommar yayi yace"au fa ba saban ba,kuda yake na manta oga ne kai da kanka wajan harkar mata"

      Sheguwar Ameenatu da cup da plate shi ya katse masu firar da suke,wajan Ommar ta kai plate din ta aje,tashi Ali yayi ya kai mata rankwashe yace"munafuka tun dazo nace ki kawo man ruwa, amman kika ki fitu wa,sai da wannan yazo shine kika fito kika kawo mashi"

    "wallahi snacks din na tsaya daukuwa cikin friage"

    "shine ya daukie ki minti dubu ko,zan kamaki yarinya"

    Dariya Ommar kawai yake yace"wallahi idan ka kara taba mani zuciyata,sai na maka wulakanci,kai baka san miji yafi wa ba"

    Tafiyarshi yayi dakin Hajja yace"ai fa naga alama tunda an nuna man zan kamaku dukkan ku ai"

    Shiga yayi dakin Hajja yace"mother ni zan tafi inda nafi wayau"

   "Allah ya bamu alkairinsa, Babana ka gaishe mana da yaran nawa,sannan kuma kana nan kana ma Azeema shirin dawowa nan gidanku da zarar ta haihu anyi suna zan taho da ita in tabbatar ta samu ingantaccin wanka mai kyau"

   Baki da damuwa Hajjata, duk abinda kika fada shine hukunci mai kyau"

    "to Allah yayi albarka"

    "ameen ngd"

       Bayan ya fito ya iske Ameenatu da Ommar a falo, hanyar fita yayi sanan ya dan tsaya yace"kee Meena ki samene mota ina jirank"

   Ommar yace"abokina babu sallama kuma,wallahi kada ka taba man mata dan zanyi kararka"

   Murmushi Ali yayi ya wuce ba tari da yace kumai ba,bayan ta sameshi a cikin mota,sai da ya bata fuska sosai yanda zata tabbatar da duk abinda ya furta da gaske ne, kuma hakan yake nufi sannan yace"wannan yarinyar ta gidana wadda naga kuna kuskus tare,ina ganin zan kyaleta saboda har yanzu akwai rawar kai a tare da ita"

    Gabanta ne ya fadi ta zaro ido waje,dan satar kallanta yayi yaga halin da ta shiga murna yayi a cikin ranshi saboda sakonshi zai ji dai dai yanda yake so yace"kinsan bana santa right?"

    Kai ta daga,sannan ya cigaba"kuda ya bata san yanda zata zauna dani ba,ita ma Zeema da nake bata kulawa dan tasanyanda zata mallaki zuciyata ne,bazan maki karya ba tanada kaso mafitsuka a cikin zuciyata,har yanzo maganar da nake maki babu kamar ita,duk fa wadannan abubuwan ina fada maki sune saboda kice abokiyar sirrina"

     Kuncinta ya shafa yana murmushi"zan fita sabgarta, zan kyaleta kafin muga yanda hali zaiyi"

   Sosai ya hango ta shin hankali a fuskarta,basarwa yayi yace"amman ke maye shawararki baby Meena"

    Wani mugun numfashi ta sauke sannan tace"ka yiwa Allah Ya Ali ka bata last chance zata gyara,kuma tace tana tsoranka ne fa"

    "karya take maki,bata tsorona tunda tana iya man rashin kunya"

    "kaban zuwa gobe kumai zai dai daita"

    "wallahi yayi man kadan sai dai daga yanzu zuwa shabiyun dari duk ina jiranki"

    "na shiga ukku yaya ka bari mana"

    Rankwashe ya kai mata yace"wai dan na kureta kanwar uwarki ce ne"

    "jinta nake kamar kai wallahi,idan kayi mata wani abu yaya anya zan iya yafe maka kuwa?"

    "sai ki aika dani inda bazan dawo ba"

    Dariya sukayi gaba daya yace"ya rage naku"

     Fita tayi yaja motarshi yana murna,dan yau yakai geji duk tsiyarta sai ya kama eftor ya kashe boss.

     Bayan ta kuma wajan Ommar suka kara fira sannan ya tafi,daki ta fada  ta dauku wayarta ta danna ma Deji kira,bugu biyu ta dauka Ameenatu bata bari tayi magana ba tace"wallahi Khadeeja kin dai yi asara,yau yaya Ali yazo yace kima zaki kama gabanki"

    A guje ta fito daga bayi ta fada saman gado tace"dan Allah kada kisa inkamu da ciwan zuciya, wallahi wannan ba wasa bane"

    "uban wa ke maki wasa,wallahi ki kika sani,sai kaci ki daya aka taba ma aure a duniya, ki ba da auran aka same ki ba"

    "wallahi Mena yana kumai dani, kullum sai ya samu sanadin da zai yamutsani san rai"

     "to wallahi ya dai rage nake babu ruwana"ta she wayarta,gaba daya

    Kuka Deji ta fara tana tunanin yanda Ali zai sadu da ita,tayi imani kasheta zaiyi da ranta,safa da marwa take daga bisani ta wawuru wayarta ta kara kiran Ameenatu,kin dauka tayi ta gama ringing dinta ta katse,wei wei take kuka ta tabbatar kashinta ya bushe yau a fili ta furta"zan yarda da shi idan na mutu nayi shahada"

    Bayan ya dawo ya zauna falo ya dinga juya wayarshi baida number dinta ,Ameenatu ya kira yace ta tura mashi number Deji,bayan ta turu mashi yayi dialing ya kira bayan ta dauka yace"sauku kasa ina neman ki" 

    ditttttt ya kashe yakira Azeema itama yace ta sauku kasa yana kiranta,a tare suka fito daga daki da kaga Deji kasan babu lafiya,hakan da ya gani yasan Ameenatu tayi magana da ita kwata-kwata yau babu kumai fuskarta na kyale kylaensu na mata, ga zumbulelen hijabin data saka wanda zai iya kadata,waje suka samu suka zauna,kallansu yayi na sakan goma ,yaga kyawawan matanshi sosai yaji suna shiga zuciyarshi,fuska ya tamke sannan yace"daga yanzo maganar nan da nake maku duk mai girki zata dinga kwana dakina"

    Tashi yayi bai kallesu ba ya nufi dakinshi yana dariya ciki ciki saboda yasan Deji haukane kadai ya rage tayi,kuka ta saka ta tashi ta haura sama,ita kuwa Azeema dadi taji ku babu kumai dai ta dinga kwana tana kallanshi, kuma a muhalli daya. 

    Sanin Dejin kida girki shiyasa ta shige dakinta take zuwa daining table wajan cin abinci,saboda bama ta san ganinshi,sunci abincinsu hankali kwance shida Azeema ba tare da ya nemi inda Deji take ba,hakan kuwa yayi ma Azeema dadi,daga ita sai gwarzon mijinta suna ci suna fira sama sama,amman hankalin Ali yana ga kufar Deji da inda zata fito.

    Chak ya daga Azeema yace"muje ki kwanta nima baccin nake ji"

     Daki ya kaita sai da ya dan lallatsata sannan ya rufe mata kufa,dakin Deji ya taba yaji ta rufe dariya yayi yace"kumin wayanki yarinya sai ansha manki"

    Sai da ya shiga daki sannan ya daga waya,ya kirata bayan ta daga cikin shirshikar kuka ta ce"Assalamu Alaikum"

    "kinsan dai ki keda aiki saboda haka,ina da bukatu da yawa ina jiranki,kuma wallahi kika bari na kara kiranki abin bazai maki kyau ba,dan bansan iskanci"

    Kashi wayarshi yayi sannan ya fada saman gado,yana jiran zuwanta.

   Waya ta kara yima Ameenatu,tayi sa'a ta daga cikin kuka tace"Meena Ya Ali fa yayi man tanajin mugunta,ina jin tsoro"

   "yi zamanki kada kije,sai ki shirya kumawa gida tunda haka kika zaba,tunda ki kadai namiji zai taba fasawa,dan Allah idan kin isa kada kije "

    Wayarta ta kashe,bata da wani zabi sai nayin abinda yake so,duk ma yanda zaiyi da ita sai dai yayi,tashi tayi ta shiga bayi tadan sakar ma kanta ruwa,ta fito tana tsane jikinta,ta shafa mayukan da ta saba shafawa,sannan ta dauku wata riga mai dugon wando ta saka,ta dauki hijabi kato ta saka,sannan ta rufe dakinta ta nufi turakar mai gida Ali.

    Da sallama ta shiga bata sameshi falon ba, zama tayi daya daga cikin kujeron dakin, tsawan minti biyar shiru yake jinta bayan kuma yasan ta shigo wayarta ce ta fara ringin tana dubawa taga shine,kan gawa tayi yace"malama ina jiran kifa"

   Dakyar ta iya daga kafa ta sada kanta da bedroom din,saman gado ta iske shi yayi rub da ciki,kamar bai san ta shigo ba sai datayi minti wajan ukku tsaye sannan yace"inasan yin wanka"

    Haka da hijab ta shiga bayi ta hada mashi ruwan wankan,dariya yake sanyi amman ya faske,shiga yayi ya gama ya dawo ya kalleta yace"bani key din dakin ki"

    Mika mashi tayi ya amsa ya  fita,dakinta ya shiga ya bude wdrope dinta cikin kayan baccinta ya duba mafi lalacewar rigar ciki ya dauku,sannan ya dawo,wurga mata ita yayi yace"cikin wannan nake san ganinki,kuma idan kika ce kala wallahi billahi sai kin raina kanki"

    Ai babu musu ta amsa ta shiga bayi,ta rufe ta saka taga ai bata da maraba da tsirara,kuka ta saka tana Allah ya taimake ta kada Ali ya kasheta,shiru bata fito ba har tsawan minti goma bakin kofar yaji ya taba yaji rufe yace"ku bazaki fito bane?"

    Babu laka jikinta haka taje jan kafa ta bude,kusa shidewa yayi ganin surar Deji cikin rigar,Amman da yake yasan takan duniya sai ya fauze kamar bata gabanshi yace"zauna nan magana zanyi daki"

    Dan das kwanawa tayi yace"zauna sosai malama,ai babu dole a zaman aure"

    Fuska ya tamke yace"ina neman hakina yau wajanki zan samu ku bazan samu ba?"

     Babban tashin hankali ne ya ziyarci gaba daya sassan jikin Deji, daga inda yake yana jin yanda katifar ke rawa"am ehmm dan Allah ku zakai hakuri in kara shiryawa"

   "ba zanyi ba,sannan cikin biyu sai dai ki zabi daya,ku dai kiban hakkina ku kuma wallahi babu ruwana dake,ku kallo baki ishine ba,kuma kinsan Allah babu wanda zai man dole zama dake"

    Mugun kallo ya watsa mata sannan yace"tashi ki ban waje"

    Kwanciya yayi yayi rub da ciki yanajin iska na yawo dashi a sararin samaniyar bukatuwa da kasancewa tare da wannan yarinyar amman tana neman ta bata mashi rai,tsawa ya daka mata yace"nace ki fita"

    A hankali ta matsa kusa dashi ta shafa kanshi cikin tsoro tace"na shirya"

   Kamar ba zai amsa ba sanna kuma yace"kada ki bataman rai khadeeja nace ki fita bana so"

   "wallahi na shirya"

   Hannunta ya jawo ya dura saman kirjinshi yace"ina fama da tsananin bukatuwa, zuciyata tana cikin tsananin kasancewa dake,ki taimaka man,nakai matsayin da bana iya hassala kumai har sai na cimma burina a kanki,kisan me nafi bukata a yanzo"?

   Kai ta girgiza hawaye na zuba a idonta yace"ke nake so Khadeeja, ke nake bukata,taimaka mani kada inkai wani matakin"

   Kukan daya tahu mata ta hadeyi tace"na shirya kuma da zuciya daya da Aminciwata,nasan zan mutu yar aljanna"

      Jawota yayi jikinshi gaba daya jikinsu ya hadu dana juna wani mugun shok ya ziyarci jikin Ali wanda bai taba dan danshi ba a dukkan sauran matan da yasan jikinshi ya hadu da naso.

    Da kyar suka gabatar da sallah raka'a biyu,lap dinkusa da gadan ya kashe, duhu ya mamaye dakin sannan ya fara aikawa da sakonne,sosai ya aika mata da sakonne duk inda yake san kaiwa, daga bisani kuma ya lalubi shiga birnin amman abu yaci tura,baida wani zabi sai shiga ta dole,wani uban ihu ta saki wanda  gaba daya dakin sai da ya amsa, da sauri ya hada bakinshi da nata,ma ban-banta duniyuyi suka fada,shi Ali ya fada wata duniya mai ciki da ni'imomi da farin ciki,ita kuma Deji ta fada duniyar wahala da azaba,duka da ya kushi babu wanda batayi ba amman abun yaci tura,dole ta raketu hakuri da juriya ta yafa ma kanta,cikakkun awanne ukku yayi bai saurara mata ba,iya jigata ta jigata bata da halin yin kuka saboda bakinshi bai bata damar yin hakan ba,da kyar ya samu ya dawo cikin natsuwarshi badan ya gamsu ba sai dan ya bata dama ta dan huta,minti talatin dinda ya bata ta huta,har bacci ya kwashe ta taji ana neman a kara gana mata wata azabar,da sauri ta bude idanta bata wani yinkuri ba aka kuma fagen daga cikakkun awanni biyu ya bata sannan ya samu cikakkiyar gamsuwarda bai taba jin makamanciyarta ba,kuda ya nemi yaran mutane bai ji cikakkiyr gamsuwa wajan kuwa ba kamar na yau,ji yake kamar yau ya taba sanin mace,shi kanshi yaji jiki,kamar saban shiga,daga kumai na jikinta baya aiki idan ka kalle idanta dake juyawa yana fidda ruwan hawaye,shi kanshi yasan yayi aika aika,wutar dakin ya kunna kai ya dafi da ya ga muguwar barnar da yayi,a hankali hawaye ki zubu mashi daga idaniyarshi kai ya dafi yace"na shiga ukku na illata yarinyar mutane"

   Da sauri ya tashi,amman ya kuma ya zauna sakamakon jin da yayi kanshi na juya wa,sai da ya dan bada tazarar wajan minti biyar sanan yaji dama, tashi yayi ya shiga toilet ya kintsa kanshi sannan ya fito inda take ya isa ya dagota ya hadata da jikinshi yana hawaye yama rasa ta inda zai taimaka mata,kara kwantar da ita yayi sauri ya shiga bayi ya hada ruwa mai zafi ya zuba dettol sannan ya fito ya dauketa ya shiga da ita bayin ya saka ta,wata kara ta saki amman bata da energy din da zata iya hana kanta shiga ruwan,sai da ya tabbatar ta gasu sannan ya fiddata ya canza mata wani,ya kara sakata sannan ya barta ya kuma ya tsaya yana kallan iyakar a dadin jinin dake bisa bedshet din,ya cire ya canza wani sannan ya kuma ya dauku ta,ya goge mata jiki sannan ya saka mata jallabiyarshi fara kal,rungumarta ya karayi a hankali yace"sannu dan Allah kiyafi man wannan abin da nai maki"

    Hannunta ta kai saman fuskarshi tana goge mashi hawayan da yake zuba daga idanuwanshi,cikin dasashiyar murya tace"please don't say dat"

    Mannata ya karayi da jikinshi,sannan ya bude wata drower ya dauku magunguna ya bata tasha, sanna ya kwantar da ita,sallah ya gabatar raka biyu na yima Allah godiya da ya mallaka mashi abinda ya dadi yana muradi mace mai juriya wadda zata iya da bukatunshi,sannan kuma ya kara da gide mashi akan duk abinda yasa a gaba yana ganin alfanun shi,dawowa yayi ya kwanta ya mannata da jikinshi,jin jikinta yake kamar wuta saboda zazzabi,rufesu yayi suka kuma bacci,sama sama yake jin ana buga mashi daki da karfin gaske,da sauri ya bude ido yaji yanda ake bugun kofar abun babu sauki,tsoro ma yake kada ta farka cikin hikima ya kwantar da ita ya nufi bakin kofar dakin, cikin fada ya bude yace"wani sakarai......"

  Gaba daya ta fado jikinshi da kyar ta iya bude baki tace"zan mutu"

    Hankalinshi tashe ya sungumeta ya fita,ana ta kiraye kirayen sallar asubah,Audidi driver ya dinga kwala ma kira,a guje ya fito riga a hannu yace"oga lafiya?"

   "shiga mota muje"

   Da sauri ya fada mota ya tada,shi kuma Ali ya tura Azeema, sanna ya shiga horn a kai ma maigadi da sauri ya bude yana mamakin abinda zai fidda masu gidan a dai dai wannan lokacin.

  "ina zamuje oga"Audidi driver ya tambaya.

      "Muje NAGARTA HOSPITAL, yi sauri Audidi,tana cikin condition mai muni"

      Inalillahi wa inna ilaihir rajiun, kawai yake mai maitawa,mintina kalilan suka sada su da asibitin,da sauri ya kara daukarta suka nufi cikin ward da sauri wasu nurses suka dauku gado aka durata,aka nufi dakin haihu da ita,binsu yake sai da aka zu shiga da ita sai Dr ya rike mashi hannu yace"addu'arka take bukata abokina"

   Baya ya kuma ya jingina da bango yana share gomen da ya jika mashi gaban goshi,ga kushi ga kwanan yunwa.

   Safa da marwa kawai yake yana dakon abinda zaiji ance,game da matarshi,kyar-kyawar awa guda aka dauka, sannan dan tsamur murin likitan ya fito ya kama kafadar Ali yace"realaz,muje office"

   "Dr a wani stage take please?"

    "muje office nace,sai muyi maganar a can"

    Hannunshi ya kama suka nufi office wanda na daga kaina naga an rubuta *DR B. MAHUTA*

    Kujera ya jama Ali ya zaunar da shi yace"kwantar da hankakinka mun samu ta dawo cikin hankalinta sai dai kuma kamar ba zata iya haihuwa da kanta ba,sai mun mata aiki"

    "aiki Dr?"

 "shiyasa ma nake san ka saka hannu a nan domin mu shiga da ita,sannan kuma ta dadi tana labour har ta jigata,ya akai ka barta ita kadai"

   "Dr idan aikin shine mafi sauki a gareta bani takardar in saka hannu"

    Dr ya mika ma Ali takarda ya saka hannu,sannan yace "zamu yi mata aiki insha Allahu yanzo"

   Dr ya tashi,Ali ya mara mashi baya,dakin tiyatar zai shiga Ali ya bishi yace"Dr matata da abinda ke cikinta"

   "suna karkashin kulawar Allah,mu taimaku ne namu,kayi mana addu'ar samun nasara"

     Hannunshi Ali ya sake yana shari hawayen da suka zubu mashi,kai ya dafi yana karanta duk wata addua'a da tazo mashi a baki,aljihunshi ya laluba yaji babu waya,waje ya fita ya same Audidi driver yace"kazo da waya?"

    "eh gata"ya mika ma Ali wayar dialing yayi ya kira number Ommar bugu ukku ya dauka cikin bacci yace"waye?"

   "Zeema zata haihu muna asibity"dit ya kashi wayan,ya lalubu number Alhaji ya danna mashi kira daga daya bangaran ya dauka yace"wake magana?"

   "barka da asuba"

   "lafiya naji muryarka haka?"

    "muna asibity zata haihu" daga haka ya katse wayar ya maida ma Audidi waya ba tare da ya fada asibitin da suke ba,cikin asibitin ya kuma ya zauna yana tunanin yau wace irin jarafta take d'awainiya dashi haka,ga wata can ya gana mata azaba a daran jiya ya farka masu diya,ga kuma mai ciki anzo asibiti anci sai an yanka ta,gaba daya kumai yayi mashi tsaye.
   
    Cikin minti kadan kuwane famil suka Hallara asibitin, hankalin Hajja ta shi ta isa inda taga Ali ya rakube ta dafa  shi tace"Babana tana ina?"

    "tana dakin teater"

    "yi mata addua'a kaji Babana"

    Hannunta ya kama yace"Hajja bana cikin natsuwa kumai ya dagule man"

    "kaita mai maita inalillahi"

    Inalillahi yake ta mai-maitawa,babu adadi.

    Ommar da momy ne suka iso inda suke suna tambayar ta haihu ne,Hajja ke fada masu tana dakin teater,hankulan kuwa a tashe yake,addu'a suke ta zubawa ana dakon fituwar likita,cikakkiyar awa daya ta dauke shi sai ga wata nurse ta fito ,rike da jariri a hannu,da sauri Ali ya isa ya amshi baby yace"how was the mother?"

     Ido ta bishi dashi sannan ta raba ta wuce, gaba daya suka bita da kallo,Dr ne ya fito yana share zufar saman gushinsa,yace "muje office please"

    Tun daga nan kuwa yasha jinin jikinshi,Alhaji ne yace ma Ommar ya rike Ali shi ya shiga ya fito wajan dr din.

    Dr ne ya cire glass din saman fuskarshi sannan ya dubi Alhaji yace"Alhaji kasan duk mai rai mamace ni haka kuma duk wanda yazo duniya yake da tabbas din tafiya,tunda Allah da kansa yace duk wani dan Adam daki duran duniya sai an fidda mashi rai,saboda haka kuyi hakuri Azeema ta rigamu zuwa gidan gaskiya"

      Inalillahi kawai Alhaji yake mai-maitawa ya dadi kafin ya bar office din likitan,bayan ya fito ne ,suka yi mashi ca suji abinda Dr yace,amman sai yace masu muje daga can gefin.

   Ido Alhaji ya tsora ma Ali yace"ka yadda kai musulmine?"

   "na yadda Alhaji"

   "ka yadda Allah na jarabtar bawansa da kaddara mai kyau da mara kyau?"

   Kai ya gyada yace"na yadda"

   "kayi tawakalli kayi hakuri matarka Allah yayi mata cikawa"

     Shima idan ya kafe Alhaji dashi bai ce kumai ba,dafa shi Alhaji yayi sannan ya rungumeshi ya dinga bubbuga bayanshi daga bisani kuma ya fara ajiyar zuciya.

   Daga gefi kuma momy da Hajja ne suke ta kuka,sosai Ommar yayi karfin hali shi ke tausarsu akan ba kuka zasu yi mata ba Addu'a take bukata,a wannan lokacin,amman kumai na karfin hali yake.

   Hannu Alhaji yasa suka dauki gawarta suka tafi gida domin ai mata sutura a kaita gidanta na gaskiya,ba nan duniya gidan karya ba.

   *BAYAN WATA BIYU DA RASUWAR AZEEMA*


     kumai ya zama dai dai soyayya ake bugawa tsakanin Deji da Ali har ka kasa gane wa yafi wani san dan uwanshi,yau ma yana ta shirye shirye barin kasar zai je yin wani taran kara ma juna sani a LONDON.

      Kwance suke shida Deji mirgenawa yayi ya hau saman ruwan cikinta yace"yan mata wai ya akai ne,ya kamata fa ayi ma Adil kanwa ku kane"

    "to ai laifinka ne,baka da kwazo,shi yasa"

    Dariya yayi yace"daman haka abin yake kinan"

    "to kai ya ka gani,kasan dai jaruma ka aura"

   "eh to lallai bari inkara bada himma,bari in fara daga yanzo"

    Da sauri ta ture shi ta fada toilet ta saka key,dariya yayi yace"da kin tsaya mana kiga aiki da cikawa"

    Bayan ta fito shima ya shiga,ya sakar ma kanshi ruwa,tare suka shirya,sunyi kyau matuka kamar wasu zara da wata,kayan su ya fiddo falo sannan yayi ma Audidi waya yazo ya kwashi ya kai mota,dakin ya kuma ya iske,tana kara gyara fuska ya zagaya ya kwanta bayanta yace"am proud of you,recious wify"

   "na fika"

   "bana tsamani haka"

  "ai aikinmu kinan,kullum gadda ma wajan banbanci wanda yafi wani san wani"

    "kuma zuwa yanzo ya kamata ace kin sallama mani kin yadda nafisanki ba"

    Hmmmm"ashi za'a mutu ana wannan gaddama kinan"

   Dariya sukayi sannan ya juyu da ita yace"bari inji test din wannan janbakin mai khamshin apple ne ku mango"

   Harshen shi yasa ya dinga lasar lebinta sai da ya tabbatar ya lashe tas,sannan yace"mai khamshin orange ne ma ashi,muje kada mu makara wify"

    Sanin baisan tana saka janbaki idan zata fita shiyasa batayi magana ba,sakalo shi tayi sanna tace"zamu gidan su Hajja kafin mu wuce ko?"

   *LONDON*


        Abubuwa sun yima Deji dadi baka taba ganinta kayi tunanin Dejin da ka sani ce da ta zama babbar mace tayi kyau na musamman ta zama yar gaban goshin Ali,duk wani abu da ta gindaya shi ake yi ,indai ta fada to hakan ake aiwatarwa,sun kulle da Aunty Yasmen,sosai take bata shawarwari na yanda zata kara damkar mijinta,a tafin hannu,sai yanda tayi dashi,to lallai kuwa ta kama Ali in kana san kaga zafin kai da rashin mutunci tara waje amman badai gida ba,gida sai ladabi da ban hakuri sabida idan anyi ansan za'a gamu wani wajan,yau ma yana zaune tana kwanci saman kafafunshi tace"zumana kasan tunjiya nake fama da tashin zuciya"

    "mai kika ci wanda bai maki ba?"

    "noting wallahi, kawai am just felling haka"

    "ok,can i take you to the Dr ne?"

    "daga tashin zuciya kuma"

   "na sani ku na samu kanin Adil ko kanwa"

    Cikinta ya shafa,sanna ya latsa na shanunta yace"kuma gashi sun kara girma sosai har wani sheki suke"

   "zafi ma nake ji yanzo idan ka taba su"

    Dariya yayi yace"dafa?"

   Fuska ta rufe tace"duk zafin dai"

    "makaryaciya kawai,dadi dai"

    Kwanciya ya gyara mata sannan ya dauki waya ya kira yace"Dr ina gida kizo please yanzo"

   Tashi Deji tayi tace"Ya Ali mai zata zo tayi,nifa ba ciki garan ba"

    "ai ki bari tagwada in babu ba sai in hakura ba"

   Kumawa tayi ta kara dai dai a jikinshi,minti kadan sai ga Dr Aisha ta shigo, bayan yan gaishe gaishe tayi ma Deji yan tambayoyi ta bata amsa sannan tace tana san fitsarinta,toilet Deji taje tayi fitsari sannan ta kawo an gwada,amman bai nuna ba sai Dr tace zata debi jininta zuwa yamma zata fada mashi result din.

    *6:56pm agogon london*


      Dr Aisha ta kirashi take tayashi murna,matarshi tana da ciki na sati ukku,da kwana biyar.

   Murna wajan Ali abun babu magana,rainan cikin suka dinga yi har ya kai wata tara Hajja ta matsa akan sai dai ya dawo da ita gida ta haihu,babu yanda ya iya haka ya haduta da kumai nata ya dawo da ita gida,amman sai da ya tsaya da ita hotel ya tsakuri abinda zai tsakura sannan ya danka ma hajja ita,washi gari ma sai da yasan yanda yayi ya latse kayanshi a dakin Ameenatu sannan yabi jirgin safe ya kuma da dinbin kiwar matarshi.

    Tun gurzar da yayi mata da safe take jin bata lafiya mararta na daurewa,tun tana daurewa har abin ya gagara ta saka kuka,Hajja ta kawo mata madarar shanu ta iske wannan al'amari,da sauri tayi ma Alhaji waya yace"maza su tafi asibiti,bayan sun isa aka kaita dakin haihuwa,suna zaune sai ga nurse ta fito tace Wadan da suka kawo Khadeeja Aliyu a bada kayan yara za'a saka masu,ta haifi yan biyu duk mata,gud'a Hajja tayi tace"Allah mun gode maka,maza Auta yi gaba ki daura ruwan wankan mai jego,da Murna Ameenatu tace"Hajja ki bari a fito dasu in gansu mana"

   "in kinyi hakuri zaki gansu ma har sai kin gaji"

    Dariya tayi sannan ta tafi driver ya tafida ita,nurse tafito da yara manya gwanin ban sha'awa gaba daya sun biyu iyayansu daya tabiyu ubanta daya kuma ta biyu uwarnta,gaba daya Hajja ta hadasu tana masu addu'a.

   Hajja tace" ya maijegon take?"

   "lafiya lau take, da mun gama gyarata zamu fito da ita ku tafi gida tunda lafiya ta haihu"

    "to masha Allahu"

   Waya ta cire ta buga ma Alhaji yayi murna kuma yayi masu addu'a,Ali ta kira take shaida mashi yace yaji bakin Aminatu da Ommar,tsananin murnar da yake ciki bata misaltuwa,sunkuma gida Cikin kushin lafiya,da kuzari.

     Anyi suna yara sunci sunan Deji da Azeema,sai aka canza aka saka masu khairiyya da islam,maijego tasha kaya,yan kauyansu sunzo sun taya ta murna sun kuma, kumai anyishi san barka cikin girma da dattako,Baffanta ya danka ma yara shanu kuwa daya daya.
 
     Watanne ukku suka rage ma Ali ya gama abinda ya kaishi London, haka kuma Hajja tace bata san ta ganshi a Nigeria har sai ya cika wadanna watannin,to yau dai sauran kwana biyu ya dawo an gyara masu gidansu,Alhaji yasa an canza kumai,haka Deji tayi kyau bana wasa ba,ita kanta sha'awar kanta take ina ga Ali gadanga kusar yaki.

   Yau ake sa ranshi Deji tayi kwaliya ta keci raini da ka ganta kaga hamshakiyar mace mai aji da kyau,tasu ta bisu a tarbushi amman Ameenatu ta hana tace suprise zamuyi mashi.

    Duk inda Deji tagilma idanshi na nan karshi dai ya kasa hakuri ya nufi dakinshi yace"ta kawo mashi yaran ya gansu, kafin ya huta hakan da yayi ne Hajja da Mahmud da Ameenatu suka tashi suka ci suma tafiya zasuyi.

    Dawowa yayi ya rakasu bakin mota yace"saiya shigo zuwa safiya"

    Ido daya Ameenatu kashi ma Deji,dariya Deji ta saka tace"naji dai"

   Tun daga compound din gidan ya sureta suka shiga gida tun a falo yake bata sakunnen da ya iso dasu na kiwarta, da kyar ta iya bashi hakuri suka shiga daki.

   Ni dai da gudu na dawo,saboda bazan iya kallan wanna bidirin ba.
